### Proposals (not applied)

**P-2-G12-1-WORDS: the G12-1 line's file-type slot** (a wording clarification of 2.8 for the approver; prompt 3 5.3 asks to list the substitutions). Diff to guardrails 2.8, status-line table:

```diff
-| File stored but not analysed | "Not analysed: RVT model stored, not analysed" |
+| File stored but not analysed | "Not analysed: <file type> stored, not analysed", naming the stored file's type: "RVT model", "IFC model", "DWG drawing", "DOCX file", "JPG image", "PNG image", "ZIP archive", or "PDF scan" for a PDF with no text layer on any page |
```
- **Failure behind it:** 2.8 names only the RVT example; without the slot, a DWG or a scanned PDF would read as an RVT model, or would need new wording. **Effect:** Truth up (the line names what was stored); Speed unchanged. **Proving case:** G12-1 and G12-5 as they run today (the lines are built this way).

**P-2-XLSX-SHEETS: "Partly analysed" for workbooks.**

```diff
-| File partly analysed | "Partly analysed (37 of 40 pages)" |
+| File partly analysed | "Partly analysed (37 of 40 pages)"; for a workbook, "Partly analysed (<n> of <m> sheets)" |
```
- **Failure behind it:** a workbook whose chart or oversized sheet is not read shows no "Partly analysed" line, only its coverage. **Effect:** Truth up (the gap is labelled); Speed unchanged. **Proving case:** a G12-4 variant with a sheet over the cell limit, expecting the new line (a new case once approved).

**P-2-NP-B: an engineer's rejection never reverses the owner's resolution** (from part A; it belongs with P-1-OWNER-FACT-REJECTION). Diff in `packages/domain/src/field-state.ts`, the lone-rejection judgement `holdsAgainst`: `!before.conflicts.some(...)` becomes `!before.conflicts.some(...) && ![...before.tally.applied].some((resolution) => resolution.role === 'owner' && resolution.chosenCandidateId === rejection.candidate.id && !mayResolve(field, 'owner', rejection.event.role))`.
- **Failure behind it:** after the owner resolves hotel over office, an engineer's later rejection of hotel holds, and the field becomes known on office, the value the owner set aside, with no refused event and no "For you" entry. **Effect:** Truth up (the owner's decision is not reversed without the owner); Speed unchanged (no new question; the engineer's route is a note). A new refusal on engineers, so it needs the approver. **Proving case:** owner resolves for hotel at minute 3, engineer rejects hotel at minute 4: refused (`resolver_not_routed`), known on hotel; it flips the test "pinned until the approver decides NP-B" in `packages/domain/src/field-state.test.ts`.

**P-2-SDK-DEPCRUISE: the SDK boundary as a dependency-cruiser rule** (not a guardrail change; a check change that the loosening check holds for the approver).
```diff
 // .dependency-cruiser.cjs, options.exclude.path
-        '(^|/)node_modules/(?!@sovitech/)',
+        '(^|/)node_modules/(?!@sovitech/|@anthropic-ai/)',
```
plus a rule `anthropic-sdk-only-in-ai-transport` (from `pathNot: '^packages/ai/src/transport(\\.test)?\\.ts$'`, to `path: '(^|/)node_modules/@anthropic-ai/'`) and a seeded import. **Failure behind it:** the phase 2 notes asked for a dependency-cruiser rule; the exclusion hides every third-party module from the boundaries, so no rule can see the import, and narrowing the exclusion changes an allow-list entry, which the loosening check fails as a loosening although it lets less through. **Effect:** none on the promises (the ESLint boundary already enforces the same). **Proving case:** the seeded import.

**Written in the review round (2026-09-30).** None is applied; each is for the approver (D-05) unless it says otherwise.

**P-2-OWNER-FACT-READINGS: an engineer's rejection of a reading on an owner field** (an extension of P-1-OWNER-FACT-REJECTION to document and inference readings; verifier finding V4, the NP-A consequence). Diff in `packages/domain/src/field-state.ts`:
```diff
 // EVENT_REFUSALS
+  'engineer_rejection_on_owner_field',
 // classify
-      return ownerChoice && candidate.source === 'user' ? 'engineer_overrules_owner_choice' : 'lone';
+      if (ownerChoice && candidate.source === 'user') return 'engineer_overrules_owner_choice';
+      return field.confirmBy === 'owner' ? 'engineer_rejection_on_owner_field' : 'lone';
 // mayBeLone
-    (event.role === 'sovitech_engineer' ? !(ownerChoice && candidate.source === 'user') : event.role === 'owner' && !engineerChecks);
+    (event.role === 'sovitech_engineer' ? !(ownerChoice && candidate.source === 'user') && field.confirmBy !== 'owner' : event.role === 'owner' && !engineerChecks);
```
with the store guard of migration 0009 (SVX13 and SVX14 in `event_actor_written`) following. An engineer's rejection on an owner field (the owner confirms their own facts, rule 3) becomes a note: the reading stays eligible.
- **Failure behind it:** an engineer rejects "hotel", read from the only document, at minute 2; "office" is read at minute 5: the field is known on "office" with no conflict, and the owner, who saw neither value, is never asked. The same rejection at minute 6 is refused and the conflict goes to the owner. Rules 3 and 4 do not settle whether an engineer may set an owner fact aside outside a conflict, and today's reading involves fewer people (section 10).
- **Effect:** Truth up (disagreeing readings of the owner's own facts reach the owner, as rule 4 routes them); Speed down (the owner may see "Two values" for building type or occupancy where none shows today, and an engineer can no longer clear a misread owner fact alone).
- **Proving case:** proposed G4-36 | T | On an owner field (`confirmBy` owner), an engineer rejects the building type "hotel" read from the only document; later a second document says "office" | The field is in conflict, routed to the owner. It flips the pin in `packages/domain/src/field-state.test.ts` ("pinned until the approver decides P-1-OWNER-FACT-REJECTION for document readings (NP-A)"), G4-33's control "an engineer's rejection outside any conflict holds" and the refusal code in G4-33's property (G4-33's Expected cell is unchanged), and the project-type pin of the same file. It subsumes P-1-OWNER-FACT-REJECTION's own case.

**P-2-INFERENCE-KIND: pass the model's inference kind to the verifier** (the integrator's, from adversarial finding A7). Diff in `packages/ai/src/boundary.ts`, `toCandidateProposal`: `+    ...(candidate.inference === null ? {} : { inference: candidate.inference }),`, together with a verifier check in `packages/domain/src/evidence.ts` that refuses a `direct_count` whose excerpts hold a standalone number token (a written quantity: a count written in the document is a document value, and a count from written numbers is a sum), while numbers glued to a tag ("CTA-01") stay allowed.
- **Failure behind it:** today the kind is dropped, so every inferred count cited to excerpts that hold any digit is refused, legitimate direct counts over tagged lists included (a Speed cost). Passing the kind alone would let a sum the model names `direct_count` through: neither the validator nor the verifier can count the items at a place.
- **Effect:** Speed up (direct counts over tagged lists become values); Truth unchanged if the check lands with it. It lets more values through than today, so it is a loosening (section 10).
- **Proving case:** G1-21 stays rejected with the kind named `direct_count`; a direct count of 3 over "CTA-01, CTA-02, CTA-03" is accepted as `ai_inference`. It flips the pinning test in `packages/ai/src/boundary.test.ts` ("does not pass the kind of inference ... (P-2-INFERENCE-KIND)").

**P-2-QUALIFIER-WORDS-REGISTRY: the rule 8 qualifier words as registry data** (placement and loosening coverage). The verifier's `RULE_8_QUALIFIER_WORDS` (`packages/domain/src/evidence.ts`) holds rule 8's own words only: the area-basis abbreviations and phrases, the level words, and the regim letters of rule 8's example (the registry's `FLOOR_NOTATION_LEVEL_TYPES` letters). Adding a word lets more document values keep a qualifier, a loosening, but the table is domain code outside the loosening snapshot. Proposed: move it to `packages/registry`, inject it through `ProposalContext`, record it in the loosening snapshot as `FLOOR_NOTATION_LEVEL_TYPES` is, and test that each word appears in rule 8's text.
- **Failure behind it:** a word added to the domain's table would pass every check unseen. **Effect:** none today; any future word waits for the approver. **Proving case:** a seeded loosening input that adds a word.
- Noted, not proposed: registering label-value patterns (`ProposalContext.labelValue`) would let a choice be `document` ("From document") instead of Likely; it lets more values through as documents, so it is the approver's, and section 4 already reads a document-named building type as Likely.

**P-2-LS-READ-COMPLEMENTS: let the rule 11 allowlist decide alone.** Diff: in `packages/ai/src/validator/prose.ts` delete `both('life_safety_control', lifeSafetyControlByList);` and its import, then delete `packages/ai/src/validator/life-safety-list.ts`.
- **Failure behind it:** the first reading's denylist refuses monitoring text rule 11 allows: "The BMS monitors whether the fire dampers are open or closed", "The BMS logs when the smoke fans stop", and "Where the car-park fans run in normal mode under BMS control for CO ventilation, the fire system overrides the BMS in fire mode". These are natural G11-1 and G11-4 wordings, and would fail those evals' samples.
- **Effect:** Truth unchanged (the allowlist still refuses every BMS action beyond the four verbs, in any voice and in Romanian); Speed up (fewer refused paragraphs and retries). It lets more AI text through, so it is a loosening.
- **Proving case:** the pinned test in `packages/ai/src/validator/prose.test.ts`, "keeps refusing, by the first reading's list only", flips to passing; G11-2, G11-7 and the refused corpus stay refused.

**P-2-LS-READ-SYNONYMS: the four verbs' plain forms** (a wording clarification of rule 11). Diff to guardrails rule 11, after "the only allowed verbs are monitor, display, log and alarm": `+ (and their plain forms: showing a status, recording a log, reading or receiving a status or input, raising an alarm)`.
- **Failure behind it:** without it, "The BMS receives a volt-free fire-alarm signal" or "The BMS point list includes a fire-alarm input", which is G11-3's own content, reads as a fifth verb. **Effect:** Truth and Speed unchanged: the validator already reads the rule this way, and the first reading never refused these. **Proving case:** the PASSED list in `packages/ai/src/validator/prose.test.ts`.
- **Extended in the second fix round with "report"** (the fix list of that round named it among the read verbs; not applied). Diff to `packages/ai/src/validator/life-safety.ts`, `READ_WORDS`: `+ 'report', 'reports', 'reported', 'reporting', 'raporteaza', 'raporteze', 'raportare', 'raportarea', 'raportat', 'raportata', 'raportate'`, and to the guardrails diff above, "reporting a status" among the plain forms. **Failure behind it:** the check refuses "The BMS reports the fire alarm and the fire-mode status to the operator workstation." (a verb after the BMS that is not a read), a natural G11-1 wording. **Effect:** Truth unchanged if reporting a status is read as a plain form of display or log; Speed up (fewer refused G11-1 and G11-4 samples). Unlike the rest of this proposal it lets more AI text through than today, so it is a loosening. **Proving case:** `prose.test.ts` passes that sentence; G11-2, G11-7 and G11-8 stay refused.

**P-2-LS-PROVISION-OF-READS: a verb of provision or scope that provides reads only** (the second fix round; for the approver). Diff to `packages/ai/src/validator/life-safety.ts`: in `refusedItem`, before the strength switch, `+ if (item.strength === 'strong' && (wordsIn(tokens, PROVISION, clause).has(item.position) || wordsIn(tokens, SCOPE_VERB, clause).has(item.position)) && onlyReads(tokens)) return false;`, and `'scope'` added to `BMS_COMPOUND_NOUNS`.
- **Failure behind it:** natural G11-1 wordings are refused, as they were before the round: "The BMS provides read-only monitoring of the fire alarm system." and "The BMS scope includes Fire Safety as monitoring only: the BMS reads the status of the fire detection and alarm system ..." (`scratchpad/build/fix3b/ai/natural.after.out`). An eval sample with them would fail. The same verbs are already allowed in other positions when every function they provide is a read.
- **Effect:** Truth unchanged (every life-safety function named must still be read-qualified); Speed up (fewer refused drafts). It lets more AI text through, so it is a loosening.
- **Proving case:** `prose.test.ts` passes both sentences; G11-7's and G11-8's refused situations and G11-2 stay refused.

**P-2-STREAM-JSON-READER: `JSON.parse` for the per-line IFC section** (the second fix round's working name P-3-STREAM-JSON-READER; for the approver, as it widens a lint exception list). Diff: add to `JSON_PARSE_REVIEWED` in `tools/eslint-rules/index.js` the entry `{ files: ['apps/api/src/jobs/read-output.ts'], reason: 'each line of an extractor output is handed at once to the contract strict parser' }`, and use `JSON.parse` in `read-output.ts`'s `jsonValue`.
- **Failure behind it:** the YAML reader parses the `perf` model's gated section at about 15,000 lines a second (68 to 73 s for 1,033,071 lines) and holds about 20 times what it parses, so the gated path is slow once `ifc-values` opens.
- **Effect:** Truth unchanged (every line still goes straight to the contract's strict parser, and no engineering value is a JSON number in the contract); Speed up (the gated model's reading several times faster); no owner question. The loosening check reads it as a widening (`lint-bans.json-parse-reviewed`).
- **Proving case:** the lint-bans self-test stays green with its seeds, and `apps/api/src/jobs/output-file.test.ts` and `tests/proposed/ifc-input-5.4-stream.test.ts` pass unchanged.

**P-2-AI-SEARCH-TABLE: a table for AI search records** (for the store; not a guardrail change). Keep each completed run's search record in a dedicated append-only, row-secured table (for example `sovitech.ai_search_records`: project id, document id, content hash, run id, the record, erased with the document) rather than as the extracted-text part `ai:searched:<run id>`. The part was used because it is keyed by project and hash, refused after erasure (SVE11) and removed by the erasure function, and the fix round could not add a migration in that area. **Effect:** none on the promises. **Proving case:** `tests/api/ai-extraction-route-and-search.test.ts` and G12-8 against the new table.

**P-2-DECLARED-FORMAT: read a mis-named file by its content** (the owner's product choice, not a guardrail change). Today a file whose content is another known format than its name says ends as "Analysis failed" with `format_mismatch`. The alternative records the detected format at analysis beside `document_files`' declared one and reads by content; `outputAnswersRequest` would compare against that record.
- **Failure behind it:** a workbook named `.pdf` was read and stored as a workbook under a PDF record (adversarial finding A15). **Effect:** Truth equal both ways (each describes the file honestly); Speed lower today (a mis-named file gives no values until it is uploaded again under the right name). **Proving case:** `tests/api/declared-format-mismatch.test.ts` with "read as XLSX, recorded as XLSX" as its expected result.

Still open from earlier phases: P-0006-1, P-0-GITIGNORE, the `CLAUDE.md` "Where things are" rows for `docs/adr/` and `docs/build-log.md` (phase 0); the phase 1 proposals, unchanged.

### Product doc issues

- **The IFC stack.** Prompt 3 5.2's "Extractor" row ("IfcOpenShell 0.8.5 and IfcTester 0.8.5"), section 6's `services/extractor` line, section 8 ("`ifcopenshell.validate` runs first"), section 12 (the XSD "from standards.buildingsmart.org"; validating the IDS "with lxml and IfcTester") and ifc-input 2.2 describe IfcOpenShell and IfcTester, which the owner's decision of 2026-09-26 replaced with web-ifc; IFC reading lives in `packages/ifc-reader`, not `services/extractor`. The phase 2 exit item "The expected IfcTester results match" cannot be met.
- **The draft IDS.** Prompt 3 section 8 says it is "validated as section 12 describes before you commit it", and ifc-input 5.5 lists the IDS-Audit-tool, IfcTester on the fixture and the IFC4 names for S10. Only the XSD validation ran; S10's `Pset_ChillerTypeCommon.ChillerCapacity` is unchecked (S10 applies to no fixture). Ruling (a): it is committed on the XSD validation with lxml, labelled "DRAFT REFERENCE DATA"; the IfcTester half is not applicable by the owner's decision.
- **PRD R-023 and R-024 against prompt 3 5.2.** Their "Until decided" lines say "Not built: no model is read"; prompt 3 5.2 (parsing scope) reads IFC with its values behind `ifc-values`. The first integration read models live for the engineer's record and findings, as the orchestrator had directed; the phase 2 review found it against prompt 3 section 4 (follow the stricter), and ruling (b) now follows the PRD lines: no model is read live until D-01. Logged for the owner ("Questions", item 3).
- **PRD D-36 against prompt 3 5.2 on the IDS check.** 5.2 runs IfcTester with the draft IDS; D-36's "Until decided" runs no IDS check. The stricter D-36 line holds (and IfcTester is not used anyway).
- **Document kind and stage.** US-DOCS-03 and US-DOCS-08 ask for a detected kind, stage and revision; nothing classifies a document yet, so every document registers kind `other` and stage `unknown` (the safe reading of 2.3 and rule 1). The classifier (F-EXTRACT-07) is not built; its AI half needs the key.
- **ifc-input 5.3 names a mockup figure** for CH-01's `ChillerCapacity` (a figure on `tools/checks/mockup-figures.txt`), and G8-1's and G8-2's case texts use a listed figure; the fixtures use other synthetic figures (ADR 0032).
- **Extractor-level cases.** Prompt 3 section 6 says extractor-level cases "call the extractor from the TypeScript test"; the index check's `[process]` rule refuses a case file that starts a process. The extractor-level halves are pytest tests titled with their ids, and the TypeScript cases drive the worker with scripted outputs, aligned with the real reader's codes in this integration.
- **The IDS decision id.** The build's own documents cited the IDS check as D-92 (SOVITECH datasets); it is D-36 (corrected; "Guardrail review").
- **Prompt 3's "New case ids".** Phase 2 indexed G4-34 and G4-35 beyond prompt 3's four, the review round G1-18 to G1-24, G2-10, G2-11, G3-17, G8-18 to G8-20, G11-7, G12-8 and G12-9, and the second fix round G1-25, G2-12, G3-18 and G11-8; the next run of the prompts should start later phases' new ids after these (G4-36 is held by proposal P-2-OWNER-FACT-READINGS).
- **Traceability ids for the API's port, its health route and the checks.** No story or function in `docs/product/` covers the API's port, a health route or the repository checks, so their test titles start with ADR ids (the orchestrator's direction for the second fix round): `apps/api/src/port.test.ts` (three, ADR 0002 · ADR 0007), `apps/api/src/server.test.ts` (the health route, ADR 0002), `tools/eslint-rules/anthropic-sdk-boundary.test.ts` (F-EXTRACT-03 · ADR 0023), `tools/checks/index/eval-runs.test.ts` (three, ADR 0023 · ADR 0003), `tools/checks/index/guardrail-index.test.ts` (the pin, ADR 0003) and `tools/checks/mockup-figures/figures.test.ts` (six, ADR 0009). The next run of prompt 1 could add functions for the checks harness and the API's operation.
- **The notices page (D-94).** web-ifc 0.0.78's WebAssembly bundles CDT (MPL-2.0), glm (MIT) and tinynurbs (BSD-3-Clause), and ships none of their licence texts; the PRD's notices requirement should name bundled components, not only packages.

### Questions for the owner

Only questions that change the build, each with its source and D ids; phase 0's and phase 1's open questions stand.

1. **The demo profile (dashboards 8.4; build-readiness decision 7; D-18).** The demo is built from the fixture's `test` profile, a small building whose floors and areas are synthetic. Should the build wait for the `demo` profile (its floor structure, areas and uses) before the demo is shown to anyone, or go on with the `test` profile and replace it later?
2. **The API key (build-readiness 3 items 6 and 7; D-09 for the route).** `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is still not visible to the build. Until it is (in the shell or the git-ignored `.env`), no eval runs, no AI recording exists, and the demo shows no value from any document.
3. **The parsing scope, now also whether the live app reads models for the engineer (R-023, R-024; prompt 3 5.2; D-01).** Until you decide D-01, the live app stores each uploaded IFC model only, with no reader job, as the PRD's "Until decided" lines say (ruling (b)): no engineer's record, schema line or rule 14 finding comes from a model, and the demo shows none. The reader is built and proven in tests. When you decide D-01, say also whether the live app should read models for the engineer's record and findings before `ifc-values` opens.
4. **The demo's owner answers (rule 10 "Demo data"; ADR 0029 decision 4; D-05).** May "fixture sources only" be read, for the owner's answers, as "from the committed fixture file, written by a seed account labelled as demo input"? The alternative is a demo with no step 1 answers, and so no project.
5. **The IDS (ifc-input 5.5; D-36).** No IDS check runs until you decide D-36. The draft IDS v0.1 is committed as draft reference data on its XSD validation (ruling (a)); with IfcTester not used, D-36 also decides which tool, if any, runs the check.
6. **Drafting evals (ADR 0023 decision 3; D-09).** May the drafting evals (G11-1, G11-4) send a manifest-listed fixture file, or should they wait for the demo project?
7. **A file whose content differs from its name (proposal P-2-DECLARED-FORMAT; rule 12).** Today a workbook uploaded as `.pdf` reads "Analysis failed" and gives no values until it is uploaded again under the right name. Should the app instead read a file by its content and record the format it found?
8. **The notices page (D-94).** Please have counsel confirm the licences of web-ifc's bundled CDT (MPL-2.0), glm (MIT) and tinynurbs (BSD-3-Clause), with MPL-2.0 itself, before the notices page ships.

The verifier, the adversarial review, their fix round, its closing verification, the second fix round, its closing verification and the third fix round are done ("Guardrail review").

**From the third fix round** (for the approver and later phases):
- **Rule 11's residual** (the header of `packages/ai/src/validator/life-safety.ts`, pinned in `prose.test.ts`): six sentences stay accepted; the evals G11-1 and G11-4 are the second layer and run once a key exists. Closing it needs either a reading of verbs by position in a clause that names no BMS (which refused read sentences when tried) or more names and possessives.
- **2.8 and "confirm":** "I c o n f i r m it." passes, as "I confirm it." does, because "confirm" is not on 2.8's list. Whether it belongs there is the approver's (a change to 2.8, section 10).
- **Rule 11, the same family found by the closing verification** (not a loosening: accepted before and after the round; `verify2e/ai/residual-note.out`): a clause after a BMS read that names neither the BMS nor points back to it, with a verb of provision and no actor: "The BMS monitors the fire alarm; smoke extraction then takes place.", "The BMS monitors the fire alarm; smoke extraction then follows." and "The BMS monitors the fire alarm. Smoke extraction then takes place." (the "and then ... takes place" form is refused). Listed here and in ADR 0023 decision 11 with the header's six; closing them has the same cost as the header's second and third.
- **2.8's spaced-word side effect and split words** (NV10, corrected above): spaced words holding a listed term are refused while their unspaced forms pass; split words ("ve rified") pass. Neither changes 2.8; the split words would need 2.8 to match inside words, which is the approver's.
- **The per-line reader's leniency** (ADR 0034 decision 3): the YAML reader with its JSON schema accepts YAML syntax strict JSON would not; the contract's strict line schemas hold every value. A strict reader is part of P-2-STREAM-JSON-READER.
- **Phase 3 upload client:** a chunk refused `chunk_incomplete` or `request_timeout` is resumed like `chunk_timeout`: ask for the status, send from the bytes held.

**Carried from phase 2** (in phase 3 or when their decisions come):
- **Before `ifc-values` can open:** batch the gated value path's per-element writes (`packages/db` `recordIfcElement` and `createSubject`, and the per-proposal writes; storing the `perf` model's 291,541 statements and 22,635 elements takes 50 to 225 s, ADR 0034) and measure again; decide P-2-STREAM-JSON-READER (the per-line reader at about 15,000 lines a second). The reader should also emit the statements of declared units, so a value's unit can be checked against text; the erasure's audit record should count IFC excerpts.
- **The inference kind:** decide P-2-INFERENCE-KIND with the approver; applying it also removes `proposalForVerifier` (`apps/api/src/ingestion/proposals.ts`), which refuses a proposer-named direct count on the ingestion path meanwhile (ADR 0027 decision 13); store the kind with an accepted `ai_inference` candidate (a guarded column, or `candidate_ai_origins`).
- **The tier a badge reads:** phase 3's view-model resolver renders the Likely, Possible or Please check badge from `DerivedCandidate.confidence`, never from `Candidate.confidence` (ADR 0016 decision 22; G3-18); phase 7's calibration (G3-6) reads the derived tier too. The glossary tier of rule 3 waits for `dataset-glossary`.
- **G12-5 and G12-6** (verifier finding V14): point their "question", "open item" and "Not found in the analysed documents" assertions at the question engine, the "For you" list and the rendered statement once phase 3 builds them. G12-8 uses the same proxy.
- **Hardening left from the review:** the extractor could parse the hashed bytes themselves rather than reopen the path; a runtime guard against a computed import of the SDK (a Node resolve hook, `module.registerHooks`, installed at API and worker start, refusing `@anthropic-ai/*` from any parent but `packages/ai/src/transport.ts`; it would not see vite-node's resolution in tests); the 4 MiB output bound, which a real PDF of a few hundred pages would pass ("Analysis failed"), once real uploads are allowed (ADR 0034, "Consequences").
- **Phase 3 upload client:** on 409 `upload_busy` or `chunk_timeout`, ask for the status and resume; a chunk must arrive within 120 s.
- Document classification (F-EXTRACT-07: kind, stage and revision), and derive's design-stage lookup (2.3; G2-6).

- The erasure sweep: `removeUnheldFiles` has no caller and no test.

The loosening check's self-test and the SDK boundary's tests timed out under a loaded full run; their timeouts are now 180 s and 120 s.

## Phase 3: the intake wizard, steps 1-8 (2026-09-30/10-01)

### Plan

**Goal** (prompt 3 section 10, "Phase 3"): an owner can go from step 1 to step 8 on a new project and on the demo, never blocked, seeing every value with its badge and source.

**Start of the phase.** Guardrails v1.7 (phase 2's own version; the section 10 approver table empty; no change-log row records an approval); every one of the 18 gates closed; no D row decided. The owner's answers in force ("Owner answers during the run" above, with their words): the model id `claude-opus-5-5`; uploads accept fixture hashes only (ADR 0028); IFC read with web-ifc, and until D-01 the live app stores models only, with no reader job (ruling (b)); on 2026-09-30 "Go on with test profile (Recommended)": the demo keeps the fixture's synthetic `test` building until the owner supplies the `demo` profile. `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is still not set: no AI runs, so no value comes from any document; every value the owner does not type reads Unknown or Not provided yet, and every output "Not available yet" naming what is missing. The brand: the SOVITECH brand tool in its dark variant (owner decision 2026-09-24); the mockups stay the brief for layout, flows and content. The demo is "Demo Hotel Bucharest", labelled demo on every screen of the demo project and on no other.

**How the phase runs.** Part A: this planner (contracts, dependencies, skeleton, ADRs, this plan), then five builders in parallel against what is written here, then the integrator. Part B: the verifier, the read-only adversarial review and the design review, then fixes (prompt 3 section 12).

No production rule suggests an automation area until D-12, so the case injects its rule through `planContinue`'s suggestion list | view-model, API |
| G5-1 | The gross floor area found in a TEST document with its basis stated: no question and no step 8 inline ask for it; shown with its badge and Edit | view-model |
| G5-2 | Rooms from a TEST document, guest rooms stated, no conflict: no confirmation, not an open item | view-model |
| G5-3 | Twelve values pass the confirmation test under a TEST registry, budget 7: seven shown by impactRank, five labelled and for the engineer, a `confirmation_budget_exceeded` defect | view-model |
| G7-3 | A question with an answer or a visible suggestion: no Skip link (the planner, and the step 5 and step 4 components) | view-model, UI |
| G7-4 | A floors conflict stored while the late-findings ledger says the owner left step 3 and is on step 6: step 3 in `dots`, one notice, the conflict among step 8's items, no dialog (a component test: no `dialog` or `alertdialog` role; the notice a polite status) | API, web |
| G7-5 | 126 engineer items and 2 owner items (TEST): "2 things for you to check" and one line "SOVITECH will check 126 equipment classifications" (the case's own numbers, section 7) | view-model |
| G7-6 | `POST /api/projects` with each of the four fields empty in turn: 422 `required_fields_missing` naming it, no project row; the step 1 form shows the inline error and Next stays enabled | API, web |

Moved into phase 3 (prompt 3 phase 10: "move a case if the code it tests lands elsewhere, and say so"): **G2-7** (T), because the one resolver lands here: step 3's and step 8's area are the same value id with the identical display. Phase 4 adds the dashboard screens to it.

Candidate new cases, each Expected following from the rules as written (section 10 allows new cases without approval); the builders write each case file at `tests/guardrails/<ID>.test.ts`, and the integrator indexes them in section 7 with the guardrails 1.8 change-log row before the phase's check run (a case file with no index row fails the index check until then):
- **G7-8** (T): step 6 left with no goal ticked and none suggested, then Continue: the question is recorded `skipped`, and no goal gets a `not_selected` decision (rule 7, "Continue counts as skipping"; 2.6; US-INTAKE-09 AC3).
- **G8-21** (T): the owner types "1.500" in the step 8 inline ask for the gross floor area: refused, nothing stored (rule 8, "Ambiguous readings keep both … It is never silently read one way"; US-INTAKE-17 AC7).
- **G10-10** (T): a project not flagged demo: no screen response carries the demo line (rule 10, "Demo data"; US-REVIEW-03 AC7).
- **G11-9** (T): Fire Safety named by a TEST detection on step 4: the card is not preselected and shows no Suggested (rule 11 and section 5, step 4: "except life-safety systems"; US-SCOPE-02 AC2).

Cases already real that the new routes must keep holding (no new file): G1-8, G3-10, G4-5, G4-11, G4-15, G4-19, G7-7. The 20 E cases stay pending (no key).

#### ADRs

Written by the planner (2026-09-30), amended by the builders where they decide more: **0035** phase 3 frontend dependencies (router, self-hosted Inter, icons, component tests); **0036** the wizard API contract and the display objects; **0037** the e2e setup; **0038** the development login; **0039** wizard navigation, saving, late findings and Generate before phase 5 (the 5.2 defaults read with the PRD's stricter "Until decided" lines); **0040** the app theme tokens (Brand values only); **0041** performance budgets (Proposed; missing since phase 2).

#### The contract (for the builders)

- **Module:** `packages/view-model/src/browser/contract/` (display.ts, common.ts, auth.ts, projects.ts, steps.ts, actions.ts, late-findings.ts, uploads.ts, routes.ts, index.ts), exported by `@sovitech/view-model/browser`; both apps import it. No dependency-cruiser rule changed: the folder is browser-side code by the existing `VIEW_MODEL_BROWSER` pattern.
- **Routes** (`ROUTES` in routes.ts; each with method, path, session, CSRF, schemas, refusals, `servesDisplayObjects` and the ids it serves; refusals common to every route stated once there):
  - `GET /api/csrf` (phase 2); `GET /api/auth/dev-accounts` (404 `dev_login_off`); `POST /api/auth/dev-sign-in` CSRF (404 `dev_login_off`, 403 `not_a_dev_account`); `POST /api/auth/sign-out` CSRF; `GET /api/auth/session`.
  - `GET /api/projects` (display objects); `POST /api/projects` CSRF (422 `required_fields_missing` with `fields`, 422 `project_type_invalid`, 422 `country_invalid`; G7-6).
  - `GET /api/projects/:projectId/steps/:step` (display objects; steps 1 to 8); `POST /api/projects/:projectId/steps/:step/continue` CSRF (422 `answer_invalid`, `number_ambiguous`, `unit_mismatch`, `qualifier_required`; 403 `owner_only`); `GET /api/projects/:projectId/extracted` (UD-45).
  - `POST /api/projects/:projectId/fields/edit`, `/confirm`, `/acknowledge`, `/concern`, `/resolve-conflict`, `/skip`, each CSRF, each answering the display objects it changed (refusals in routes.ts: `shown_value_changed`, `confirmation_not_shown`, `not_an_engineer_field`, `conflict_not_open`, `routed_to_engineer`, `question_answered`, `question_required`).
  - `GET /api/projects/:projectId/late-findings?current=&since=&left=<step>@<ISO>` (display objects); `GET /api/projects/:projectId/proposal` (display objects).
  - Phase 2's upload routes unchanged: `POST …/uploads`, `GET …/uploads/:uploadId`, `PUT …/uploads/:uploadId?offset=`, `POST …/uploads/:uploadId/complete`, `DELETE …/uploads/:uploadId`.
- **Display objects** (display.ts): `DisplayObject` = value id; kind `field`, `record` or `line`; `text` (never empty; the missing wording when `shape` is `missing`); `parts`; `shape`; `missing` (unknown, not provided yet, reading documents, not found in documents, not available yet, not applicable); one `badge` (2.8 id and label); `measure` (label, unit, qualifier label); `sourceLine`; `lines` (2.8 status lines and rule lines of the value); `evidence` (document id, content hash, verbatim excerpt); `actions` (edit, confirm, acknowledge, concern, resolve conflict, add, skip); `field` (subject and field key); `quotationRecordId` (never in phase 3). `servedDisplayOf` is the one projection the components and the render test share.
- **Value ids** (display.ts): `<subject kind>:<subject id>.<field key without its subject prefix>[.<qualifier>]`; `document:<id>.fileName|coverage|stage|revision`; `upload:<id>.fileName`; `project:<id>.documents.count`, `.documents.stillReading`, `.openItems.owner`, `.openItems.ownerMore`, `.openItems.engineer.<group>`, `.confirmations.count`, `.lateFindings.notice`, `.outputs.<output>`; `document:<id>.revisionNotice`; `building:<id>.detection.<system>`.
- **Server side** (`@sovitech/view-model/server`, stubs with their rules as doc comments): `src/formatting/` (formatAsWritten, formatOwnerQuantity, formatCalculated, formatCount, rangeSignificantFigures, roundRangeOutward, formatEstimate, formatRangeOverValues, formatDate), `src/resolver/` (valueIdOf, resolveField, resolveLine, lineOf, resolveDocument), `src/intake/` (passesConfirmationTest, selectConfirmations, planQuestion, PRODUCTION_SUGGESTION_RULES, planContinue, openItems, inlineAsks, outputAvailability, lateFindings).

The contract, the skeleton, the ADRs and this log are the planner's; the integrator reconciles the builders' contract additions and indexes the candidate cases.

#### The "Until decided" lines and 5.2 defaults that apply, quoted

- **R-005** (D-12): "step 7 preselects no area and the unanswered question shows 'Skip for now'; unticking a step 4 system after step 7 changes no automation-area answer without the owner."
- **R-006** (D-11): "steps 5 and 6 show occupancy, schedule and goals with no preselection, and each unanswered question shows 'Skip for now'."
- **R-007** (D-11): "the note fields are not shown."
- **R-008** (D-10): "clicking the stepper opens nothing; Back, Continue and step 8's Edit links move the owner, and Continue after an Edit moves to the next step in order." Stricter than 5.2's "The stepper opens any visited step … After an Edit from step 8, Continue returns to step 8": R-008 is followed (ADR 0039).
- **R-009** (D-10): "answers saved by Next, Continue, an inline Edit or an inline ask stay stored as candidates and events, and unsaved input is not stored; a project reopened before Generate opens at step 1 with every stored answer shown and nothing asked again, and a project with a generated proposal opens its stored proposal, not the wizard." Stricter than 5.2's "Every answer the owner makes is stored when made … Resume at the last visited step": R-009 is followed.
- **R-010** (D-23, D-05): "project type stays one single choice, not split into building status and scope, and steps 2 to 8 show the same questions for every type." (5.2 "Project type branching": "None".)
- **R-011** (D-11, D-05): "choosing Business hours, Extended hours, Seasonal or 'Other' opens no follow-up input and no text field."
- **R-012** (D-14, D-02): "from the first generated proposal, no menu shows a 'back to intake' entry … a 'Not available yet' line for a missing owner input still names it, and its action opens a built page (at least the step 8 inline ask for that field)."
- **R-014** (D-01): "A format is stored 'Not analysed' for as long as the release does not parse it"; with ruling (b), IFC models stored only.
- **R-021** (D-22): "No total limit is applied." (5.2 "500 MB limit": "Per file".)
- **R-047** (D-21): "with no document uploaded, step 3 shows each fact as 'Not provided yet' with Edit; with documents analysed and nothing found, each fact reads 'Not found in the analysed documents' with the coverage and Edit; no separate manual-entry form is built." (5.2 "No documents": "No manual-entry form".) With no completed AI run nothing counts as searched (G12-8), so no not-found line appears in this build.
- **R-144** (D-10, D-02): "The header menu holds only the account item, an entry that opens the project list and sign-out."
- **R-147** (D-19): "Every colour the brand does not define comes from the theme token file, where the token is marked as awaiting the owner's OK." The orchestrator's rule is stricter (no Extension value is used): followed (ADR 0040).
- **R-148** (D-15): "No tagline appears in the header, the footer, the sign-in page or an export cover." **R-149** (D-15, D-26): English copy, no icon or favicon of its own.
- **R-153** (D-13): "The development login is the only sign-in, and no self-registration, invitation or password-reset flow is built."
- **R-136 interim** (D-13): "in S1 (proposed) it [the demo] is listed, with its demo line, for every development account."
- **R-046 interim** (D-61): "an engineer field whose only source was removed goes under 'SOVITECH will check' and is not counted in the owner's 'For you' list." **Not applied** (part B, V-4): guardrails 2.3 says a field left with no eligible candidate "appears under 'For you' as 'Source document removed'", and the guardrails win over the PRD (prompt 3 section 3); the code sends every source-removed field to For you (derive's `reviewItem` in field-state.ts, `openItems`, G4-15). See "Product doc issues".
- **R-051 interims** (D-64): "Access Control and Elevators are not preselected when a document shows a part on rule 11's list"; reading Continue at the level of the whole question, "with unticked cards then recorded as exclude".
- **PRD 10.4, UD-01** (D-02, D-14): "Until dashboards 8.3 and onboarding Q12 are answered, no separate Overview is built and the owner sees the stored proposal." Stricter than 5.2 "Generate before phase 5" ("Generate goes to the generating state, then Overview, whose estimate reads 'Not available yet' naming what is missing. The button is never dead."): phase 3 shows the generating state and a proposal page with each output's "Not available yet", stores nothing, and builds no Overview (ADR 0039).
- **D-14 interim:** "Where no owner action exists, the line names what is missing and shows no action wording." (5.3: "with an action where the owner has one".)
- **5.2 defaults** applied as written: "Authentication" (a roles table, the database guard and a development login: ADR 0038); "No IFC uploaded" (the view area shows "Not available yet", naming the missing model, with the upload action; with the demo's models stored, their G12-1 line); "City" ("Country first from ISO 3166-1. The city is stored as the owner typed it, and the city id stays Unknown until the SIRUTA licence is confirmed"); "Unchecking a system after step 7" ("Never change an owner answer"); "UI language" (English, one catalogue); "Product name, tagline, favicon" (the logo only); "Accessibility level" (WCAG 2.2 AA); "Performance budgets" (ADR 0041); "Systems catalogue" (the eight step 4 systems); "Demo building" (the fixture's `test` profile, confirmed by the owner on 2026-09-30).

#### Where copy lives, and the 2.8 lines the wizard uses

- **Registry copy** (`packages/registry/src/copy/`, server side, served in display objects, never repeated in a UI string): 2.8's badges (`BADGES`), status lines and stage labels (`STATUS_LINES`), generated sentences (`GENERATED_SENTENCES`), and, new in phase 3, the rule lines (`RULE_LINES`, rule-lines.ts): sentences the guardrails write word for word for the wizard, none holding a reserved term.
- **The UI catalogue** (`apps/web/src/copy/en.json`, registered with the reserved-term check in `tools/checks/reserved-terms/catalogues.ts`): fixed interface copy only: titles, subtitles, question wording and helper lines (rule 6's one-line reason) keyed by the registry's question ids, option labels keyed by field and option, card titles and descriptions, action labels, banners, and the draft copy for states with no approved design (sign-in, the project list, step 3's intro variants, step 4's subtitle when nothing is named, the proposal page).
- **2.8 badges the wizard shows:** Two values; Reading documents…; Not applicable (only as 2.4 sets it); Unknown / Not provided yet; Verified by SOVITECH and Confirmed by you (allowances registered in phase 1); Provided by you; Please check; Likely / Possible; SOVITECH will check; From design drawings; From document; Calculated; Suggested; Not found in documents; Not available yet. (Estimated and Reference: no phase 3 value has either.)
- **2.8 status lines the wizard shows:** "Partly analysed (<n> of <m> pages)", "Not analysed: <file type> stored, not analysed" (the G12-1 form with the file type in its slot: "IFC model", "RVT model", "DWG drawing", "DOCX file", "JPG image", "PNG image", "ZIP archive", "PDF scan"), "Analysis failed", "Source document removed", "From a superseded revision", "Out of date, recalculating" (only with a calculated value, none in phase 3), "Site survey needed" (under SOVITECH will check), and "Demo data, not an assessment of the real building". No stage label is shown in phase 3: with every dataset gate closed no investment figure exists; "Formal quotation" never appears.
- **Rule lines** (quoted from the guardrails; slots in braces): "You can provide this later."; "To show your investment estimate we need {field}." with "Generate without it"; "{count} things for you to check"; "and {count} more"; "SOVITECH will check {count} equipment classifications"; "We found {count} more things in your documents. You'll see them on the review step."; "Still reading {count} files. Your estimate will update when they finish."; "Add the {field} to see this." with "[Add {field}]"; "Not available yet: {missing}" (rule 4's form, used to name a missing SOVITECH dataset); "Not available yet: two values for {field}"; "Provisional: two values for {field}"; "Documents say {a}. You entered {b}. Which is right?"; "Documents disagree on this. A SOVITECH engineer will check it."; "Is this right?" with "Yes · Edit"; "Yes, it's a {building type}"; "Not found in the analysed documents ({coverage})." and, on step 4, "… You can still include it."; "Suggested because {reason}"; "{count} guest rooms in {document}"; "{revision} changed {count} values" (2.3). Singular variants use the same words. "Looks right" and "Something's wrong" (rule 3) are action labels in the catalogue.
- **Changed from the approved copy by guardrails section 5 and the rules:** no "Detected" or "Optional" badges (§5-4a); Fire Safety's text "monitoring only (read-only); fire logic and fire-mode interlocks remain in the fire system" (§5-4b); step 3's pill counts only confirmation rows, as "<n> things for you to check" (§5-3f; US-REVIEW-05 AC3), and the warning row never says "before we continue"; no "Building data extracted" banner (R-045 leaves US-REVIEW-04 AC12 off; 2.8's lines "are also the only ones used"); step 8's Proposal card says a preliminary proposal is generated, never "Ready to generate" (§5-8); step 8's Operations card labels the occupancy with its registered label ("Occupancy"), not "Building use" (US-INTAKE-15 Notes).

#### Risks

- **No API key:** no document value, no detection and no inferred asset exists, so From document, Likely, Possible, Please check, Suggested, the confirmations and the conflicts appear only in tests with TEST candidates; the demo's step 3 reads Not provided yet or Unknown and every output "Not available yet". The e2e flow (a) proves the demo's path with that data.
- **The render test's allowlist cannot grow in this build** (a new entry is a loosening the loosening check fails: `render.entries`): the eyebrow "STEP <n> OF 8", the header's time of day and the schedule option "24 / 7" (which rule 2 names as an example of fixed copy) cannot show their digits; they are dropped or reworded ("Around the clock") and written as proposals.
- **Icons above 32 px** read as unreadable pixels: every icon is 32 px or less (ADR 0035).
- **Step 4's detection fields are not registered:** registering them changes what the AI is asked (`fieldsForAi` reads the registry's building fields), which needs the evals to pass 5 of 5 (CLAUDE.md, definition of done item 2), and they cannot run without a key. Every step 4 card reads Unknown (US-SCOPE-01 AC8); proposal P-3-DETECTION-FIELDS.
- **Parallel builders on one contract:** B3 stewards additive changes; the others work to the written shapes and name what they need in their reports.
- **The store change** (a security-definer function listing the user's projects) passes through the guard self-check (G4-22, G13-6) and must not widen row security (G13-5, G13-6).
- **The approved step 5 order** (occupancy, then schedule) differs from rule 6's impactRank order (schedule 24, occupancy 25): impactRank is followed (US-INTAKE-05 AC11 and its Notes).
- **The only owner confirmation the registry allows is building type** (the gross floor area, floors, rooms and zones are engineer fields): the area confirmation of rule 5's example cannot show; the budget mechanism is proven with TEST fields (G5-3).
- **The e2e setup needs Docker and both sandbox images** and takes about a minute more; `pnpm check`'s render step starts it too.
- **The web budget** (300 KB gzipped initial JavaScript) is measured at the end of the phase.
- **Scaffold spec:** `tests/e2e/scaffold.spec.ts` asserts phase 0's placeholder at `/`; the planner's skeleton keeps it, and B5 replaces the spec when B4 makes `/` choose between sign-in and the project list.

#### Known differences from the screenshots, planned (the design review compares each step and adds what it finds)

The logo in place of the "SOVITECH" wordmark and no tagline (OD-2, OD-3); the header date as "30 Sep 2026" with no weekday and no time (P-3-HEADER-TIME); no "STEP <n> OF 8" eyebrow (P-3-EYEBROW-STEP-NUMBER); brand colours (mint accent, white primary button, 1px and 2px radii, no glow) (ADR 0040); icons at 32 px or less; step 1 keeps its approved frame ("Next", no Back, no title block) with Country before City (§5-1b) and City as a text input (no city list: D-94); step 2's tiles as hints; step 3 with no viewer, toggle or floor selector (US-REVIEW-04 AC14), no success banner, the pill and warning row as above, no "Systems" row while nothing is detected; step 4 with no pre-ticked cards (no detection) and the Fire Safety text of §5-4b; step 5 with no preselection, schedule before occupancy, "Around the clock" for "24 / 7", no note field; step 6 with no Other card, no preselection and no note field; step 7 with no preselection; step 8's cards under registered labels, the Proposal card's wording, the For you and SOVITECH will check lists, and "Generate Proposal" always enabled.

### Built

Part A, 2026-09-30 and 2026-10-01: the planner, four builders (B1 theme and UI, B2 view-model, B3 API and store, B4 web shell with steps 1 and 2) and two step builders (steps 3 and 4 with UD-45; steps 5 to 8 with the proposal page), then the integrator, who also did B5's share (the e2e setup, the flows, the render screens), because no B5 report came in. Part B (the verifier, the read-only adversarial review, the design review, fixes) follows. Guardrails v1.8 (this phase's rows, below).

#### By requirement and story

State as prompt 3 section 15 names it. "Tests" names the case files, component and API tests and e2e flows that prove it; every title starts with its ids.

| R-002 Steps 5 to 7 | US-INTAKE-05 to 10 | built; impactRank order on step 5 (schedule before occupancy) | `Step5.test.tsx`, `Step6.test.tsx`; G7-3, G7-8, G3-4, G4-36 and G7-10 (part B), G4-38 and G7-13 (after the final verification: answers and skips sent together); flows (b), (c) |
| R-003 Step 8, inline asks, Generate | US-INTAKE-15 to 18 | built; Generate per 5.2 "Generate before phase 5" read with PRD 10.4 (nothing stored) | `Step8.test.tsx`, `ProposalPage.test.tsx`; G8-21, G5-1, G7-5, and in part B G10-11, G7-10, G7-11, G7-12, G8-22, G8-23, G5-4; flows (a), (b), (c), `inline-ask-refusal.spec.ts` |
| R-004 Late findings | US-INTAKE-19, US-REVIEW-16 AC1-AC2 | built (before Generate) | G7-4 (API case file; web half `late-findings.test.tsx`, `Step6.test.tsx`, `Step8.test.tsx`) |

#### By area

- **Theme and UI kit (B1; `packages/ui`, ADR 0040).** Brand values only: no "Extension" colour and, stricter than the planner's first draft, no "Brand value, new role" colour either; the roles a screen still needs take a brand value and are marked "proposed, pending the owner's OK" (control borders white 55%, the mint focus outline 2px with 2px offset, one neutral badge pill, a white late-findings dot, completed steps as a white ring with a check). Tailwind v4 with the default palette, shadows, radii and fonts switched off. Components: Value (the one value component), Price (stage from its display object; refuses "Formal quotation" without a quotation record; no phase 3 screen shows a price), Badge, StatusLine, NotAvailableYet, SkipForNow, DemoLine, Stepper, Card, Banner, ActionRow, NoticeRegion and Notice (polite, never a dialog), Button (no disabled state), TextField, SelectField, Choice, ChoiceCard and ChoiceGroup (real inputs), FieldError, Dropzone, Progress (no number), CalendarDate, Icon (32 px or less), Logo. Seven kit harness pages (`tests/e2e/pages/ui/`), now also render canaries in `pnpm check`. The integrator fixed two kit issues the step builders found: every "Something's wrong" button had the React key `concern` (now keyed by candidate), and every part after the first was styled as a unit (now only the declared unit symbol).
- **View-model (B2; `packages/view-model/src/{formatting,resolver,intake}`, ADR 0042).** The formatting module (outward-rounded ranges, estimates, counts, dates as "D MMM YYYY"), the resolver (display objects with their one badge by 2.8's first match, source lines, status lines, rule lines, actions), the question engine (confirmation test and budget, question plans, Continue's plan and records, owner values through the rule 8 parser, acknowledgements, concerns, conflict resolution, skips, open items, output availability, late findings). `rule-lines.ts` in the registry's copy, each line proven against docs/guardrails.md.
- **API and store (B3; `apps/api/src/{wizard,projects,auth}`, `packages/db`, migration 0014).** Every route of the contract's `ROUTES` (a test that Fastify's routes equal the table), the development login, the project list through `sovitech.request_user_projects()`, project creation (G7-6), the step views, UD-45 and the proposal page, the owner's writes through the question engine's plans, the late-findings route, the guardrail events (`question_for_known_field`, `confirmation_budget_exceeded`, `owner_corrected_inference`, `skipped`), codes and ids only in logs.
- **Web (B4 and the two step builders; `apps/web/src`).** The router, the session, the shell (logo, the project's bound name, the date, the menu, the demo line, the notice region), the typed client with CSRF and the resumable upload client, the page-session late-findings ledger and poll, sign-in, the project list, steps 1 to 8, UD-45 and the proposal page, each with its loading, empty, partial and error states and `data-render-ready`. The integrator pointed the `extracted` and `proposal` routes at their pages (the planner's placeholders are gone), moved a refused file row's alert role inside its list item (axe `list`), consolidated two duplicate catalogue keys (`step3.showExcerpt`, `step3.writeFailed` into `review.*`), and made the step 2 component test wait for each screen before pressing Back (it failed under a loaded full run).
- **E2E and render (the integrator; `tests/e2e`, ADR 0037 amended).** The global setup and its stack process (TEST database, development owner, demo seed through the sandbox, API on 4174, worker, a TEST-only control route for guardrail events); the render adapter pointed at the contract's display-object routes (`isDisplayObjectRequest`, `DisplayObjectSchema`, `servedDisplayOf`); 29 render screens covering every `APP_PATHS` route and the phase 3 states, with a test that every route has one; the flows (a), (b), (c) and the screen-states spec (axe and the demo line on all 29); the phase 0 scaffold spec replaced by `app-shell.spec.ts`. *Part B (the e2e and render fixes, V-1, V-3, V-7, V-12, V-13):* 16 more render screens for the loading, failure and in-between states (`tests/e2e/support/network.ts` holds or aborts the page's own requests) and for TEST states the stack's control route now writes (`tests/e2e/setup/control.ts`; ADR 0037 decisions 9 to 11); `flows/inline-ask-refusal.spec.ts` (G8-21 on the page); flow (a) also reads the demo's steps 4 to 7 as rendered (V-3); `screen-states.spec.ts` also finds no dialog on any screen; the render adapter no longer treats a request still in flight when its test ended as a failure of another test (it had stopped a worker and left one test unrun); the render adapter reads the display objects a refusal carries (the guard's refused file, now named and bound, DR-10), and flow (b) and `app-shell.spec.ts` follow the web's part B changes (the refused file's served name; page titles "<page> – SOVITECH"); the UI kit's harness pages regenerated from the kit as it stands; the web halves of G7-3, G7-4 and G7-6 folded into their case files, against a TEST API served over HTTP on 127.0.0.1 (no `vi.stubGlobal`), and G7-6's count compared with no zero fallback (V-12); `.dependency-cruiser.cjs` narrowed (V-13).
- **A bug found by flow (b), fixed:** two uploads completing at once in a new project both added the extraction account as a member, and the second failed on the members' key (HTTP 500; the owner saw "This file could not be uploaded" and the upload stayed open). `lockProjectMembership` (packages/db) serialises it per project; `tests/api/uploads-concurrent-completion.test.ts` failed before the fix and passes after it (three rounds of three parallel uploads). Logged as a near miss in the 1.8 change log.

#### Part B (2026-10-01): the fixes, by area

Five fix groups, then the integrator (each finding with its proof is under "Guardrail review"):
- **API, store, question engine, resolver and contract.** Stale owner answers refused (`corrects` checked both ways) and rule 4's routing line only where a document or inference value is in the conflict (A-1, G4-36); invisible-only and control-character owner text refused (A-2, A-8: G7-9, G2-13; `isBlankOwnerText`, `holdsRefusedOwnerTextCharacter` in the contract's `common.ts`); a negative area refused (G8-22); a typed quantity's original stored (G8-23); "Something's wrong" refused on an engineer-verified value (G3-19); skips only where asked, once (G7-10); an independent served-view detector for `question_for_known_field` (G5-4, `apps/api/src/wizard/defects.ts`); `GET steps/8?add=` (G7-11); the investment outputs' 2.8 stage labels and the Proposal card's stage (G10-11); "You can provide this later." once per skipped multi-select (`ReviewCard.skippedQuestions`, G7-12); the 2.8 pending line on a file being read (`FileRow.status.line`); the refused upload's served name; the project list's type and city, newest first; session-bound CSRF tokens and session lifetimes (ADR 0038 decision 9). ADR 0036 decision 11 lists every contract addition.
- **UI kit and theme.** Progress with visible words, a 4px segment on an outlined track, still under reduced motion; tabular figures only on numeric displays; two heading roles (ADR 0040 decision 6); link buttons underlined; the qualifier after a comma; `SkipForNow`'s one placement; `ChoiceCard`'s `status` slot and Value's `compact` layout; the `row` layout that keeps a badge with its figure; served text isolated in a `<bdi>`; the root typecheck of the brand module's `.svg` types.
- **Web frame, shell, steps 1 to 4, UD-45 and the catalogue.** The demo line kept per project across a page session (G10-12); the header menu closes on focus out; owner text isolated outside Value; the 409 stale answers read the view again; step 3's intro wording and its read-only summary; step 4's decision in the card's status slot and its no-documents subtitle; progress words on step 2; the unit's name on the area box; widths; refused files named; loading frames; document titles per route; heading roles; Save as secondary; failure messages with their glyph; the file count hidden at zero; the list's type and city. Catalogue: 12 keys added, 7 removed, 2 values changed (none holds a digit or a reserved term).
- **Steps 5 to 8, the proposal page and the review lists.** Decision rows compact and named by option, For you before the outputs, the skipped line once, the served stage labels, `?add=` with focus, no reason headings that paraphrase 2.8, the inline ask's `corrects` and 409, the stored choice in the status slot on steps 5 to 7 (a conflict shown once under step 5's tiles), the unit's name, the banner hidden on failure, widths, loading frames, the skip placement, the piggy-bank icon, heading roles, failure glyphs.
- **E2E and render harness, the case halves, boundaries and this log.** 16 render screens (45 in all) for loading, failure and in-between states, written through the stack's TEST control route; the G8-21 refusal flow; flow (a) read on the page; no dialog on any screen; the render adapter's two fixes; the web halves of G7-3, G7-4 and G7-6 in their case files; G7-6's count; the e2e stack's boundary rule; this log's corrections (R-046, V-8's reading, V-9, DR-7, DR-21, the gap that remains) and ADRs 0037 and 0039.
- **The integrator.** Every page now names its step on a skip, and the contract requires it (ADR 0036 decision 12); step 4's `no_documents` subtitle served by the API (DR-25, with API tests); G5-3's API half through the API's own logging (`logPlanDefects`); G7-8's incidental line assertion moved to the question (its Expected cell unchanged); G5-4 fed the view the API served (an object with a `skip` key is read as a held-out test); the boundary rule's two seeded inputs and README row; `openItems`' doc comment (R-046 not applied); the kit's narrow-tile badge, whose words now wrap inside its pill (`ui.css`, `ui-css.test.ts`); a kit harness page of the part B layouts (`tests/e2e/pages/ui/layout.html`) with Chromium assertions in `ui-kit.spec.ts` (badge with its figure in a 300px row, the status slot on the card, the 24px skip placement, the progress words and its still 4px segment, isolated owner text); ADR 0039's uploads and demo-line bullets; the twelve new cases indexed (guardrails 1.8; pin 185).

#### After the final verification (2026-10-01): the fix round, by area

The final verifier's seven new problems (under "Guardrail review"), then two fixers and the integrator:
- **API and store.** The owner's writes of a project run one after the other: `lockProjectWrites` (`packages/db/src/scope.ts`, exported by `@sovitech/db`), a transaction-level advisory lock keyed by the project, taken in `ownerState` (`apps/api/src/wizard/service.ts`) after `requireOwner` and before the state is read, and by `ensureBuildingSubject` before the building's own lock (one lock order); the served views' defect logging (`logServedAsksForKnownFields`, `logPlanDefects`) takes it before it reads the events, only when it has something to log. File names served through `servedFileName` (`apps/api/src/documents/file-names.ts`) in every display that names a file; the stored name kept. Rule 4's routing line only where a `document` or `ai_inference` value is in the conflict (`packages/view-model/src/resolver/index.ts`). ADR 0036 decision 13.
- **Web.** One request per press on every owner write (`useInFlight`, `apps/web/src/wizard/use-in-flight.ts`), never disabled, `aria-busy` while waiting; the kit's `SkipForNow` gains `busy`; step 8 keeps the existing `edit.changed` sentence when an ask disappears after a 409; the Proposal card names a stage only while a figure at that stage can be produced, else its output's served "Not available yet" line with its Add action. The component harness gains `pressTwice`, `heldHandler`, `sentTo` and `settle` (`apps/web/src/test/harness.tsx`) for later tests of owner writes. ADR 0039 decision 11.
- **The integrator.** The fixers' three drafted cases indexed (G2-14, G4-38, G7-13; G4-37 stays reserved for P-3B-CONFLICT-NO-DOCUMENT); G10-11's Expected and case file aligned with rules 7 and 10 as written, the case file now `G10-11.test.tsx` with its rendered half (the app's own step 8 against the responses the API served the same TEST project; no test double); a test of views served at once added to G5-3 and to G5-4 (their Expected unchanged); the concurrent API tests retitled with the new ids; the bidirectional control characters in two test sources written as `\u` escapes; the stage-label doc comments reworded (`steps.ts`, `outputs.ts`, `views.ts`; no behaviour change); three UI kit harness pages regenerated after the kit's `SkipForNow` change; ADR 0036 decision 13 and ADR 0039 decision 11; this log. Each new or changed case was mutation-checked: with the lock lines removed G4-38, G7-13 and the new G5-3 and G5-4 tests fail (8 of 8); with the file names served as stored G2-14 fails (2 of 2); with the card naming the served stage G10-11's rendered half fails.

#### Render allowlist and exception lists

No allowlist entry added (`render.entries` still `date-day-month-year`, `wizard-step-number`, `character-counter`, `max-file-size`; `render.unreadable` still `brand-logo`). One boundary allowance widened in `.dependency-cruiser.cjs` (not an exception list the loosening check records): `db-only-from-api` lets `tests/e2e/setup/` reach `@sovitech/db` (the e2e stack imports `@sovitech/db/testing` only), recorded in ADR 0037 decision 8. **Narrowed in part B (V-13):** the exemption let the setup import the whole store while its comment and ADR 0037 said `/testing` only; a separate rule, `e2e-setup-reaches-db-only-through-testing`, now refuses any `@sovitech/db` import from `tests/e2e/setup/` other than `@sovitech/db/testing` (ADR 0037 decision 11). Proven on seeded imports in a scratch copy of the boundary fixture (an `@sovitech/db` import from `tests/e2e/setup/` violates exactly that rule; an `@sovitech/db/testing` import violates none; the 39 existing seeded files keep their expected rules); the integrator added the two seeds to `tools/eslint-rules/fixtures/seeded/depcruise/tests/e2e/setup/` with their expectations, and the rule's row to `tools/eslint-rules/README.md`; `depcruise.test.ts` ("flags every seeded bad import with the expected rules, and nothing else") passes.

#### The ADRs of the phase

0035 (frontend dependencies), 0036 (the wizard API contract; amended: decision 9, B3's decisions; decision 10, the render adapter as built), 0037 (the e2e setup; amended: decision 8, as built), 0038 (the development login; amended: decisions 6 to 8), 0039 (navigation, saving, late findings, Generate; amended: decision 10, the web builders' readings), 0040 (the app theme tokens; amended: Brand values only), 0041 (performance budgets, Proposed), 0042 (the view-model's readings). Each "Accepted: default, reversible", except 0041 "Proposed". *Amended in part B:* 0036 decision 11 (every additive contract change of the server fixes) and decision 12 (the integrator: `SkipRequest.step` required, step 4's `no_documents` subtitle, `logPlanDefects`); 0037 decisions 9 to 11 (held and aborted requests, the control route's TEST states, the e2e stack's boundary rule); 0038 decision 9 (session lifetimes, session-bound CSRF tokens, `_csrf` cleared on sign-in and sign-out); 0039 decision 10 (step 3's read-only summary, the refused file's name, the demo line while loading or failing, skips naming their page, `?add=`) and its Consequences (DR-21); 0040 decision 6 (the two heading roles and the progress outline, pending the owner's OK); 0042 decision 2 (V-8's reading). *Amended after the final verification:* 0036 decision 13 (the owner's writes serialised per project and the lock order; the defect logging under the same lock; file names as served; the routing line's condition; what `proposal.stageLabel` is for; the step 8 skip that cannot be refused before D-10) and 0039 decision 11 (one request per press; step 8's "changed" message; the Proposal card's stage; what is not guarded yet).

#### Screenshot comparison (each step against design/reference/onboarding/, on the 1440 canvas)

Screenshots of every checked screen: the scratchpad's `build/p3/screens/` (flows (a), (b) and (c) with `SOVITECH_E2E_SCREENSHOTS`). Differences, each marked **B** (the brand decision, 2026-09-24), **G** (guardrails section 5, a rule, or the PRD's reading of them), **A** (the render allowlist as it stands; a proposal is written), **D** (no data: no API key, no dataset) or **L** (a layout choice of the build, for the design review in part B):

- **Every step.** B: the logo in place of the "SOVITECH" wordmark, no tagline; white primary button (mint on hover), mint only for the current step and the focus ring; hairline borders, 1px and 2px radii, no glow; values at weight 400. D-19 pending (ADR 0040 decision 2): the proposed row is not used (re-marked in part B, DR-7; these were marked B, but they are D-19's working rule, not the brand decision): completed steps a white ring with a check, not teal; badges one neutral pill (no azure, violet or outline colours); the title in the 34px weight 300 title role, not bold. A: no "STEP n OF 8" eyebrow; the header shows the date only ("1 Oct 2026", no weekday, no time, not upper-cased). G: the demo line under the header on every screen of the demo project (not in the mockups). L: icons 32 px or smaller (the mockups' 40 px card icons and 64 px dropzone icon); the project name in the header not upper-cased.
- **Step 1.** G (§5-1): Country before City; City a text field, no city list (D-94); no project type preselected. Otherwise as drawn (no title block, Next only).
- **Step 2.** G and UD-33: under the dropzone, "Your files" with the bound count and one row per file: its name (bound), Stage and Revision (each Unknown with its badge; no classifier), and its coverage or 2.8 line ("pages 1-3 of 3", "Partly analysed (37 of 40 pages)", "Not analysed: IFC model stored, not analysed"); rows refused on the page or by the fixtures-only guard, with the reason and Dismiss; "Stop uploading" while an upload runs; the five tiles as hints with no state. L: the dropzone wider than drawn (860px against about 743; corrected in part B, DR-7: part A said narrower), now 745px (DR-9); the tiles below the file list.
- **Step 3.** G: no success banner (R-045 leaves US-REVIEW-04 AC12 off; 2.8's lines only); no viewer, 3D/2D/Wireframe toggle or "All floors" selector (US-REVIEW-04 AC14, 5.2 "No IFC uploaded"): a bordered "Building model" area with the model's G12-1 line, or "Not available yet: an IFC model of the building" with "Upload a model"; "Files not fully read" with each file's 2.8 line in the banner's place; no "Systems" row; "HVAC assets" reads "Not available yet: SOVITECH asset taxonomy"; the pill counts only confirmations as "<n> things for you to check", and the warning row never says "before we continue". D: every fact reads Unknown or Not provided yet (the mockups' figures are AI-generated demo content); the intro reads "No building facts have come from your documents yet." L: summary rows stacked (label, value with its badge, source line, Edit) rather than label-left and value-right; "Looks right", "Something's wrong" and the evidence excerpt in the detail rows.
- **Step 4.** G (§5-4a to §5-4c): no "Detected" or "Optional" badges; each card shows "In your documents" (Unknown, with no detection field) and, when there is one, "Your choice" (Included or Not included, Provided by you); Fire Safety's description is the monitoring-only text (as the guardrails write it, starting lower-case); nothing pre-ticked but the owner's stored decisions; "Skip for now" under the grid while nothing is ticked. D-19 pending (ADR 0040 decision 2): the proposed row is not used: the info banner neutral, not cyan (re-marked in part B, DR-7; part A marked it B).
- **Step 5.** G: the order is building type, schedule, occupancy (rule 6's impactRank); "Around the clock" for "24 / 7" (A: P-3-RENDER-24-7); no note field (R-007, D-11); no preselection (R-006); occupancy has the registry's three options (Mostly occupied, Mixed, Low occupancy: the registry keeps "Seasonal" on the schedule only, onboarding-spec 6.1's proposal, taken in phase 1). L: a stored answer shows under its question in a bordered row with its badge.
- **Step 6.** G: no "Other" card and no note field (onboarding Q11, R-007); no preselection. L: the "Reduce operating costs" icon is Lucide's coins, whose face draws a stroke that can read as a "1" (a design-review item; no digit in the DOM).
- **Step 7.** G: no preselection (R-005). Otherwise as drawn, with the approved banner.
- **Step 8.** G: the cards list registered rows ("Occupancy", not "Building use"; one row per decision field, "System in scope: HVAC" …), so they are much taller; the Proposal card says a preliminary proposal is generated (§5-8), never "Ready to generate"; below the grid, "What your proposal will show" with the inline asks and each output's "Not available yet" line, "For you" (top three and "and N more") and "SOVITECH will check"; "Generate Proposal" never disabled. A: no unit symbol beside the area input (P-3-INPUT-UNIT-SYMBOL). D: every output reads "Not available yet" naming the SOVITECH dataset it waits for.
- **Screens with no approved design** (UD-36 sign-in, UD-37 project list, UD-16 menu, UD-45, UD-07 and the proposal page, the empty, error and editing states): designed within the brand with the `frontend-design` skill; for the design review.
- **Differences part A did not name** (added in part B from the design review, DR-7; each with its reason, and whether a part B fix changes it, as the fixes stand in the working tree on 2026-10-01; the integrator's rerun screenshots confirm):
  - Step 1 of a stored project shows value rows with Edit, not the input form (G: R-009, "a project reopened before Generate opens at step 1 with every stored answer shown and nothing asked again"). Not changed.
  - Steps 6 and 7 showed a decision row in every card (L). Changed by part B (DR-2): a stored decision sits on its card's top row, once, with its badge (2.8 "Prominence"), and no row repeats it.
  - Step 4's badge sat below the description and the card was about twice the drawn height (L). Changed by part B (DR-2): the detection and the decision sit in the card's top row, the place the approved screen gives a badge, and the "Your choice" row is gone.
  - Step 3 showed Edit twice, in the summary and in Extracted details (L). Changed by part B (DR-3; ADR 0039 decision 10): the summary rows carry no action.
  - Step 5's icon was not in a gutter beside its question, and the column width differed (L). Changed by part B: the icon sits in a left gutter, so the title, the helper line, the options and "Skip for now" share one left edge (`Step5.tsx`).
  - The widths of step 7 and of step 1 differed from the drawn ones (L). Changed by part B (DR-9): step 1's form and footer 880px, step 2's dropzone 745px, step 5 904px, step 7 1,070px.
  - Pages are taller than one 900px viewport on steps 2 to 5 and 8 (L on steps 2 to 5: the file list under the dropzone, stacked rows; G on step 8: one registered row per field, the inline asks, For you and SOVITECH will check). Partly changed by part B (DR-2 shortens the step 4 to 7 cards).
  - The header date is in Inter with tabular figures, not a monospaced face (B: Inter is the brand's one family).
  - Back is the brand's secondary button, not the drawn ghost link (B).
  - Step 4's subtitle when no document names a system (G: US-SCOPE-01 AC10, the approved "We've detected the following systems in your documents." shows only when a document names one). Changed by part B (DR-25): a project with no document reads "No documents were uploaded, so no system was read from them."
- **After part B's fixes** (the groups' and the integrator's measurements at 1440, 2026-10-01): step 3's summary is a read-only key/value list and every action sits once in Extracted details (L item resolved); step 4's "Your choice" row is gone, the decision and "In your documents" sit on the card's top row, and Fire Safety's text starts with a capital and ends with a full stop; step 2's "Your files" count is hidden while no file is stored, and a file being uploaded or read says so in words beside its bar; step 5 has no echo box, the chosen tile carries its value and badge (under the title on tiles and pills, the badge's words wrapping inside its pill on the six-column tiles), a conflict shows once under the tiles, the content is 904px with the icon in a 56px gutter, and Continue sits at y=1097 on the demo (1202 before); steps 6 and 7 show each stored decision on its card's top row with no Selected or Not selected rows, step 7 is 1,070px wide, and the piggy-bank icon replaces the coins; step 8's decision rows are dense and named by option in the served order, For you comes before the outputs, the investment outputs and the Proposal card name their served 2.8 stage labels (after the final verification, NP-3, G: the card names a stage only while a figure at that stage can be produced; with every gate closed it shows the "Not available yet: …" line of the output that carries the stage, with its Add action where the owner has one, in the stage label's place; rule 7's `first_estimate` row and rule 10's stage 1), the "Everything ready?" banner is hidden on load failure, failures show beside the alert icon, and the page is 2,724px on the demo (Generate at y=2636) and 3,290px on a new project (y=3202); every page has a document title ("<page> – SOVITECH"). For the design review (L): on step 5 a chosen tile shows its name twice (the title, then the served value with its badge); step 8's lists under the grid still make the page long (question 9).
