### Exit criteria

Prompt 3 phase 3: "Playwright flows pass. (a) The demo project from step 1 to step 8. (b) A new project with fixture uploads. (c) A new project with no documents that skips everything and still reaches step 8. On every step and state: the render test, axe with zero violations, the reserved-term scan and the demo line (demo project). There are no `question_for_known_field` events in (a). You compared each step with its screenshot and noted the differences in the build log."

| Criterion | Holds? | How |
|---|---|---|
| (a) The demo from step 1 to step 8 | **holds** | `flows/demo.spec.ts`: the project list, step 1 (stored answers, nothing asked), steps 2 to 8, UD-45 from step 3 and back, Generate, the proposal page (11 screens checked) |
| (b) A new project with fixture uploads | **holds** | `flows/fixture-uploads.spec.ts`: three fixtures (PDF and XLSX read in the sandbox, the IFC model stored "Not analysed"), a non-fixture PDF refused by the owner's guard, a format refused on the page; steps 3 to 7 answered, the gross floor area typed at step 8's inline ask and stored, Generate (9 screens checked) |
| (c) No documents, everything skipped, step 8 reached | **holds** | `flows/no-documents.spec.ts`: sign-in, the list, step 1 empty and with its four inline errors (G7-6: no project created), steps 2 and 3 with nothing, every "Skip for now" on steps 4 to 7 pressed, step 8, Generate, the proposal page (16 screens checked); Continue and Generate enabled throughout |
| Render test on every step and state | **holds for the states named here** (corrected in part B, V-1: part A's "every step and state" covered only settled screens) | each flow checks every screen it reaches (`flows/screen-checks.ts`, a new observation per screen, display objects from the API through the contract); the render project checks the 45 screens and states of `screens.ts`, which cover every `APP_PATHS` route. **Settled (part A, 29):** signed out and sent to sign-in; sign-in; the project list; the header menu open; step 1 of a new project empty and with its four inline errors; the demo's steps 1 to 8 with their stored answers, step 1's inline editor open, UD-45 and the proposal page; a new project's step 1 stored, step 2 empty and with a refused format, step 3 with no documents, steps 4 and 5 unanswered and skipped, steps 6 and 7 unanswered, step 8 with its inline asks, and the proposal page. **Added in part B (16):** loading, the step view held (the demo's step 3; a new project's step 8; UD-34, UD-35); load failure, the view and the project list aborted (the demo's step 8, reached by Continue from step 7; a new project's step 8; a new project's proposal page, UD-47); step 2 with a file uploading (its chunk held), a file being read (a TEST document queued) and files dragged over the dropzone (UD-33); step 3 with a document still being read and with the inline editor open (UD-34); step 5 with the building type in conflict ("Two values") and with an inferred building type to confirm ("Yes, it's a hotel"); step 8 with conflicts put to the owner ("Choose this value") and more than three For you items ("and <n> more"), with the confirmation among For you ("Yes, it's a hotel", Yes and Edit), and with "Still reading <n> files"; step 6 with a floors conflict that arrived after the owner left step 3 (the dot and the quiet notice, G7-4). The TEST states are written through the stack's control route into TEST projects (ADR 0037 decision 11). **In a flow:** step 8's inline ask refusing "1.500" (G8-21; `flows/inline-ask-refusal.spec.ts`), read by the render test once the owner has cleared the box (P-3B-RENDER-TYPED-INPUT). **Not checked:** "Is this right? Yes · Edit" on a field other than the building type (the production registry allows no other owner confirmation); a document whose analysis failed; a source document removed |
| Axe, zero violations (WCAG 2.2 AA tags) | **holds for the states named above** | on every screen of the flows, and on all 45 render screens (`flows/screen-states.spec.ts`; a loading or uploading screen is read while its request is held); the refused "1.500" both with the typed text and with the box cleared; the UI kit pages (`ui-kit.spec.ts`). One violation found and fixed on the way (a refused file row's `role="alert"` in place of its list item). `screen-states.spec.ts` also finds no `dialog` or `alertdialog` role and no modal element on any of the 45 (rule 7; G7-4 in the browser) |
| Reserved-term scan of the rendered copy | **holds for the states named above** | part of every render check (text, accessible names, titles, placeholders, generated content), with the registered allowances only, so it runs on the 45 render screens and on every flow screen; each flow also asserts the scan read copy on every screen; the repository's reserved-term check passes on the catalogue |
| The demo line, demo project only | **holds for the states named above, with the gap below** | on every screen of flow (a) and on every demo render screen, its loading (the step view held, the project list answered) and its load failure (reached from step 7, so the page knows the demo) included; on the demo's list row only; on no screen of flows (b) and (c) or of the new-project render screens, their loading and failure states included; G10-10 at the API, G10-12 in the web |
| No `question_for_known_field` in (a) | **holds** | read after flow (a) through the stack's TEST-only control route: none, and no `confirmation_budget_exceeded` either (the route reads whatever detector logged them, the question engine's and the served-view detector of part B). And on the page (part B, V-3): on steps 4 to 7 of the demo every answer the demo seed stores (`fixtures/demo/owner-answers.json`) renders as that question's chosen option, and no "Skip for now" and no "You can provide this later." renders |
| Each step compared with its screenshot | **holds** | "Screenshot comparison" under "Built" above; screenshots in the scratchpad's `build/p3/screens/` |

Not an exit criterion of the phase, reported: part B (the verifier, the adversarial review, the design review) has not run; G7-4's "no dialog" half is a web component test, not an e2e flow. *Part B:* the reviews ran; G7-4's rendered half is now in its case file, and the e2e render screen of step 6 shows the dot and the notice in a browser with no dialog.

**Re-run after part B's integration** (the integrator, 2026-10-01): every row above holds on the final tree: `pnpm e2e` 143 passed twice (flows (a), (b), (c), the G8-21 refusal flow, the 45 screens through the render test, axe and the demo line, the UI kit's 8 pages), and `pnpm test:render` 72 passed alone and inside `pnpm check`. The UI kit's new `ui/layout.html` adds Chromium checks of part B's layouts (a badge on its figure's line in a 300px row, the status slot on the card and inside a six-column tile, the 24px skip placement, progress words and a still 4px segment under reduced motion, isolated owner text).

**Re-run after the fix round that followed the final verification** (the integrator, 2026-10-01): every row above holds on the final tree: `pnpm e2e` 143 passed (flows (a), (b), (c), the G8-21 refusal flow, the 45 screens through the render test, axe and the demo line, the UI kit's 8 pages, regenerated after the kit's `SkipForNow` change), and `pnpm test:render` 72 passed inside `pnpm check`. Flow (a) still reads no `question_for_known_field` and no `confirmation_budget_exceeded`; flow (c) presses every "Skip for now" on steps 4 to 7 through the new one-request-per-press guard and still reaches step 8, where the Proposal card now shows the "Not available yet" line of the output that carries the stage, under the render test and axe. On screen the round changed step 8's Proposal card (NP-3; the screenshot comparison's step 8 entries are amended for it) and added `aria-busy` to the owner-write controls while a request is on its way; the page heights measured in part B were not measured again.

**A gap that remains, stated** (part B, V-1 and A-10, after the web fix of the demo line, G10-12): a cold direct load of a demo screen (a reload or a typed link) where every request fails, the project list and the step view alike, cannot know it is the demo, so it shows its load failure without the demo line. The demo line shows on a demo screen that is loading or failing once any response of the page session has said the project is the demo. No render screen claims the cold, all-failing state.

### Guardrail review

**Part A's own findings** (found while building, before the reviews), each fixed and logged in the 1.8 change log: the concurrent upload completion (500, owner blocked from that file); the step 2 alert role inside a list (axe); the render adapter still on phase 0's placeholder route (no display object of the app would have been read); the seeded run-guard specs inheriting the new global setup (the self-test would have failed for a reason not its own); the render screens' sign-in racing the app's redirect (a screen could be checked on the project list instead of step 1); a number word typed into a TEST project name read as a bare number (the names now carry none); a display-object response delivered to a page that was navigating away counted as a problem and failed one render screen in the second `pnpm check` (the delivery to the replaced document is now dropped; the next document still receives everything collected, and a value it shows unannounced still fails; ADR 0037 decision 8).

**Part B (2026-10-01).** The verifier (13 findings, V-1 to V-13, with V-4a), the read-only adversarial reviewer (15 findings, A-1 to A-15) and the design review (27 findings, DR-1 to DR-27) ran on part A's working tree (prompt 3 section 12); `git status` was the same before and after each (their snapshots in the scratchpad's `build/p3b/{verifier,adversarial,design}/`). A triage sorted the findings into five fix groups (the API, store and view-model; the UI kit and theme; the web frame and steps 1 to 4; steps 5 to 8 and the review lists; the e2e and render harness and this log), wrote four proposals, rejected eight findings or parts of them with reasons, and logged three for later phases. Each group reproduced its findings first, then fixed them with a permanent proof; the integrator then did what the groups asked of each other, indexed the twelve new cases (guardrails 1.8, both change-log rows extended) and re-ran every check. **Result, over 54 distinct findings (56 raised: 14 by the verifier, 15 by the adversarial review, 27 by the design review; A-10 and A-13 repeat V-1 and V-11 and are counted once, with them; corrected after the final verification, which found the earlier tally of 56 counting them twice): 44 fixed (A-3, V-7, DR-1, DR-2, DR-6 and DR-25 in part), 1 needing no change (DR-26, now under test), 1 written as a proposal (A-9), 4 logged (V-4 and V-9 in this log; A-5 and A-14, with A-3's plausibility half, for later phases), 4 rejected (A-12, V-8's code change, DR-8, DR-21) with the rejected parts of DR-2, DR-6, DR-25 and V-11. Proposals written in part B: P-3B-CONFLICT-NO-DOCUMENT, the extension of dashboards 7.2.29, P-3B-OWNER-TEXT-AS-QUOTATION, P-3B-SKIPPED-MULTISELECT-ONE-ROW (the triage's) and P-3B-RENDER-TYPED-INPUT (the e2e group's). Owner questions: none new from the triage; "Questions for the owner" adds those the proposals raise.**

Each finding below carries the severity its own review gave it (corrected after the final verification: this log had raised V-1 to high, and A-6 to A-11, V-3 to V-7 and V-11 to V-13 one step each).

**Adversarial findings.**
- **A-1 (high) An owner's answer from a stale screen became a second owner value. Fixed, including answers sent together (the project write lock, after the final verification).** On a TEST project with no documents, two Edits of the gross floor area, both naming no shown value, both answered 200; the area read "1,200 to 1,300 m²", Two values, "Documents disagree on this. A SOVITECH engineer will check it.", and step 8 listed it under SOVITECH will check; the same on Continue with the building type ("Hotel or Office", put to the owner). Now `writeOwnerAnswer` (`apps/api/src/wizard/answers.ts`) keeps its no-op for an answer equal to the owner's own value, then refuses 409 `shown_value_changed` when the field shows a value the request did not name in `corrects`; `planContinue` (`packages/view-model/src/intake/continue.ts`) refuses the same for a single-choice or text answer (multi-select rows unchanged: derive reads the owner's latest decision there). The resolver pushes rule 4's routing line only when a value in the conflict is not a person's entry (a document or an inference): the line's words are about documents, and on a project with none it was untrue; the Two values badge, each value's source line and the "SOVITECH will check" listing stay. The owner-facing wording for a conflict of entries alone (an owner's answer against an engineer's site entry) is proposal P-3B-CONFLICT-NO-DOCUMENT; no sentence was invented. The web reads the step again on the 409 and says so (`copy.edit.changed`): Continue (WizardProvider), InlineEditor and step 8's InlineAsk, which now sends the served `shownCandidateIds`. Proofs: **G4-36** (edit, Continue and resolver paths over a TEST database), `intake.test.ts` and `resolver.test.ts` ("G4-36 · …"), `Step3.test.tsx`, `Step4.test.tsx`, `InlineEditor.test.tsx` ("A-1 · rule 4 …"), `Step8.test.tsx` ("… A-1 …"). *After the final verification* (its new problem 1): the fix held for answers sent one at a time only; two sent together both stored ("Two values 1,200 to 1,300 m²", "Hotel or Office", 6 of 6 rounds). Now the owner's writes of a project take its write lock before they read, and each control sends one request per press: **G4-38**; `tests/api/wizard-owner-writes-concurrent.test.ts`; the web's component tests titled "A-1".
- **A-2 (medium) Required fields of invisible characters created nameless projects. Fixed:** one helper, `isBlankOwnerText` (the contract's `common.ts`, so the web may reuse it), treats text as empty once white space, default-ignorable and format characters, U+180E, the Hangul fillers and the Braille blank are removed; project creation, Next on step 1, Edit and `parseOwnerAnswer` use it (422 `required_fields_missing` naming the field; no project row). Proof: **G7-9**; `intake.test.ts` "G7-9 · G2-13 · …".
- **A-3 (medium) A negative area was stored, and so were 0, 0.0001 and 999999999999999. Fixed in part:** `parseOwnerQuantity` refuses a value below zero for an area, a volume or a length (`answer_invalid`; no registry change: `valueShape` is in the loosening snapshot). Proof: **G8-22**. Logged for phase 5: zero and implausibly large values become "Please check" and stay out of totals once the field has a plausible range (rule 8's plausibility check; SOVITECH ranges, D-93).
- **A-4 (medium) An owner-typed quantity's original text was not stored, under a comment that said it was. Fixed:** `parseOwnerAnswer` returns `original: { text }` for a quantity; `OwnerValue` gains `original` (`packages/domain/src/correction.ts`), and every owner write carries it to the store, which writes `original_text` for `user` candidates; the comment is corrected; the display is unchanged (rule 9 shows "as written" for document values only). Proofs: **G8-23**; `resolver.test.ts` and `correction.test.ts` ("G8-23 · …").
- **A-5 (medium) The reserved-term list misses forms such as "compliance", "guarantee", "certify", "exactly", so the approved goal card "Ensure compliance" passes. Logged:** the term list is the approver's (removing or adding a term is section 10's); written as the extension of dashboards proposal 7.2.29 ("Proposals"); the approved card stays until the approver decides, then the owner gives its neutral wording.
- **A-6 (low) "Something's wrong" on an engineer-verified value appended a rejection. Fixed:** `planConcern` refuses 409 `shown_value_changed` when the candidate's derived verification is engineer_verified (the page never offers the action there). Proof: **G3-19**; `intake.test.ts` "G3-19 · …".
- **A-7 (low) A repeated or early skip wrote twice and took the area off step 8's asks; Continue skipped a confirmation the page only claimed it showed. Fixed, including skips sent together (the project write lock, after the final verification):** a skip is recorded only where its question is asked now (its step's plan, step 8's inline ask, or the ask served on the owner's "Add"), a repeat writes nothing, and anything else is 422 `answer_invalid`; Continue skips a confirmation only when the server shows it now. The skip names the page's step; the integrator made every page send it (steps 4 to 7 their own number, step 8's Generate and "Generate without it" 8) and the contract require it (ADR 0036 decision 12), which closes the residual the server group named (a skip from no page). Proofs: **G7-10**; `intake.test.ts` "G7-10 · …"; `Step4.test.tsx`, `Step5.test.tsx`, `Step8.test.tsx` ("G7-10 (web half)"). *After the final verification* (its new problem 1): three goal skips sent together wrote 21 events in place of 7, and a browser double click on step 4's "Skip for now" (which had no busy guard) wrote the eight systems' skips twice and took the systems off step 8's asks. Now the write lock, and one request per press: **G7-13**; `tests/api/wizard-owner-writes-concurrent.test.ts`; the component tests titled "A-7 · G7-10 (web half)"; a browser probe (one worker, under the e2e lock) double-clicking "Skip for now" on steps 4 and 6: 15 `skipped` events over 15 fields, and step 8 still asks for the systems.
- **A-8 (low) A NUL answered 500, a lone surrogate was stored as U+FFFD, and a U+202E project name rendered as "TEST Provided by". Fixed** in three layers: the API refuses control characters, lone surrogates and the bidirectional controls in owner text with 422 `answer_invalid` (`holdsRefusedOwnerTextCharacter`; creation now builds its four answers through `parseOwnerAnswer`); the Value component renders served text in a `<bdi>` with `unicode-bidi: isolate`; the header, the project list and step 1 isolate the name and city outside Value. Proofs: **G2-13**; `Value.test.tsx` and `ui-css.test.ts` ("G2-13 · A-8 · …"); `sign-in-and-projects.test.tsx` "A-8 · …"; `NewProjectStep1.test.tsx` "G2-13 · A-8 · G7-6 …"; in Chromium, `ui-kit.spec.ts` on the new `ui/layout.html` (visual order copy, text, badge, copy).
- **A-9 (low) Owner text that repeats app words ("Verified Final Offer 12" as a city; a project named with the demo line's words) shows unmarked. Proposal** P-3B-OWNER-TEXT-AS-QUOTATION: refusing such names would be a new blocked state, which is not ours to add; meanwhile owner text renders isolated, as Provided by you, and no demo line comes from anything but the project's flag (G10-10).
- **A-10 (low) = V-1's demo-line half: with the project list and the step view pending or failing, the demo's step 3 showed no demo line. Fixed** (V-1 below).
- **A-11 (low) CSRF tokens were not bound to the session and survived sign-in and sign-out; sessions never expired. Fixed:** the token is bound to the live session (`@fastify/csrf-protection` `getUserInfo`), `_csrf` is cleared on sign-in and sign-out, and sessions have an idle (30 minutes) and an absolute (12 hours) lifetime, settings with safe defaults (`SOVITECH_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES`, `SOVITECH_SESSION_ABSOLUTE_HOURS`; ADR 0038 decision 9). The web already fetched a new token after a 403. Proofs: `tests/api/session-csrf.test.ts` (5 tests), `apps/api/src/auth/sessions.test.ts`, `config.test.ts` ("A-11 …"), `tests/api/wizard-flow.test.ts`. Not indexed: security basics (prompt 3 section 11), no guardrail sentence.
- **A-12 (low) The approved banners of steps 7 and 8 and step 2's subtitle speak of an AI step. Rejected:** PRD US-INTAKE-10 and US-INTAKE-16 Notes keep them as drawn and the copy review is dashboards proposal 7.2.15 (not approved); "Product doc issues" already lists them; step 3's reading intro is taken under DR-13.
- **A-13 = V-11. Fixed** (below).
- **A-14 (low) Emergency and escape lighting (DALI emergency luminaires) and door release on escape routes appear in step 4 and step 7 card copy. Logged for phase 5:** the generated scope text and point list keep them monitoring-only (rule 11; G11-1, G11-3); the approved card copy stays (PRD US-INTAKE-10 Notes already record that Security & Access reaches into safety monitoring).
- **A-15 (low) The header menu stayed open over the page after focus left it (WCAG 2.4.11). Fixed:** it closes on focusout outside its container; Escape still returns focus to the button. Proof: `sign-in-and-projects.test.tsx` "A-15 · WCAG 2.4.11 · UD-16 …".

**Verifier findings.**
- **V-1 (medium) "On every step and state" was claimed for settled screens only, and the demo line was missing while a demo screen loaded or failed. Fixed.** The web keeps what any response of the page session said about a project's demo flag (`SessionProvider.rememberProject`, the list's Open in router state) and shows the served line while a screen loads and after it fails; the comments name the one gap left (a cold direct load where every request fails). The render list grew from 29 to 45 screens: loading (the view held) and load failure (the view and the list aborted) on the demo and on a new project, UD-47, step 2 uploading, being read and drag-over, step 3 still reading and with the inline editor open, step 5 with "Two values" and with "Yes, it's a hotel", step 8 with "Choose this value" and "and <n> more", with the confirmation among For you, and with "Still reading <n> files", and step 6 with a late finding's dot and notice; each runs the render test with its reserved-term scan, axe (WCAG 2.2 AA) and the demo line, and finds no dialog. The TEST states come from the stack's loopback control route (TEST projects only; ADR 0037 decisions 9 to 11). Step 8's inline ask refusing "1.500" is checked in a flow (`inline-ask-refusal.spec.ts`). Proofs: **G10-12**; `screens.ts`, `screen-states.spec.ts`; "Exit criteria" names exactly what is checked and what is not.
- **V-2 (medium) Step 8 named the investment outputs "Investment estimate from benchmarks" and "… from your project's data", not 2.8's stage labels. Fixed:** `OutputAvailability.label` serves "Indicative range" and "Preliminary investment estimate" as stage-label lines, and the Proposal card the served `proposal.stageLabel` (derived from stored state; never "Formal quotation"); the catalogue keys are gone. Proofs: **G10-11**; `Step8.test.tsx` and `ProposalPage.test.tsx` ("… G10-11 (web half) …"). *After the final verification* (its new problem 3): the card named "Preliminary investment estimate" with no figure behind it, on every project whose first-estimate inputs are missing, the demo included; it now names a stage only while a figure at that stage can be produced (below).
- **V-3 (low) The `question_for_known_field` check read the planner's own plans, so it could never fire, and flow (a)'s zero proved nothing. Fixed:** an independent detector (`apps/api/src/wizard/defects.ts`) reads each served view and each field's derived state and logs once per field; flow (a) also reads the demo's steps 4 to 7 as rendered (every seeded answer shown, no "Skip for now", no "You can provide this later."). Proofs: **G5-4** (fed the step 5 view the API served before the answer), `flows/demo.spec.ts`.
- **V-4 (low) PRD R-046's interim (engineer fields whose source was removed go to SOVITECH will check) against guardrails 2.3 ("For you"). Logged:** the guardrails win; the Plan marks the interim not applied, "Product doc issues" records it, and `openItems`' doc comment now says so (the integrator).
- **V-4a (medium) After Generate skipped the area, "Add gross floor area" opened step 8 with no ask. Fixed:** `GET steps/8?add=<field key>` serves a first-estimate field's inline ask whatever its skip count (the owner asked for it; no `question_for_known_field`); the proposal page's Add passes the field, and step 8 focuses the ask. Proofs: **G7-11**; `ProposalPage.test.tsx` "G7-11 · US-INTAKE-22 AC6 · R-012 …".
- **V-5 (low) "For you" headings paraphrased 2.8 and rule 4 lines beside the served ones, and `routed_to_engineer` had its own sentence. Fixed:** no heading for a conflict or a removed source (the served display carries its badge and line); `routed_to_engineer` reads the view again with no sentence; the keys are removed. Proof: `Step8.test.tsx` "… V-5 …".
- **V-6 (low) Step 8 repeated "You can provide this later." on every option of a skipped multi-select (8, 7 and 6 times in flow (c)). Fixed:** the resolver no longer puts the line on a decision field's display; each step 8 card serves `skippedQuestions`, rendered once under its question's rows; rows are ordered chosen, not chosen, not provided. Proofs: **G7-12**; `resolver.test.ts`, `Step8.test.tsx` "G7-12 · …". G7-8's case file followed (its incidental assertion, not its Expected cell).
- **V-7 (low) The web halves of G7-3, G7-4 and G7-6 lived outside their case files, and G5-3 proved only the planner's flag. Fixed in part:** G7-3, G7-4 and G7-6 are `.tsx` case files that render the real routes against a TEST API over HTTP (no test double); G5-3 now also runs the API's own logging (`logPlanDefects`, exported by the integrator for it) over a TEST database: one `confirmation_budget_exceeded` per field over the budget, none more on a second read. Not yet: a run through the served step view, which needs a seam that hands the API a TEST registry ("Waiting").
- **V-8 (low) The resolver shows "(stage unknown)" beyond 2.3's literal scope. Rejected as a code change:** the resolver's scope is a superset of the literal one, so it does not narrow the text; the reading is recorded under P-3-STAGE-UNKNOWN-SCOPE and in ADR 0042 decision 2.
- **V-9 (low) US-INTAKE-09 AC1 lists eight goal cards; step 6 shows seven. Logged** ("Product doc issues": the "Other" goal is not registered and changes no declared output).
- **V-10 (low) Six test titles did not start with ids. Fixed** (`rule-lines.test.ts`, `projects.test.ts`, `intake.test.ts`).
- **V-11 (info) Step 3 asked the owner to "confirm or correct" every detail, and step 4's subtitle spoke of documents on a project with none. Fixed:** "Please review the details and correct any information if needed."; step 4 serves `no_documents` ("No documents were uploaded, so no system was read from them."; the API half by the integrator). The "Extracted details" title is approved and stays (rejected part). Proofs: `Step3.test.tsx` "V-11 · …", `Step4.test.tsx` "DR-25 · …", `wizard-flow.test.ts` and `wizard-demo.test.ts` "DR-25 · …".
- **V-12 (info) G7-6 compared a count through a zero fallback. Fixed** (the count must be a number, then `before + 1`).
- **V-13 (info) The e2e stack's exemption let it import the whole store. Fixed:** the rule `e2e-setup-reaches-db-only-through-testing`, with its two seeded inputs (added by the integrator; `depcruise.test.ts` passes) and the boundary README row.

**Design review findings** (each step on the 1440 canvas against `design/reference/onboarding/`; G = guardrails or PRD, L = layout, B = brand; the review's severities: DR-1 high; DR-2 to DR-8 and DR-10 medium; DR-9 and DR-11 to DR-27 low).
- **DR-1 (step 8: 21 tall option rows, cards stretched, For you after the outputs, 2,881px and 3,536px). Fixed in part:** the kit's row layout keeps a badge with its figure in a 300px card; decision rows are compact rows named by the option, in the served order; For you and SOVITECH will check come before the outputs; each skipped multi-select says "later" once (G7-12). Measured: the demo's step 8 is 2,724px (Generate at y=2636) and a new project's 3,290px (y=3202). The rest is the design review's (the lists under the grid) and proposal P-3B-SKIPPED-MULTISELECT-ONE-ROW. Single-choice rows keep their label above the value (the steps 5 to 8 group's reading; `Step8.test.tsx` pins it).
- **DR-2 (stored choices shown twice; step 4's card twice the drawn height). Fixed for steps 4 to 7:** `ChoiceCard` has a `status` slot on its top row (under the title on a tile or a pill) and `Value` a compact layout; the stored decision shows there once with its badge, and the "Your choice", "Selected/Not selected" and step 5 echo rows are gone; a building type in conflict shows once under the tiles. On 4-column cards the badge sits under its text inside the slot; on step 5's six-column tiles its words wrap inside the pill so it never passes the tile's border (the integrator). Step 1's part is rejected (R-009: a reopened project shows every stored answer). Proofs: `forms.test.tsx`, `Value.test.tsx`, `ui-css.test.ts`, `Step4.test.tsx`, `Step5.test.tsx`, `Step6.test.tsx` ("DR-2 · …"); `ui-kit.spec.ts` in Chromium.
- **DR-3 (step 3: 8 Edits for 4 values, stacked summary). Fixed:** the summary is a read-only key/value list (Value `row`, no actions); every action sits once in Extracted details (ADR 0039 decision 10). Proof: `Step3.test.tsx` "… DR-3 …".
- **DR-4 (progress named only for screen readers; a 1.33:1 track). Fixed:** `Progress` shows its words beside a 4px bar on an outlined track, keeps its name when the words are hidden, and stands still under reduced motion; step 2 shows "Uploading", and for a file being read the served 2.8 line ("Reading documents…", `FileRow.status.line`). Proofs: `surfaces.test.tsx`, `ui-css.test.ts`, `Step2.test.tsx`, `wizard-rows.test.ts` ("DR-4 · …"); `ui-kit.spec.ts`.
- **DR-5 (the area box names no unit). Fixed:** the box is labelled "Gross floor area, in square metres" (`edit.inUnit`, `units.m2`); a single qualifier is a visible radio. The symbol waits for P-3-INPUT-UNIT-SYMBOL. Proofs: `InlineEditor.test.tsx`, `Step8.test.tsx` ("DR-5 · …").
- **DR-6 (the "Everything ready?" banner on step 8's failure state). Fixed in its state part** (hidden on load failure; `Step8.test.tsx`); the wording change is rejected (PRD Notes; proposal 7.2.15).
- **DR-7 (this log's screenshot comparison). Fixed:** the dropzone corrected to "wider than drawn", D-19's working rule re-marked, and the differences part A did not name added with reasons.
- **DR-8 (all badges look alike). Rejected:** expected while D-19 is open; the badges' words carry the meaning; a filled pill would put a brand value in a new role.
- **DR-9 (widths of steps 1, 2, 5 and 7). Fixed:** step 1 880px, step 2's dropzone 745px, step 5 904px with a 56px icon gutter, step 7 1,070px. Proofs: `NewProjectStep1.test.tsx`, `Step2.test.tsx`, `Step5.test.tsx`, `Step6.test.tsx` ("DR-9 · …").
- **DR-10 (refused files unnamed). Fixed:** the guard's refusal serves `upload:<id>.fileName`, rendered through Value; a page refusal names a short extension in words. Proofs: `wizard-rows.test.ts`, `Step2.test.tsx` ("DR-10 · …"), `fixture-uploads.spec.ts`.
- **DR-11 (tabular figures on file names). Fixed:** tabular figures only on numeric displays. Proofs: `Value.test.tsx`, `ui-css.test.ts` ("DR-11 · …").
- **DR-12 (steps collapsed to "Loading", the footer jumping). Fixed:** each step keeps its frame and one "Loading your answers" line. Proofs: the "DR-12 · …" tests of steps 1 to 7, UD-45, the project list and `PageState.test.tsx`.
- **DR-13 (step 3's reading intro promised facts that cannot fill in). Fixed:** "We are reading your documents." unless a served fact is pending. Proof: `Step3.test.tsx` "DR-13 · …".
- **DR-14 (the skip line's place differed by step). Fixed:** `SkipForNow` owns one placement, 24px under the grid at its left edge. Proofs: `SkipForNow.test.tsx`, `ui-css.test.ts`, `Step4.test.tsx`, `Step5.test.tsx`; `ui-kit.spec.ts` measures 24px.
- **DR-15 (Fire Safety's text started lower-case). Fixed:** "Monitoring only (read-only); fire logic and fire-mode interlocks remain in the fire system." (G11 cases untouched).
- **DR-16 (the coins icon draws a "1"). Fixed:** PiggyBank.

- **DR-18 (five heading styles for one role). Fixed:** two roles, a section heading (20px, 300) and a group or question label (16px, 500), marked "proposed, pending the owner's OK" in `tokens.css` (ADR 0040 decision 6), applied on every step, sign-in and the list.
- **DR-19 (InlineEditor's Save a filled primary). Fixed** (secondary).
- **DR-20 (failure messages with no glyph). Fixed** (CircleAlert beside the text, as FieldError).
- **DR-21 (no completed steps on a step opened directly). Rejected as a fix:** expected while D-10 is open; listed under "Waiting".
- **DR-22 (the project list names only the project). Fixed:** each row shows the served type and city, newest first (no migration). Proofs: `wizard-rows.test.ts`, `sign-in-and-projects.test.tsx` ("DR-22 · …").
- **DR-23 ("Your files 0" beside two uploads). Fixed** (the count shows once a stored document is listed).
- **DR-24 (link buttons with no icon read as text). Fixed** (underlined at a 3px offset).
- **DR-25 (step 4's subtitle on a project with no documents). Fixed for the subtitle** (`no_documents`, the integrator's API half and the web's sentence); the approved banner stays (rejected part: its "optional" is not the removed "Optional" badge).
- **DR-26 (icon sizes). No change needed:** the kit already drew 32px icons on the dropzone and tall cards; now under test.
- **DR-27 (the qualifier in parentheses inside parentheses). Fixed:** "Gross floor area, gross total (Scd)".

**Found while integrating** (the integrator, 2026-10-01): G5-4's first draft held a view object with a `skip` key, which the index check reads as a held-out test (it now feeds the detector the view the API served before the answer); the kit's narrow-tile status overflowed the tile (above, DR-2); the skip's `step`, optional in the fixes, left a page-less skip able to drop step 8's ask (now required). Each is logged in the 1.8 change log.

**The final verification and the fix round after it (2026-10-01).** The final verifier re-ran every check on the integrated tree, one suite at a time (`pnpm check` 8 of 9 steps, the index check's by-design list only; `pnpm e2e` 143 passed; `pnpm test:proposed` 51 passed, 1 todo; `git status` the same before and after), replayed every finding (A-1 and A-7 "partly fixed", the rest as above) and found seven new problems. Two fixers (the API and store; the web) and then the integrator:
- **NP-1 (medium) The owner's writes were not serialised per project. Fixed.** `writeOwnerAnswer`, `planContinue` and `planSkip` read the state and then wrote inside a READ COMMITTED transaction with no lock, so requests that arrived together bypassed the A-1 and A-7 fixes: two edits at once stored two owner candidates ("Two values 1,200 to 1,300 m²", 6 of 6 rounds); two step 5 Continues gave "Hotel or Office" (6 of 6); three goal skips wrote 21 events in place of 7; and in the browser a double click on step 4's "Skip for now", which had no busy guard, wrote the eight systems' skips twice, after which step 8 no longer asked for the systems (rule 7, "At step 8 the review asks for it once, inline"; section 8). Server: `ownerState` takes the project's write lock (`lockProjectWrites`, a transaction-level advisory lock keyed by the project) before it reads, so every owner write (Continue on steps 1 to 8, edit, confirm, acknowledge, concern, resolve conflict, skip) decides on the result of a write sent with it; `ensureBuildingSubject` takes the same lock before the building's own, so the two are always taken in one order (ADR 0036 decision 13). Found in the same family and fixed: the served views' defect logging read the events and then appended, so views served together logged one field twice; it now takes the lock before it reads, only when it has something to log. Web: every owner write sends one request per press and is never disabled (`useInFlight`; ADR 0039 decision 11). Proofs: **G4-38**, **G7-13**, and a new test each in **G5-3** and **G5-4**, all failing with the lock lines removed (the integrator's mutation run, 8 of 8); `tests/api/wizard-owner-writes-concurrent.test.ts` (8 tests, 4 rounds each); the component tests titled "A-1" and "A-7 · G7-10 (web half)" in `NewProjectStep1`, `Step1` to `Step8` and `SkipForNow.test.tsx` (19 fail with the guard's ref check removed); a browser probe double-clicking "Skip for now" on steps 4 and 6 (15 `skipped` events over 15 fields; step 8 still asks for the systems).
- **NP-2 (low) Step 8's inline ask lost its "changed" message when, read again, the ask was gone. Fixed:** after a Save refused `shown_value_changed` whose view no longer serves the ask, step 8 shows the catalogue's existing `edit.changed` sentence once, where the asks are, until the owner leaves the step or the ask is served again (no new wording). Proof: `Step8.test.tsx` "A-1 · rule 4 · rule 7: a Save refused as changed …".
- **NP-3 (low, a reading) The Proposal card named "Preliminary investment estimate" with no figure behind it, on every project whose first-estimate inputs are missing (the demo included). Fixed:** the card names the served stage only while the investment output carrying that stage is served as a range (or `proposal.stage` is non-null), and otherwise shows that output's served "Not available yet: …" line with its Add action; with every gate closed it shows the line of the output named "Preliminary investment estimate", naming the SOVITECH datasets and any missing first-estimate input (rule 7's `first_estimate` row, "or else 'Not available yet' with the missing item and an action to add it"; rule 10's stage 1, "when first-estimate data is missing"). The API needed no change; its doc comments now say what the served stage is for. G10-11's Expected followed PRD US-INTAKE-16 AC2's default rather than the rules as written: its section 7 cell and case file are aligned with rules 7 and 10 (the case is new in this uncommitted version; its first wording is recorded in the 1.8 change log for the approver), and the disagreement is under "Product doc issues". Proofs: **G10-11** (now `.tsx`, with its rendered half, which fails with the card naming the stage); `Step8.test.tsx` "G10-11 (web half) …" (four tests).
- **NP-4 (low) File names were served with bidirectional controls: "TEST plan" U+202E "fdp.xlsx.pdf" showed as "TEST planfdp.xslx.pdf". Fixed:** every file name the API serves goes through `servedFileName`, which drops format characters and bidirectional controls; a name with nothing left reads as no name (the missing wording); the stored name is kept and the upload accepted (no new blocked state). Proofs: **G2-14** (fails with the names served as stored); `tests/api/wizard-file-names.test.ts`; `apps/api/src/documents/file-names.test.ts` (their control characters now written as escapes, by the integrator). Not yet: the phase 2 raw route `documents.list` (not used by the phase 3 web) returns the stored name; phase 4's Documents view builds on `servedFileName` ("Next").
- **NP-5 (low) A skip naming step 8 is accepted on a project whose owner has not reached step 8, and drops the area's ask. Not fixed, explained:** the server stores no visited-step state (ADR 0039 decision 2; R-008 and R-009 "Until decided"; D-10); the late-findings ledger is page-session state; and step 8 opens directly by link, reload or "Add <field>", so a skip that names step 8 while step 8 serves the ask cannot be told from the owner's own "Generate without it". The path is API-only (no page sends step 8 from another step), and the ask returns through "Add <field>" (G7-11). The follow-up waits for D-10 ("Proposals", P-3C-STEP8-SKIP-AFTER-VISIT; "Waiting").
- **NP-6 (low, documentation) This log's severities and tally differed from the reviews. Fixed** (above: each finding with its own review's severity; 54 distinct findings).
- **NP-7 (info) The resolver added rule 4's routing line for any value in the conflict that is not a person's entry, calculated and reference values included, against its own comment. Fixed:** only where a `document` or `ai_inference` value is in the engineer-routed conflict. Proof: `resolver.test.ts`, the test titled `G4-36 · rule 4 "Routing": the line about documents needs …` (an approved TEST reference value against the owner's entry gives Two values with no line about documents; an inference keeps it; it fails on the old condition). Observation, logged under "Next": the owner-routed "Documents say <a>. You entered <b>. Which is right?" still reads any value that is not the owner's as the documents'; only a calculated or reference value on an owner field could make it untrue, and none exists before phase 5.

Result: of the seven, 6 fixed and 1 explained and left for D-10. New cases indexed: G2-14, G4-38, G7-13; G10-11's Expected corrected; G5-3 and G5-4 each gained a test. Found while integrating: the fixers' two test sources held bidirectional control characters themselves (written as `\u` escapes now, so a reader sees them); the rendered half of G10-11 first ended before the page's late-findings poll was answered, and its aborted read printed a socket error (the test now waits for the answer); and the kit's `SkipForNow` change left three generated UI kit harness pages (`lines.html`, `forms.html`, `layout.html`) older than the generator's output, which the first full `pnpm e2e` caught (`ui-kit.spec.ts`, "F-RENDER-06: every kit page on disk is the generator's current output"; the web fixer had run one spec); they are regenerated, and the only difference is `aria-busy="false"` on the skip button.

**Closing the phase (2026-10-02).** Two items left after the fix round, each reproduced first over a TEST database through the API (the scratchpad's `build/p3d/`: `repro-before.log`, then `repro-after.log` on the fixed tree):
- **LOW (rule 7) With the gross floor area the only first-estimate input missing, step 8's Proposal card named neither it nor its action. Fixed.** On a TEST project with the building type and the systems answered and the area skipped, the card read "Not available yet: SOVITECH point templates; SOVITECH cost ranges and benchmarks", with no action: it shows the line of the output that carries the served stage (G10-11), "Preliminary investment estimate", whose formula does not read the area, so "Add gross floor area" showed only on the outputs below. The view-model's `outputAvailability` now has the stage 2 output wait for every first-estimate input, not only those its formula reads (rule 10's table gives stage 1 the figure "when first-estimate data is missing"; rule 7's `first_estimate` row, "or else 'Not available yet' with the missing item and an action to add it"). The card and that output now read "Not available yet: SOVITECH point templates; SOVITECH cost ranges and benchmarks; gross floor area" with "Add gross floor area", the action the outputs carry, which serves the ask through `?add=` after Generate skipped it, as G7-11 does; with the area typed, the line names the datasets only. Existing catalogue keys and rule 7's words only; no API, contract or web code change (doc comments of `outputs.ts`, `views.ts` and `Step8.tsx`, and ADR 0042 decision 9, say so). Proofs: `tests/api/wizard-proposal-card.test.ts`; `intake.test.ts` and `Step8.test.tsx` ("R-003 · … · G7-11 · rule 7 …"); with the stage 2 clause removed, the API and unit tests fail and the rest pass (`mut/mut1-card.log`). G10-11's case file is unchanged and passes. G7-2b's Expected ("Asked inline once, then 'Not available yet' with an Add action") describes this situation; it still has no case file.
- **INFO "Looks right" and "Something's wrong" sent twice appended two events. Fixed.** Sent one after the other (a stale second tab) or at once, each answered 200 twice and appended a second `owner_acknowledged` or `rejected`. `planAcknowledge` and `planConcern` now read the candidates' stored events, which the API passes from the state `ownerState` reads under the project's write lock, and plan nothing for a value this owner already acknowledged, or already rejected: 200 with nothing appended, as a repeated skip. The other action, another value or another owner's is recorded as before, and the refusals still come first (G3-19). It only removes duplicate events: no loosening, and nothing new is asked (ADR 0036 decision 13 amended; the contract's route comments say so). Proofs: `tests/api/wizard-owner-writes.test.ts`, "G3-3 · G3-10 · rule 3 · ADR 0036 decision 13 …" (one after the other; at once, three requests each, 4 rounds) and `intake.test.ts` (the planners); both API tests fail with the repeat check removed (`mut/mut2-repeat.log`), and the concurrent one alone with the lock removed (`mut/mut3-lock.log`); each file was restored from its copy and compared byte for byte.

### Definition of done

1. **The guardrail checks pass:** holds with the D-33 exception (the integrator's `pnpm check` after the fix round that followed the final verification, 2026-10-01, run again on the final tree after the first attempt was cut off; `pnpm e2e` 143 passed with it, "Checks run"): guardrail tests (141 real case files, 484 tests, 0 failed, the run matching the case files), the render test (72: the 45 screens, the route, schema and server tests, the 16 harness canaries and the 8 UI kit pages), registry validation with the sensitivity test (30 of 30 question fields) and the loosening check (no loosening, 18 gates closed, 30 exception lists as recorded), the reserved-term check, and the index check's by-design list: 188 ids at v1.8, 141 real, 20 pending, 27 no automated check yet, **0 other problems**. `pnpm check` is not green while D-33 stands (ADR 0003). *Earlier states, for the record:* part A 126 real case files and 55 render tests; part B 138 real case files, 473 tests, 185 ids.
2. **Evals when `prompts/`, the model id or the output schema change:** not running yet: neither part B nor the fix round after the final verification changed any of them (part A changed only the prompt's version line, to v1.8); no API key is set, so the 20 E cases stay pending.
3. **New behaviour has a case, indexed:** holds for the four part A cases (G7-8, G8-21, G10-10, G11-9), the nine phase 3 cases, and the twelve part B cases (G2-13, G3-19, G4-36, G5-4, G7-9, G7-10, G7-11, G7-12, G8-22, G8-23, G10-11, G10-12), each with its case file and index row; the web halves of G7-3, G7-4 and G7-6 are in their case files, and G5-3 proves the API's own logging. After the final verification: G2-14, G4-38 and G7-13 indexed with their case files; G10-11's case file holds its rendered half; G5-3 and G5-4 each prove one event per field when views are served together; each mutation-checked ("Built"), and the mutation checks repeated on the final tree ("Checks run"). **Does not hold, stated:** G5-3's run through the served step view waits for a seam that hands the API a TEST registry ("Waiting"); the CSRF and session fixes (A-11) and the design-review fixes are proven by API, component, stylesheet and Chromium tests, not by indexed cases (security basics and layout within the rules, no guardrail sentence of their own); the concurrent upload completion (part A) by `tests/api/uploads-concurrent-completion.test.ts`; the web's one request per press and step 8's kept "changed" message by component tests (the server half of the first is G4-38 and G7-13); NP-5 (a skip naming step 8 before the owner reached it) has no check: it is explained and waits for D-10; the e2e flows are not case files (GS-1, phase 7's, has no case file yet).
4. **`docs/guardrails.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and the in-app prompt agree:** holds: v1.8 in all three (version-sync); part B and the fix round after the final verification added rows within 1.8 (and corrected G10-11's Expected, a case new in 1.8) and changed no version line, label or rule number.
5. **Rules touched and cases named:** below, with part B's and the fix round's in their own bullets.

A run through the served view itself needs a TEST registry in the API, which reads the production registry as module constants (`registry.ts`, `plan.ts`, `views.ts`) and whose only owner confirmation is the building type; a case file may not mock it (`tools/checks/index/case-files.ts`). Waits for a seam that takes the registry as a dependency (phase 4, with the API's next registry change); the e2e group's run with `vi.mock` from the scratchpad passed (`build/p3b/fix-harness/g5-3/`) *Closed in phase 4:* the API's registry seam (docs/adr/0044 decision 4) serves step 8 with a TEST registry, and G5-3's served-view test passes (phase 4, "Waiting"). |
| Completed steps in the stepper on a step opened directly (added in part B, DR-21) | US-INTAKE-20; PRD R-008, R-009 "Until decided"; ADR 0039 decisions 2 and 3 | D-10 | none | The done state is page-session (ADR 0039): a step opened directly (a reload, a typed or bookmarked link, a project reopened at step 1 and moved through) shows no completed step before it, only the steps left in this page session. Once D-10 is decided, the done state derives from the served step state (a step whose questions are all answered or skipped), with no new stored record unless D-10 asks for resume |
| An engineer's own unverified site entry, an ambiguous reading on an owner field, "(stage unknown)" on the source line, a stale calculated value | 2.8's first-match order; 2.3; rule 9 | D-05 | none | ADR 0042's readings (a) to (f): "SOVITECH will check"; "Please check"; "(stage unknown)" on existing-building and BMS-modernization projects and superseded revisions only (P-3-STAGE-UNKNOWN-SCOPE); "Out of date, recalculating" in place of the figure |
| The owner's line for an engineer-routed conflict whose values are all people's entries (added in part B, A-1) | rule 4 "Routing"; 2.8 | D-05 | none | Two values, each value's source line and the "SOVITECH will check" listing; no routing line, since "Documents disagree on this" would be untrue (P-3B-CONFLICT-NO-DOCUMENT) |
| Reserved-term forms such as "compliance", "guarantee", "certify", "exactly", and the approved goal card "Ensure compliance" (added in part B, A-5) | 2.8 "Reserved terms"; rule 11; dashboards 7.2.29 | D-05 | none | The list as written (21 English, 14 Romanian terms); the approved card's copy stays until the approver decides and the owner gives a neutral wording |
| Owner text that repeats the app's own words (added in part B, A-9) | 2.8 "Where they are allowed"; rule 10 "Demo data" | D-05 | none | Shown isolated as the owner's text with its badge; never refused (that would be a new blocked state); the demo line comes from the flag only (P-3B-OWNER-TEXT-AS-QUOTATION) |
| One row for a multi-select skipped whole on step 8 (added in part B, V-6, DR-1) | rule 7; 2.8 "Prominence" | D-05 | none | Every option's row with its Not provided yet badge, and "You can provide this later." once per question (G7-12; P-3B-SKIPPED-MULTISELECT-ONE-ROW) |
| Plausible ranges for owner-typed quantities (added in part B, A-3) | rule 8 "Plausibility checks"; approver setting 5 | D-93 | none | A negative area, volume or length is refused (G8-22); zero and very large values are stored as typed, Provided by you, until SOVITECH ranges exist (phase 5) |
| The two type roles and one border role of part B's design fixes (added in part B, DR-18, DR-4) | app-alignment decision 7; ADR 0040 decision 6 | D-19 | none | Used with brand values, marked "proposed, pending the owner's OK" in `tokens.css`: the section heading (20px, weight 300), the group or question label (16px, weight 500), and the control boundary as the progress track's outline |
| A skip that names step 8 before the owner reached step 8 (added after the final verification, NP-5) | rule 7, "At step 8 the review asks for it once, inline" and "Skip means skip"; PRD R-008, R-009 "Until decided"; ADR 0039 decision 2; ADR 0036 decision 13 | D-10 | none | Accepted whenever step 8 serves that ask now (worked out from stored state), since no visited step is stored; no page sends step 8 from another step, and the ask returns through "Add <field>" (G7-11). Once D-10 stores the last visited step, the server can refuse it (P-3C-STEP8-SKIP-AFTER-VISIT) |
| The stage 1 "Indicative range" output while a first-estimate input is missing (added after the final verification, from the web fixer) | rule 7's `first_estimate` row, "falls back to a stage 1 Indicative range where the registry allows one"; rule 10 stage 1; G7-2a | none | `dataset-cost-ranges` | Unreachable while the gate is closed: every investment output reads "Not available yet", naming the dataset and any missing input. The view-model's `outputAvailability` marks `capex.indicativeRange` not available whenever a first-estimate input it reads is missing, while `proposalStage` allows stage 1 there; when the gate opens (phase 5), `outputAvailability` applies the same allowance, with G7-2a as its case |

### Proposals (not applied)

Each as the change, the failure behind it, the effect on both promises and the proving case; none is applied.

- **P-3-EYEBROW-STEP-NUMBER.** Change: in `tests/e2e/render/allowlist.ts`, let the `wizard-step-number` entry also accept one eyebrow element per page, `data-render-allow="wizard-step-number-eyebrow"`, whose text is exactly "STEP <n> OF 8" with <n> the step on screen (a new step_number entry, rule 2's category). Failure: the approved screens show the eyebrow (US-INTAKE-01 AC9), and the render test fails its digits because the step-number entry accepts digits only inside the stepper. Truth: unchanged (a step number counts nothing in the building); Speed: unchanged. Proving case: a harness page with the eyebrow on step 3 passes; "STEP 3 OF 9", "STEP 4 OF 8" on step 3 and an eyebrow outside the page title block fail.
- **P-3-HEADER-TIME.** Change: add a `date_time` entry `time-hours-minutes`, format `HH:mm`, for the header's `<time>`. Failure: US-INTAKE-01 AC1 asks for the current date and time; the allowlist holds a date format only. Truth and Speed: unchanged. Proving case: `<time datetime="2026-09-30T12:36">12:36</time>` passes; "12:36" outside a `<time>` fails.
- **P-3-RENDER-24-7.** Change: add the fixed-copy entry "24 / 7" (rule 2 names it as an example of the reviewed list) for the schedule option label. Failure: the approved step 5 labels the option "24 / 7"; the entry is not on the list, so the build shows "Around the clock". Truth and Speed: unchanged. Proving case: the label passes on step 5; "24 / 7 m²" and "24 rooms" still fail (the schema's quantity rule).
- **P-3-DETECTION-FIELDS.** Change: register eight detection fields `building.system.<id>` (kind `enum`, options `present`, confirmBy owner, criticality optional, `affects` the scope suggestion's template slot), so the AI may propose a system's presence with verified evidence and step 4 can show From document, Likely or Possible and preselect with Suggested. Failure: without them step 4 cannot show §5-4a's badges. Truth: unchanged (verified evidence only, no absence as a value); Speed: up (the owner confirms fewer systems). Needs: the evals of CLAUDE.md's definition of done item 2 (the AI is asked about new fields), so the API key (D-09); a registry addition checked by the loosening check. Proving case: a TEST detection from a document names HVAC: step 4 shows From document and Suggested with its reason; Fire Safety named: no Suggested (G11-9).

- **P-3-APPROXIMATE-AS-WRITTEN** (the view-model builder; a wording clarification, not applied). Change: in rule 8, "Approximate wording is kept", add "A document value shows its approximate word as written ('cca. 2350 mp'); 'about' is added only where no original carries the word (an owner's typed 'about 500', an estimate)". Failure behind it: rule 8 says approximate wording is "shown as 'about'", while rule 9 says "Document values display as written"; for "cca. 2350 mp" they read differently. The build shows the original as written (ADR 0042 reading (d)). Truth: unchanged (the approximate mark shows either way). Speed: unchanged. Proving case: `resolveField` on G8-20's candidate ("cca. 2350 mp", approximate) shows "cca. 2350 mp"; an owner quantity typed "cca. 2350" shows "about 2,350 m²".
- **P-3-STAGE-UNKNOWN-SCOPE** (the view-model builder; a wording clarification, not applied). Change: in 2.3, "When the stage is unknown, the source line says so", add "on an existing building or BMS modernization project, and on a value from a superseded revision". Failure behind it: with no classifier, every stored document's stage is `unknown`, so read literally every document value's source line would carry "(stage unknown)" on every project. Truth: unchanged where the stage matters (2.3's two project types). Speed: less noise on new construction. Proving case: `resolver.test.ts` "G2-6 · US-REVIEW-01 AC4 · 2.3" (existing building: "(stage unknown)"; new construction: none).
  - *The reading in force (part B, V-8).* 2.3's sentence "When the stage is unknown, the source line says so" sits in the bullet "Old-only values stay visible", so its literal scope is a value only an old (superseded) revision had. The resolver shows "(stage unknown)" there, and also on every document value of an existing-building or BMS-modernization project (ADR 0042 decision 2): a superset of the literal scope, so the build does not narrow the text. Read the other way (every document value of every project), the build shows the label on fewer values; that is the reading this proposal asks the approver to settle, and it stays a proposal, not applied.
- **P-3-INPUT-UNIT-SYMBOL** (the steps 5 to 8 builder; a render-allowlist loosening, not applied). Change: add a `fixed_interface_copy` entry for a registered unit symbol shown as the suffix of a quantity input (the InputSpec's `unit.symbol`, such as "m²" beside the step 8 gross floor area box). Failure behind it: rule 7's words are "To show your investment estimate we need the gross floor area. [ m² ] · Generate without it", but the render test reads the superscript two of "m²" as a number outside a value element, so the build shows no unit beside the box and names the basis ("gross total (Scd)") in its select. Truth: unchanged (the symbol comes from the closed unit registry). Speed: unchanged. Proving case: an inline ask with the suffix passes the render check; "m² 12", or a suffix that is not the served spec's symbol, fails.
- **P-3B-RENDER-TYPED-INPUT** (part B, the e2e fixer; a render-test loosening, not applied). Change: in `tests/e2e/render/in-page.ts`, do not report `bare-digit-input` for the value of a text input the owner typed into on this page and has not saved (its value is not served, and no value element shows it). Failure behind it: step 8's inline ask refusing "1.500" (G8-21; US-INTAKE-17 AC7) keeps the owner's own typed digits in the box, and the render test reads them as an unbound number, so that refused state is read by the render test only after the box is cleared (`tests/e2e/flows/inline-ask-refusal.spec.ts`; axe reads it with the typed text). Truth: unchanged for what the app shows (served values still need their display objects; typed text is the owner's own draft, not a value of the building). Speed: unchanged. Proving case: the refused state with "1.500" still in the box passes; the same digits in a served label, a placeholder or a read-only field still fail.

Added in part B (2026-10-01; none applied):
- **P-3B-CONFLICT-NO-DOCUMENT** (A-1; the triage, with the server group's alternative wording). Change: in rule 4, "Routing", after 'The owner sees: "Documents disagree on this. A SOVITECH engineer will check it."', add: 'When no value in the conflict comes from a document or an inference (every value is a person's entry, such as an engineer's site entry and the owner's answer), the owner sees: "Two values for this. A SOVITECH engineer will check it."' (the server group proposed "These entries disagree. A SOVITECH engineer will check it."; the approver chooses the words), and the same line in `packages/registry/src/copy/rule-lines.ts` as `conflict_for_engineer_entries`, which the resolver pushes on an engineer-routed conflict whose candidates are all `user`. Failure behind it: A-1, where two owner answers from stale screens became an engineer-routed conflict on a project with no documents and the owner read "Documents disagree on this". The stale answer is now refused (G4-36), but an engineer-routed conflict of entries alone can still arise (the owner's answer against an engineer's own entry, or an engineer_verified `user` value), and 2.8 and rule 4 have no truthful owner-facing line for it. Meanwhile (a reading applied in part B, reversible in one line of `packages/view-model/src/resolver/index.ts`): the routing line shows only where its words are true, a document or inference value being in the conflict; the Two values badge, each value's source line and the "SOVITECH will check" listing still show, so no value loses its badge or its source. Truth: up (no false claim about documents, and the owner is told who acts). Speed: neutral (nothing asked, nothing blocked). Proving case (G4-37 when approved): an engineer-routed conflict whose candidates are all `user` shows the new line and never "Documents disagree on this"; a conflict with a document value keeps rule 4's line.
- **The extension of dashboards proposal 7.2.29 (reserved-term forms)** (A-5; the triage). Change to 2.8 "Reserved terms", English list: add compliance, comply, complying, conformity, guarantee, guarantees, certify, certification, offers, quoted, exactly, precisely, firm prices (with "conforms" already listed), keeping parity with the Romanian list as 7.2.29 asks. Failure behind it: `findReservedTerms` returned nothing for "Ensure compliance", "Meet local regulations", "We guarantee", "certify", "complying", "conformity", "guarantees", "offers", "Quoted", "exactly", "precisely" and "firm prices"; the approved goal card "Ensure compliance: Meet local regulations and industry standards." and the registry label "Goal: Ensure compliance" (served on step 8) pass the check, although rule 11 says the app never attests compliance. Truth: up. Speed: neutral, but once approved the goal card's title and description need the owner's neutral wording (a copy change for the owner and the design review). Adding terms tightens the list and needs the approver. Proving case: a reserved-term case in which "Ensure compliance" and "we guarantee savings" in UI copy are flagged, while "Confirm" as an action label and "Formal quotation" as a stage 3 status line still pass.
- **P-3B-OWNER-TEXT-AS-QUOTATION** (A-9; a clarification that adds an allowance, so a loosening for the approver). Change to 2.8 "Where they are allowed", after "verbatim document text shown as a quotation, such as an evidence excerpt or original text", add: "text the owner typed (a project name, a city, a file name), shown as the owner's own verbatim text, marked and isolated, and never inside a generated sentence"; and to rule 10's "Demo data": "The demo line is shown only by code from the project's demo flag; owner text that repeats its words is shown as owner text." Failure behind it: the city "Verified Final Offer 12" was created and shown, with "Provided by you", and nothing marks it as the owner's words; a project that is not the demo can be named with the demo line's words. The e2e reserved-term scan would fail such a screen, while the live app has no rule for it, and refusing such names would be a new blocked state, which is not ours to add. Meanwhile owner text renders isolated (`<bdi>`, A-8) with its badge, and the demo line comes from the flag only (G10-10). Truth: up (owner words read as the owner's, never as the app's status). Speed: neutral. Proving case: a TEST project named "Verified Final Offer" shows the name inside the owner-text marker and the scan passes; the same words outside the marker fail; a non-demo project named with the demo line's words carries no `data-demo-line`, and G10-10 still holds.
- **P-3B-SKIPPED-MULTISELECT-ONE-ROW** (V-6 and DR-1; shows fewer badges, so a loosening for the approver). Change to rule 7, after 'The question then shows "You can provide this later." once, inline', add: "On the review step, a multi-select question whose every option was skipped shows as one row: the question's label, Not provided yet, and that line once." Failure behind it: in flow (c) step 8 lists 21 option rows reading "Not provided yet" for three skipped questions (8 systems, 7 goals, 6 automation areas); part B shows the line once per question and compacts the rows (the page is 3,290px, Generate at y=3202), but every row stays. Truth: neutral (every option has the same state, stated once for the question). Speed: up (a shorter review). Proving case: flow (c)'s step 8 shows three rows for the three skipped multi-selects, each with the Not provided yet badge and the line once; a partly answered multi-select still shows each option's row with its own badge.

Added after the final verification (2026-10-01; not applied):
- **P-3C-STEP8-SKIP-AFTER-VISIT** (NP-5; the API fixer; a build change tied to D-10, not a guardrail change). Change: once D-10 is decided so that the last visited step is stored (prompt 3 5.2, "Resume at the last visited step"), `planSkip` refuses a skip that names step 8 on a project whose stored progress has not reached step 8 (422 `answer_invalid`), as it refuses a skip from a step that does not ask the question now. Failure behind it: NP-5, where a skip naming step 8, sent by an API call on a fresh project, was accepted and dropped the area's inline ask; today the server cannot tell it from the owner's own "Generate without it", because no visited step is stored (ADR 0039 decision 2). Truth: up (an ask is never dropped by a request no page sends). Speed: neutral (no owner question, gate, step or confirmation; the owner never sends such a skip). Proving case (a new case once D-10 is decided): on a project whose stored progress stops at step 5, a skip of the gross floor area naming step 8 is refused and step 8 still asks for it; once the owner has reached step 8, "Generate without it" is recorded once.

### Product doc issues

- **Prompt 3 section 9 (OB-3) against PRD R-045:** prompt 3 builds the approved success banner "from stored coverage"; R-045 leaves US-REVIEW-04 AC12 off because 2.8's status lines "are also the only ones used" (traceability 10.2 conflict 1). The guardrails win and prompt 3 section 4 says a criterion the PRD left off is not built: no banner.
- **Prompt 3 5.2 against PRD R-008, R-009 and 10.4 (UD-01):** resume, the clickable stepper, "Continue returns to step 8" and the Overview landing; the PRD's stricter "Until decided" lines are followed (ADR 0039).
- **Prompt 3 section 9 (OB-5 to OB-7) "Suggested with a reason":** R-005 and R-006 keep steps 5 to 7 free of suggestions until D-11 and D-12; followed.
- **US-INTAKE-01 AC1 and AC9** (the time of day, the eyebrow) cannot hold under the render allowlist as it stands; proposals above.
- **US-INTAKE-05 Notes against the approved step 5 order:** rule 6's impactRank order puts the schedule before the occupancy; followed.
- **US-SCOPE-01's data entities** name a detection field per system that the registry does not hold (P-3-DETECTION-FIELDS).
- **The orchestrator's theme rule against PRD R-147's interim and prompt 3 5.2 "Extension tokens":** Extension values are not used (stricter); ADR 0040.

- **The phase 3 split named five builders; B5 (e2e and render) reported nothing.** The integrator built its share (ADR 0037 decision 8): not a product doc issue, recorded so the part B verifier reads the e2e files as the integrator's.
- **Prompt 3 5.2 "Extension tokens" against PRD R-001's Sources and the orchestrator's rule:** the UI builder read both as excluding the "Brand value, new role" rows too (stricter than the plan and ADR 0040's first draft); followed, and ADR 0040 amended.
- **The approved copy of step 7's banner and step 8's "Everything ready?" banner** ("Our AI will tailor the solution …", "… to let our AI create a customized solution") speaks of an AI step that Generate does not run before phase 5, and that no run can take while no key is set. The copy is approved and holds no value, badge or reserved term; it is kept and listed for the design review (part B).
- **Onboarding-spec 3 step 5** draws four occupancy options with "Seasonal"; the registry (phase 1) keeps "Seasonal" on the schedule only (onboarding-spec 6.1's proposal); the screen follows the registry.

Added in part B (2026-10-01):
- **PRD R-046's interim (D-61) against guardrails 2.3** (V-4). R-046 says "an engineer field whose only source was removed goes under 'SOVITECH will check' and is not counted in the owner's 'For you' list"; 2.3, "Deleting a document", says a field left with no eligible candidate "appears under 'For you' as 'Source document removed'", for any field. The guardrails win (prompt 3 section 3): the code sends every source-removed field to For you (derive's `reviewItem` in `packages/domain/src/field-state.ts`, `openItems` in `packages/view-model/src/intake/open-items.ts`, G4-15), and the R-046 interim line the Plan cites is marked "not applied". The doc comment of `openItems` still names the R-046 interim (stale wording, no behaviour; for the view-model's owner). Whether engineer fields should instead go to SOVITECH is D-61 / new Q13, the owner's.
- **US-INTAKE-09 AC1 lists eight goal cards** (V-9); step 6 shows seven. The "Other" goal is not offered: it is not a registered field and changes no declared output, so rule 6's sensitivity test could not pass for it (US-INTAKE-09 AC4, G6-1; onboarding Q11, PRD R-007, whose "Until decided" line keeps the note fields off). The registry's own test holds it (`packages/registry/src/production/production.test.ts`, "no 'Other' goal"). Registering it would be a new owner question, not ours to add.

Added after the final verification (2026-10-01):
- **PRD US-INTAKE-16 AC2 against rules 7 and 10** (NP-3). AC2 says the Proposal card "names the stage its investment figure will carry, derived from stored state: 'Preliminary investment estimate' as a range, or 'Indicative range' where a first-estimate input is missing, the registry allows one and an approved dataset version for it exists", so it names "Preliminary investment estimate" by default, also while first-estimate data is missing and no Indicative range can be produced. Rule 7's `first_estimate` row says that then "the output falls back to a stage 1 Indicative range where the registry allows one, or else 'Not available yet' with the missing item and an action to add it"; rule 10 gives stage 1 "when first-estimate data is missing"; 2.8's stage labels "are also the only ones used". The guardrails win (prompt 3 section 3): the card names a stage only while a figure at that stage can be produced, else the "Not available yet" line of the output that carries the stage, with its action. Section 5, step 8's "a Preliminary investment estimate as a range" applies once a figure can be produced. G10-11's first Expected followed AC2 and is corrected ("Guardrails 1.8" above).

### Questions for the owner

Only those that change the build; the earlier phases' questions stand (the API key first).

1. **The render allowlist (D-05, D-54):** may the approver, once named, add the eyebrow "STEP <n> OF 8", the header's time and "24 / 7" (P-3-EYEBROW-STEP-NUMBER, P-3-HEADER-TIME, P-3-RENDER-24-7)? Until then the screens differ from the mockups there.
2. **The theme's Extension colours (D-19):** do you accept the proposed input border, status colours and badge colours of "App theme", or keep the app to brand values only?
3. **Step 4 detections (D-09):** with the API key set, may the build register the detection fields and run their evals, so step 4 can show what the documents name?
4. **A development account's writes on the demo project (D-13):** the development owner is a member of the demo (PRD R-136 interim) and may write answers there; refusing that would be a new blocked state. Should the demo refuse owner writes from anyone but its seed, or stay writable in development builds?
5. **Conflict choices on step 3 (new Q17, D-14):** a conflict put to the owner is offered on step 3 and on step 8. Keep both, or step 8 only?
6. **Step 8's "Generate without it" (US-INTAKE-17; R-003):** it skips the unanswered asks and generates at once, as "Generate Proposal" does. Should it instead skip only that ask and stay on step 8?

Added in part B (2026-10-01):

A SOVITECH engineer will check it." or "These entries disagree. A SOVITECH engineer will check it." (P-3B-CONFLICT-NO-DOCUMENT)? - **Carried from the fix round after the final verification:** phase 4's Documents page builds its display-object view of `documents.list` on `servedFileName` (the raw phase 2 route still returns the stored name; NP-4); the kit's Value action buttons gain `aria-busy` while a write is on its way, and step 3's per-row guard covers the whole screen (the caller's guard already ignores a second press; phase 4, when the kit is next changed); P-3C-STEP8-SKIP-AFTER-VISIT once D-10 is decided; in phase 5, before calculated or reference candidates exist, the owner-routed "Documents say <a>. You entered <b>. Which is right?" names only document and inference values as the documents' (NP-7's observation), and `outputAvailability` applies `proposalStage`'s stage 1 allowance once `dataset-cost-ranges` opens (G7-2a; "Waiting").

## Phase 4: workspace, building model and scope (2026-10-02)

### Plan

**Goal** (prompt 3 section 10, "Phase 4"): after the intake, the owner explores the building and the scope, driven by the register and, where uploaded, the IFC model, within what v1.5 and the closed gates allow.

**Start of the phase.** Guardrails v1.8 (phase 3's version; the section 10 approver table empty; no change-log row records an approval); all 18 gates closed; no D row decided. Phase 3 committed (`2ae9669`). The owner's answers in force ("Owner answers during the run", their words): the model id `claude-opus-5-5`; uploads accept fixture hashes only (ADR 0028); IFC read with web-ifc, and until D-01 the live app stores IFC models only, with no reader job; the demo keeps the synthetic `test` profile; G10-11 kept as corrected to rules 7 and 10; and, on 2026-10-02, for the 3D viewer, **"Trial now, decide later (Recommended)"**: this phase runs the viewer spike (That Open on web-ifc) on the synthetic fixture models and the `perf` model, in the no-network sandbox, and records time and memory in its ADR (0046); no viewer is wired into the live app; every page that would draw a model area renders without it, never with an illustrative, generic or mockup model (R-080; US-MODEL-05 AC2); the owner decides D-03 from the measurements in the phase 4 report. No public sample model is downloaded (prompt 3 section 12: ask first; not asked). `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is still not set: no value comes from any document, so every register but Documents is empty in the live app and says why, and every value the owner did not type reads Unknown, Not provided yet or "Not available yet" with what is missing. The brand: the SOVITECH brand tool in its dark variant (Brand values only, ADR 0040); the mockups stay the brief for layout, flows and content. The demo is "Demo Hotel Bucharest", labelled demo on every screen of it (in the workspace, by the 48px status footer) and on no other project.

**How the phase runs.** Part A: this planner (this plan, the dependencies, the contract, the skeleton, the copy keys, ADRs 0043 to 0046), then seven builders in parallel against what is written here, then the integrator. Part B: the verifier, the read-only adversarial review and the design review, then fixes (prompt 3 section 12).

**What the owner's viewer answer means, screen by screen.** No live page draws a model: DB-01 (3D View) is not built (R-078, R-079, R-085 "Until decided"; its content, levels, systems and equipment by floor, is reached through the floor filters of the register pages, R-056, R-077); System Scope has no canvas and no 3D / 2D / Section control (R-054, R-074); Topology offers its Logical view only, with no 3D or 2D mode (R-073, R-075); Equipment and Zones have no plan strip, Floor Plan mode or pin (R-064, R-070, R-084); step 3 keeps phase 3's view area (UD-46: "Not available yet: an IFC model of the building", or the stored model's G12-1 line). The spike's numbers go to the owner for D-03; D-01 and D-04 stay open.
