#### Cases

Each written before the code it tests; titles start with the ids they prove.

| Case | What proves it | Builder |
|---|---|---|
| G2-7 (extend) | The same value id with the same filter renders identically on two workspace pages and on the wizard: the area and the project type on the project card and on step 3 and step 8; a scope decision on System Scope, Topology's group, step 4 and step 8; a system's equipment line on System Scope and Topology (one display object, one projection) | B2 (view-model), B7 (e2e half) |
| G9-6 (new file) | A TEST document's "424 spaces" including technical rooms, ingested through the verifier: stored with qualifier `all_spaces`, shown as all spaces on the project card, and no room-controller count, asset or point figure anywhere is derived from it | B2 with B3 |
| G2-14 (extend; Expected unchanged) | Documents' rows and inspector, a revision's "Revision of" and an asset's evidence show file names without bidirectional or format controls | B3 |
| G4-38 (extend; Expected unchanged) | Two System Scope decisions for one system sent at once from screens that showed no value: one stored, Provided by you, no conflict | B3 |
| G5-3 (extend; Expected unchanged) | The twelve TEST values through the served step view, over the registry seam (the build log's "Waiting" row) | B3 |

Candidate new cases, each Expected following from the rules as written (section 10 allows new cases without approval); the builders write each case file at `tests/guardrails/<ID>.test.ts` (or `.tsx`), and the integrator indexes them in section 7 with the guardrails 1.9 change-log row:
- **G1-26** (T): a document stored with no classification of its kind (the upload's stored default): its category reads Unknown (rule 1, "never filled with ... a guess"; 2.3; 2.8 Unknown). B2, B3.
- **G1-27** (T): Topology's Logical view of a project whose documents name no controller, network, bus or protocol: none is shown (rule 1, "Interfaces": "A protocol is a `document` value only when a document names it"; "Model knowledge is not a source"; 2.1). B2.
- **G3-20** (T): a visible Suggested system on System Scope, left in place, then "Save and Continue": a new `user` candidate with `user_confirmed` and `accepted_suggestion` events; Provided by you; not provisional (rule 3, "A suggestion left in place counts as the owner's answer"). B3 (with a TEST suggestion rule, as G3-4).
- **G4-39** (T): Delete is opened on a document that is the only source of three fields and one of two sources of a fourth: the confirmation states that 3 values will return to Unknown (2.3, "Deleting a document"). B3 (API), B4 (page half).
- **G4-40** (T): the owner changes a system's scope on System Scope: a new decision candidate holds it, and the earlier candidate is kept, unchanged, in the history (rule 4, "A new value is always added, never swapped in"; 2.4). B3.
- **G7-14** (T): no floor structure is known: the floors list and the floor filter read "Not available yet", naming the floor structure, with the actions to upload a document and to enter the floors (rule 7, "'Not available yet' never appears alone"; rule 8 "Floors"). B2.
- **G7-15** (T): Topology with no include decision recorded: "Not available yet", naming the systems in scope, with the action to choose them; no group is drawn (rule 7). B2.
- **G11-10** (T): a document names Fire Safety, and System Scope shows the systems the documents name with Suggested: Fire Safety is not preselected and shows no Suggested (rule 11; section 5, step 4, "except life-safety systems"). B2, B3.
- **G11-11** (T): Topology with Fire Safety and HVAC included: Fire Safety is its own group, with "monitoring only (read-only); fire logic and fire-mode interlocks remain in the fire system" and a link in the monitoring direction only, never a control command (rule 11, "Read-only by default", "Fire mode is hardwired and wins"). B2 (view), B5 (rendered half).
- **G12-10** (T): the Equipment and Zones registers and Topology's groups of a project whose documents no completed AI run searched: no "Not found in the analysed documents" statement appears (rule 12, "Absence of evidence is not evidence of absence"; G12-8). B2.
- **G13-9** (T): a user who is a member of projects A and B opens the project switcher: it lists A and B, under the names entered on step 1, and no other project (rule 13, "Project boundary"; rule 1). B3 (API), B1 (rendered half).

Proposed, not indexed (`tests/proposed/`, the non-blocking job): **IFC-12** (ifc-input 5.4): an inline SVG plan with an area printed into it fails the render test, and the same plan with no text passes; if the spike produces a plan section of the ARH fixture, it holds no text element. Waits for D-03, D-04, D-01 (a converted plan), as PRD R-025 says. B7.

Cases already real that the new routes must keep holding (no new file): G1-8, G2-1, G2-8, G3-3, G3-10, G3-19, G4-5, G4-15, G4-17, G4-19, G4-28, G4-31, G10-10, G10-12, G12-1, G12-5, G12-6, G13-3, G13-4, G13-5. GS-1 stays phase 7's. The 20 E cases stay pending (no key).

#### ADRs

Written by the planner (2026-10-02), amended by the builders where they decide more: **0043** workspace navigation and shell (the PRD's D-02 "Until decided" lines over 5.2's 2.5 default; the sidebar, the switcher, the landing, the footer, the floor selection); **0044** the workspace API contract and the API's registry seam; **0045** the registers under v1.5 and the closed gates (no new production field; counts stay the engine's; the level register; document categories; the delete effect; no preview); **0046** the viewer spike (Proposed; the dependencies; measurements to be filled by the spike builder). ADR 0041 (performance budgets) gets phase 4's measurements (viewer, registers, web).

#### The contract (for the builders)

- **Module:** `packages/view-model/src/browser/contract/workspace.ts` (exported by `@sovitech/view-model/browser`), ten routes added to `ROUTES` with `phase: 4`; the planner's contract test (`contract.test.ts`, "ADR 0044 · F-RENDER-06") passes. B3 stewards additive changes during part A, each named in its report.
- **Routes** (every one session; reads no CSRF; every one serves display objects; each answers the envelope `asOf`, `project` (with the demo line from the flag only), `displayObjects`, plus its view):
  - `GET /api/projects/:projectId/workspace` (`workspace.frame`): `pages` (the built pages in sidebar order), `projectCard`, `footer.stillReading`, `levels` (the level register: known, unknown with its actions, or conflict).
  - `GET …/workspace/documents` (`workspace.documents`): `state`, `rows` (file name, format, category key and its Unknown display, revision, stage, `addedAt`, status, `revisionOf`, `downloadable`), `stillReading`.
  - `GET …/workspace/documents/:documentId/delete-effect` (`workspace.documents.deleteEffect`): the bound "<n> values will return to Unknown".
  - `GET …/workspace/system-scope?level=` (`workspace.systemScope`) and `POST …/workspace/system-scope/decisions` (`workspace.systemScope.decide`, CSRF; `{ decisions: [{ field, choice, corrects }], visibleSuggestions }`; refusals 409 `shown_value_changed`, 422 `answer_invalid`, 403 `owner_only`; answers the refreshed view).
  - `GET …/workspace/equipment?system=&level=&zone=&badge=&search=&page=` (`workspace.equipment`, 50 rows a page, previous and next) and `GET …/workspace/equipment/:assetId` (`workspace.asset`, UD-08).
  - `POST …/fields/concern-many` (`fields.concernMany`, CSRF; `{ candidateIds }`; as `fields.concern` for each, under one lock).
  - `GET …/workspace/zones?level=&system=&search=` (`workspace.zones`).
  - `GET …/workspace/topology?level=` (`workspace.topology`).
  - Reused: `projects.list` (the switcher), `uploads.*` (the upload surface), `documents.revisionOf`, `documents.file`, `documents.delete`, `fields.edit` (UD-09), `fields.acknowledge` (a selection's "Looks right"). The raw `documents.list` stays for tests and the seed; the web never calls it.
- **Value ids** added: ADR 0044 decision 3.
- **The registry seam:** ADR 0044 decision 4 (B3).
- **Web routes** (`APP_PATHS`): `/projects/:projectId/system-scope`, `/topology`, `/zones`, `/equipment`, `/equipment/:assetId`, `/documents`, in the workspace frame; `/projects/:projectId/proposal` moves into the frame as the landing (B1). The render spec fails for each new path until B7 adds its screens (part A's integrator closes it).

#### The skeleton (written by the planner; each builder replaces its part)

- `packages/viewer-spike/` (package.json, tsconfig.json, `src/index.ts`): the spike's package, imported by nothing.
- `packages/view-model/src/browser/contract/workspace.ts`, `routes.ts` (ten routes), `index.ts`, `contract.test.ts` (one test added).
- `packages/view-model/src/workspace/index.ts` (the builders' list, exports nothing yet), exported by `src/server.ts`.
- `apps/api/src/workspace/routes.ts` (every route registered, reading and answering with the contract's schemas) and `service.ts` (stubs that refuse with `WorkspaceNotBuilt`, answered 500 until written); `apps/api/src/routes.ts` registers them; `route-table.test.ts` passes.
- `apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspaceLayout.tsx` (renders the page only; B1 builds the frame), `apps/web/src/workspace/pages/{documents,system-scope,topology,zones,equipment,asset}/<Page>.tsx` (titled placeholders), `apps/web/src/routes.tsx` (the routes, their titles, `APP_PATHS`).
- `packages/ui/src/components/workspace.tsx` (exported by the kit): working, accessible, unstyled `Switch`, `Tabs`, `MenuButton`, `InlinePanel`, `Pager`, `ChipGroup`, `Inspector`, with stable props; none is a dialog, none has a disabled state.
- `apps/web/src/copy/en.json`: the `workspace.*` namespace and six `titles.*` keys (below).
- Planner's checks: `pnpm run typecheck` (every package and the root) passes; ESLint on every file above passes; `pnpm vitest run` of `contract.test.ts` (8 passed) and `apps/api/src/route-table.test.ts` (2 passed); `pnpm checks` as above.

#### How the work is split (part A builders; each owns its files, and no two write the same file)

The planner's files (this plan, ADRs 0043 to 0046, the contract, the skeleton) pass to the builders as written; the integrator reconciles the builders' contract additions, the catalogue and `routes.tsx`, indexes the candidate cases (guardrails 1.9, one MINOR bump for the phase), and runs the full checks under the lock.

**Resources** (the run's working rules): builders run only the tests of the files they change (`pnpm vitest run --maxWorkers=2 <paths>`, the components project for `.tsx`), their package's typecheck, ESLint on their files and `pnpm checks`; at most one e2e spec file, under the lock (`scratchpad/build/p3/e2e.lock`), with one worker; never the full `pnpm check`, `pnpm test` or `pnpm e2e` (the integrator's); every server, stack or container they start is stopped; throwaway files under `scratchpad/build/p4/<area>/`.

#### Where copy lives, and what changed from the approved copy

- **Server side** (served in display objects): 2.8's badges, status lines and stage labels, the demo line, the rule lines (phase 3), and the missing names of "Not available yet: {missing}" (`SOVITECH asset taxonomy`, `SOVITECH point templates`, `floor structure`, `the systems in scope`, `SOVITECH's design of the controllers, networks and integrations`), each in the view-model; the delete-effect sentence ("<n> values will return to Unknown", 7.1.1-D4 and US-DOCS-21 AC1's words) in the view-model's server copy with a test that its words occur in design/dashboards-spec.md.
- **The UI catalogue** (`apps/web/src/copy/en.json`): the new `workspace` namespace (frame, pages, levels, actions, back links, pagination, documents, systemScope, equipment, asset, zones, topology) and `titles.systemScope|topology|zones|equipment|asset|documents`; fixed copy only, no digit, no reserved term (the reserved-term check reads it). The system names and descriptions stay `systems.*` (Fire Safety's monitoring-only text, §5-4b).
- **Approved copy changed** (for the design review; each with its reason): System Scope's subtitle drops "based on the topology" (no inventory drives the scope in this build); its "From Topology" column reads "Equipment" (7.1.1-C1: a register query) and "Coverage" is gone (7.1.1-C4); "Edit Scope" is not drawn (it repeats the row's switch; no other target); Equipment's subtitle reads "Explore and filter the equipment found in your documents." (R-066: no copy says the list holds all the building's equipment; proposal 7.2.31); Zones' subtitle reads "View and correct the zones found in your documents." (no "+ Add Zone", R-062; control strategies are SOVITECH's design, 7.2.10); Topology's title and subtitle read "Topology" and "The systems in scope, and the equipment your documents show for each." (the approved "Building automation logical view" and "functional architecture ... data flow" describe SOVITECH's design, 7.2.10, R-071); Documents loses Uploaded By and Size and gains Stage and Analysis (R-017, R-018; 7.1.1-D1), its Version shows the revision as written (7.1.1-D2), its chips show no count and its pager no number (R-017), and its inspector no preview, size, uploader or description (P-4-DOCUMENT-PREVIEW, R-017, R-018, R-019). Draft copy for undesigned states (the empty states, the menus, the inline panels, the asset page, the zone editor) is marked draft for the design review.

#### Phase 3's carried items

- **The registry seam** (G5-3 through the served view): B3, ADR 0044 decision 4; G5-3's served-view test added (its Expected unchanged); the "Waiting" row closes.
- **Documents' display-object view on `servedFileName`** (NP-4): `workspace.documents` (B3); the raw `documents.list` keeps the stored name and is not read by the web.
- **The kit Value action buttons' `aria-busy`**, and **step 3's screen-wide guard**: B1.
- Left for later, as phase 3 said: P-3C-STEP8-SKIP-AFTER-VISIT (D-10); A-3's plausibility half and A-14 (phase 5); GS-1's case file (phase 7); the owner's `demo` profile; the step 8 length and step 5 tile echo (question 9).

#### Risks

- **No API key:** every register but Documents is empty in the live app; the pages are proven with TEST registries and TEST data (unit, component, API and e2e TEST states), and the live screens show their empty states. The demo shows its documents, answers and decisions only.
- **Shared files under parallel builders:** `en.json` (B1 stewards; page builders add only their own namespace, re-reading before each edit), `routes.tsx` and `ProjectLayout.tsx` (B1 only), the contract (B3 stewards, additive only). The integrator reconciles.
- **The proposal page moves into the workspace frame:** phase 3's `ProposalPage.test.tsx`, flows (a) to (c) and render screens must stay green (B1 and B7).
- **The registry seam touches phase 3's wizard modules** (B3): behaviour must not change; phase 3's API tests for the wizard are run for the files touched.
- **Memory** (16 GB machine, 8 GB Colima VM): the spike's perf-model conversion (4g container) and Chromium bench run alone, under the e2e lock; the 5,000-row measurement runs over a TEST database with one worker.
- **That Open's defaults reach the network** (the importer's web-ifc WebAssembly path and the Fragments worker URL point at CDNs): set local paths; the sandbox (`--network none`) and the bench's request filter prove it.
- **Headless Chromium has no GPU** (SwiftShader): orbit frame rates are not "integrated graphics" numbers; ADR 0046 says which backend measured them.
- **The delete confirmation** is required by 7.1.1-D4 and R-016, while the PRD flags for the approver whether a destructive-action confirmation counts as a section 10 "confirmation step" (traceability 10.2 item 14): built inline, never a dialog, and listed.
- **Level labels from owner-entered floors** use the regim's notation (ADR 0045 decision 3; D-18).
- **No zone or asset attribute exists live**, so the zone editor and the Equipment filters are reachable only in TEST states; their e2e screens use the control route.
- **R-060's "every zone count, including step 3's Zones row, is a Calculated count over this register"** against phase 1's `building.zones` count field (a fact a document may state): see "Product doc issues"; nothing changes on step 3 in this phase.

#### Known differences from the screenshots, planned (the design review compares each page and adds what it finds)

The logo and no tagline; no "BMS LIVE" chip or "BMS Live · Last sync" footer (the footer carries the demo line and "Still reading"); no top tabs on 16 and 17 (R-146); the sidebar lists the built pages only (Proposal, System Scope, Topology, Zones, Equipment, Documents); no photo on the project card and no Status or BMS Platform line; brand colours (mint the single accent, white primary button, 1px and 2px radii, no glow); no system colour palette (an Extension row, D-19): systems are told apart by name and icon; Documents as above (copy); System Scope with no canvas, no 3D / 2D / Section, no Coverage column, no "Edit Scope", "Equipment" in place of "From Topology", a detail panel beside the list in the canvas's place; Equipment with no plan strip, floor stack, Floor Plan mode, Status column, Export, product photo or commissioning date; Zones with no plan, compass, scale bar, Status column, photo, "+ Add Zone" or count; Topology as a Logical view of the registers only (no SAUTER boxes, Vision Center, buses, protocols, data flow, key metrics or integration status); every value Unknown, Not provided yet or "Not available yet" with what is missing where no source exists (D: no key, no dataset).

### Built

Part A (2026-10-02): the planner, then six builders against the plan (the kit; the web frame with Documents; the view-model with the API and store; System Scope with Equipment and the asset record; Topology with Zones; the viewer spike), then the integrator, who also did B7's share (the render screens, flows (d) to (f) and IFC-12). Nothing here is committed. With no API key, every register but Documents is empty in the live app and says why; the pages are proven with TEST registries and TEST data in unit, component and API tests, and in the browser with the TEST states of the e2e stack's control route.

The history lists candidates by role and date; candidate events (acknowledged, rejected, verified) are not listed, since 2.8 writes no history wording for them (for the design review). `AssetPage.test.tsx` (6); flow (e); 2 render screens. **Part B:** each history entry with its own badge, source line and 2.8 status line ("Source document removed", "From a superseded revision"), levels and zones named; a rejected entry, with no 2.8 wording, is left out (V-3, A-8; G3-21; P-4B-HISTORY-ENTRY-WORDING); the coverage line at the small size (DR-10) |

**The pieces under them.**
- **The kit** (`packages/ui`): the planner's controls (Switch, Tabs, MenuButton, InlinePanel, Pager, ChipGroup, Inspector), styled within the brand and extended only; new SideNav, WorkspaceFrame, InspectorLayout, PageHeader, StatusFooter (takes only the served demo line, "Still reading" and 2.8 status lines, R-139), RegisterTable (native table, caption, headers, badge column, selection, `aria-sort`, arrow keys between rows, a named scroll region), SelectionList, ActiveFilters, ValueName (a bound name inside a button, link or heading; refuses numeric displays) and ModelArea (no model drawn; used by no page yet); shell sizes as tokens (sidebar 208px, inspector 360px, footer 48px); no dialog and no disabled state anywhere; the carried `aria-busy` on Value's action buttons. 120 kit component tests, `ui-css.test.ts` 19, three new harness pages (`ui-kit.spec.ts` 27). ADR 0040 decision 7.
- **The web frame** (`apps/web/src/workspace/`): the frame around every workspace route (ProjectLayout), the proposal page moved into it as the landing, the frame read once per project and every 5 s while "Still reading" is served, `useWorkspaceView`, `useWorkspaceFrame`, `useLevelSelection` (the shared `level` parameter), `workspaceActionPath`; render-ready counts its callers; step 3's screen-wide write guard (carried).
- **The view-model** (`packages/view-model/src/workspace/`): pure builders over the project the API reads, generic over the registry: the frame, Documents and the delete effect, System Scope and its decision planning, Equipment and the asset, Zones, Topology, the register states (never "not found" without a completed AI search, G12-10). Unit tests in `workspace.test.ts`.
- **The API** (`apps/api/src/workspace/`): all ten routes of the contract, with no contract change; owner writes under `requireOwner`, then `lockProjectWrites`, then the read; the phase 2 `documents.delete` and `documents.revisionOf` now take the write lock; a read-only `readDocumentRegistrations` (no migration). The registry seam (ADR 0044 decision 4): `ApiServices.registry`, production by default and set explicitly in `src/index.ts`; TEST registries only in tests (`apps/api/src/wizard/registry.test.ts`). Integration tests: `tests/api/workspace-routes.test.ts`, `workspace-test-registry.test.ts`, `workspace-equipment-scale.test.ts`.
- **The viewer spike** (`packages/viewer-spike`, `services/viewer-spike`, `tools/viewer-spike`): see "The viewer spike" below. Imported by nothing in the app: the dependency rule `viewer-spike-imported-by-nothing` with five seeds (apps/web, apps/api, packages/viewer and tests/e2e refused; tests/proposed allowed), its README row, and three `.dockerignore` lines for the image's build context. No root `package.json` dependency is added: IFC-12 imports the package by path, which the rule allows.
- **The integrator's share:**
  - 34 render screens for the six new routes and their states (`tests/e2e/render/screens.ts`, now 79): the demo's System Scope (decisions and the first panel; the floor menu open; Fire Safety's panel, Equipment tab; the switcher open), Topology (groups; the system filter open; load failure), Zones, Equipment (Filters row open), Documents (the register, an inspector, a row menu, the filter panel, the delete confirmation, the revision panel, loading); a new project's System Scope (undecided; load failure), Topology (no decision; HVAC and Fire Safety with the monitoring lane), Zones (empty; loading), Equipment (empty; loading; three TEST tags; the inspector's Overview and Documents tabs), the asset record (found; not in the register), Documents (empty; the upload panel; a file being read; the delete confirmation stating "1 value will return to Unknown" for a TEST inference), and the frame failing. Each runs the render test (`render.spec.ts`) and, in `screen-states.spec.ts`, axe, the demo line rule and the no-dialog check.
  - A TEST state `assets-listed` in the stack's control route (`tests/e2e/setup/control.ts`): one TEST equipment list writing three tags and one untagged fan coil (asset subjects, identities, appearances and their evidence, through the store's TEST machinery only).
  - Flows (d) `workspace-demo.spec.ts`, (e) `workspace-new-project.spec.ts`, (f) `workspace-keyboard.spec.ts`, with `tests/e2e/support/workspace.ts` (page actions only) and `readGuardrailEvents` in `support/control.ts`.
  - `?system=` on System Scope, with its component test written first (it failed before the change): Topology's "View in System Scope" now opens that system's panel (the Topology builder's link, the System Scope builder's file).
  - The project card's dead Tailwind arbitrary variants removed (an underscore in `[&_.sov-value__line]` compiles to a space, so they matched nothing; the kit's sidebar rule does the wrapping).
  - The render test's network tracker drops a page's open requests when its main frame starts a new document (a request cancelled by a navigation had kept every later check of that page waiting out its settle budget: 31 s a check, now about 4 s; "Guardrail review").
  - `tests/proposed/IFC-12.test.ts` (ifc-input 5.4, the planner's B7 item), replacing the stale `it.todo` in `tests/proposed/ifc-input-5.4-api.test.ts`.
  - Guardrails 1.9 (eleven index rows, two change-log rows), the version lines of `CLAUDE.md` and `prompts/sovitech-ai-system.md`, the index pin (199: 179 T, 20 E), two stale file references in the G1-26 and G11-10 case comments, ADR 0041's phase 4 web and route measurements.

**Not built** (each with its reason, from the plan): Property (UD-02; R-050, PRD 10.2), Overview (UD-01; phase 5 and D-02), tabs and a grouped sidebar (R-146), DB-01 and every model view (R-078; the owner's answer), DB-07 and DB-10 (R-073), the 2D plan component UD-04 (R-084, Later; R-080), UD-31 (R-054, R-074), UD-44 (R-023, R-024), UD-23 and UD-24 (links with no target on DB-13 and DB-22, phase 6's pages: removed, for the owner, dashboards 8.10), Export (phase 5), an Analysis filter on Documents (its options would carry coverage digits or invent status wording), Equipment's type filter (`dataset-asset-taxonomy`; "Waiting"). System Scope's "All Systems" filter, left out in part A, is built in part B (V-10; G3-22).

**For the design review (part B)**, from the builders' reports and the screenshots below: the badge column's reading (one value column's badge in its own cell, bound to the same value id, so "Unknown" shows as text and as the badge on the same row); badges wrapping under their figure in table cells and in the 208px sidebar; the square switch; the register's scroll region as a tab stop; undecided System Scope rows with "Include" and "Leave out" in place of a switch; the three-tab zone details (Control Points folded into Equipment); the area cell's measure label above the figure; the filter disclosures styled as secondary buttons; Topology's dashed design level and Fire Safety's right-hand lane; no system colours or photos; the history's partial build (candidates only); the empty 48px footer band on a project that is neither the demo nor reading anything; the skip link's target (`#main` holds the sidebar too, so it does not pass the sidebar; WCAG 2.4.1); the StatusFooter inside `main` (the kit suggests it outside, as the contentinfo landmark); the draft copy for undesigned states (Documents: `filterFormat`, `allStages`, `allFormats`, `activeFilters`, `removeFilter`, `openDetails` "Show details", `uploadsHeading` "Uploads", `revision.chooseFirst`, `replace.cancel`, `replace.firstOnly`, `delete.effectFailed`, the empty sentences and panel copy; System Scope: "Include", "Leave out", the empty-tab sentences; Equipment: "Equipment by type", "Your answer is saved.", "Clear the selection"; the asset record's subtitle and section copy; Zones and Topology: the keys listed in their builders' reports), and the approved copy changed (the plan's "Where copy lives").

**Part B (2026-10-02)**: the verifier, the read-only adversarial review and the design review on part A's tree, a triage, five fix groups, then the integrator ("Guardrail review" lists every finding and its outcome). What changed, by piece; nothing here is committed either:
- **The contract** (`workspace.ts`; ADR 0044's part B amendment): the level register's conflict branch is `{ state: 'conflict', line, field, actions }` (the floors field's own display beside the line; `enter_floors` only where the owner resolves the conflict); `ZoneDetail.systemDecisions` is `[{ decision, systemId, lifeSafety }]`; `ZoneDetail.fields` gains `description` as its sixth entry.
- **The view-model and the API** (ADR 0045's part B amendment): levels and zones named by the register's label and the zone's name everywhere a value is served, the wizard's write answers included (`referenceDisplays`); the asset history resolved per entry (`resolveCandidateEntry`); the delete effect derived with the domain's derive (fields and the asset register, the document treated as removed); `revisionOf` from the derive's effective predecessor (`documentStatuses().predecessor`, additive); tags served without controls (`servedTag`); zones listed only with a live value; `no_documents` only for a project that never had one; uploads registered under the project's write lock, and Delete's file removal in a second locked request; `projects.list` read four projects at a time.
- **The kit** (ADR 0040 decision 7, part B): radios never take the checkbox's mixed state; one page padding (24px from the gutter); pinned row names and row controls in a scrolling register; the frame's landmarks (`aside`, `main#main`, `footer`); a missing value's badge once in its own cell; whole words in the inspector; `StatusLine` `size="small"`; the register a tab stop only while it scrolls; `ValueName` delegating a numeric display to the Value element; "Show details".
- **The web**: the shell draws no main on workspace routes (`landmark="none"`); the workspace route group's own error element inside the kept frame; the switcher's height above the footer; the proposal page in the workspace layout; the card's own failure sentence; Documents' empty category sentence, its revision panel without cycle-closing documents, the focus after Delete and a full-width Download; one `FloorFilter` in each register page's header with the conflict's field display and actions (ADR 0043's part B items 6 to 8); System Scope's system filter; the zone description; Fire Safety's monitoring-only text from the served flag; the focus after Equipment's selection actions; the duplicated helpers merged; phase 3's carried V-8 (one write per press across the inline editor).
- **The e2e and the tools**: render screens 79 to 85 (the integrator added System Scope's system filter open); the TEST state `assets-numbered` (ADR 0037 decision 11); flow (f)'s skip-link and keyboard-Delete steps; G11-11's case file with its rendered half (`tests/guardrails/G11-11.test.tsx`); the viewer-spike tool's images written as derived files and erased (ADR 0046 finding 11).
- **Guardrails 1.9**: 13 more cases indexed (212 ids: 192 T, 20 E), the two 1.9 change-log rows extended, the index pin updated; no rule text and no version line changed.

#### Screenshot comparison

Each built screen against its approved screenshot in `design/reference/dashboards/`, on the 1440 canvas (the flows' screens with `SOVITECH_E2E_SCREENSHOTS` set, and the references as PNG, in the scratchpad's `build/p4/screens/` and `build/p4/screens/ref/`). Each difference names its reason: the brand, dashboards-spec section 7, the PRD's scope or "Until decided" line, a closed gate or missing dataset, the owner's viewer answer, or a real miss (for part B's design review).

Every page: the logo with no tagline, white primary buttons, mint as the one accent, 1px and 2px radii, no glow (the brand); no TOPOLOGY / WIREFRAME / METRICS tabs, no BMS LIVE chip and no "BMS Live · Last sync" footer (R-146, R-139, the `operations` gate); the 48px footer carries the demo line (demo only) and "Still reading" (ADR 0043); the sidebar lists the built pages only (Proposal, System Scope, Topology, Zones, Equipment, Documents; R-146, R-050); the project card has no photo, Status or BMS Platform, and its facts read as served (Unknown, Not provided yet; R-049, proposals 7.2.9 to 7.2.11; no key); titles in the brand's title role, not upper case; the header's date without the time (P-3-HEADER-TIME).

- **DB-15 Documents** (15 against `d-DB-15-documents`, `d-DB-15-inspector`, `e-DB-15-*`): chips with no counts, no "Showing 1-8 of 12" and no page numbers (R-017); no Uploaded By or Size columns, Stage and Analysis added (R-017, R-018; 7.1.1-D1); Category Unknown on every row (G1-26); Version is the revision as written, Unknown here (7.1.1-D2); the inspector has no preview, size, uploader or description (P-4-DOCUMENT-PREVIEW; R-017 to R-019); the eyebrow carries the brand's bullet. *Real misses (design review):* with the inspector open, the register is squeezed: an IFC row's Analysis line wraps over five lines and long file names break across two; Download sits as a secondary button beside "More actions", not full width.
- **DB-16 System Scope** (16 against `d-DB-16-system-scope`, `e-DB-16-decided`, `f-DB-16-keyboard`): no 3D / 2D / Section control, canvas, building section or legend (R-054, R-074; the owner's answer); no "All Systems" in part A (built in part B, after Floor in the header: V-10; G3-22); no "From Topology" counts or Coverage (7.1.1-C1, C4): the Equipment column reads "Not available yet: SOVITECH asset taxonomy" on each row (`dataset-asset-taxonomy`); the eight step 4 systems only (R-055); Fire Safety with its monitoring-only text and, on the demo, not included (rule 11; the screenshot draws it switched on at 100%); the detail panel in the inspector slot with Overview, Equipment and Zones only, no Controllers, Field Devices or Network tabs, no counts and no "Edit Scope" (R-058; the copy change); square switches (the brand); undecided rows show "Include" and "Leave out" (US-SCOPE-05 AC4; design review).
- **DB-17 Equipment** (17 against `d-DB-17-equipment`, `e-DB-17-listed`, `e-DB-17-inspector`): no plan strip, floor stack, List / Floor Plan control or "View on Floor Plan" (R-070, R-084, R-080), no Export (phase 5), no Status, Model, Commissioned or Last Update, no product photo (D14; 7.2.x); the subtitle and the count line changed ("Equipment by type: Not available yet"; R-066, proposal 7.2.31); a Badge column; no checkboxes live (no row offers an answer: no asset field is registered); previous and next only. *Real misses (design review):* with the inspector open the register is wider than its column, so the open-details and record columns scroll out of view at 1440; "Unknown" shows twice on a row (the Type text and the Badge cell, the badge column's reading); the inspector's search-filtered order is the register's (FCU-B before FCU-A).
- **DB-20 Zones** (20 against `d-DB-20-zones`, `e-DB-20-zones`): List only: no floor plan, compass, scale bar, Floor Plan or Matrix control, "+ Add Zone", count or Status column (R-062 to R-064, R-084, R-080, D14); no zone exists live (no zone field registered, ADR 0045 decision 1), so the register says so; the floor filter reads "Not available yet: floor structure" with its two actions (G7-14). The zone details and editor are checked only in their component tests (TEST registries).
- **DB-08 Topology** (08 against `d-DB-08-topology`, `e-DB-08-topology`): none of the screenshot's design levels: no SAUTER Vision Center, automation stations, products, buses, protocols, field devices, data flow, key metrics or integration status (7.1-r12, r13; proposal 7.2.10; dashboards 8.8; G1-27; `dataset-sauter-catalogue`): one dashed "Not available yet" line names SOVITECH's design; no Physical / Logical / Hybrid control and no LOGICAL tab (R-073); the title "Topology" (the copy change); one group per included system; Fire Safety in its own lane with one upward link, "Monitoring direction only" (rule 11; the screenshot draws it as an automation station integrated over BACnet with dampers and panels).
- **The proposal page** (`d-UD-07-proposal-in-frame`): phase 3's content, now inside the frame (ADR 0043 decision 3).
- **Undesigned states** (UD-08 asset record, UD-21 upload panel, UD-32 switcher, UD-42 delete confirmation, UD-43 revision panel, the loading, failure and empty states): drawn per the frontend-design skill within the brand, all inline, none a dialog. *For the design review:* on the asset record the Documents section's coverage line ("pages 1-1 of 1") is set larger than its labels; the record's two columns leave the History column short beside a long Details column.

**Added from part B's design review (DR-16), each with its reason** (the fixes, where part B's fix list holds one, are named by their finding id; their outcomes are the integrator's):
- **DB-15 and DB-17, the squeeze's cause** (DR-2): the page padding was applied twice, the kit's page column (`.sov-workspace__page`, 24px 32px) and each page's own `px-8 pt-6 pb-10`, so the content began at x=272 and the column beside the 360px inspector was 732px, where Documents needs about 852px (887 on a new project) and Equipment about 814px. Documents also hid its row controls (Show details, More actions) and cut its file names (`l-mep-rev-`) once the register scrolled, not only DB-17's record column. A real miss against the approved screens 15 and 17 and App theme "Shell sizes" (16px gutter, 12px panel gap; dashboards-spec 3.4).
- **DB-08 and DB-20, the floor control** (DR-5): Topology and Zones drew the floor selection as a full-width notice bar under the header beside a System dropdown, System Scope as a "Floor" dropdown in its header with "Not available yet" inside its menu, and Equipment inside its Filters disclosure with its two actions not underlined: one shared selection drawn three ways. A real miss against dashboards-spec 2.5 rule 6 and 3.5 ("Fixed order: view mode, floor, system filter"), ADR 0043 decision 6 and ADR 0040 decision 6 (DR-24: link buttons with no icon are underlined).
- **The proposal page** (`d-UD-07-proposal-in-frame`; DR-7): besides phase 3's content inside the frame, the page kept the wizard's centred 1040px column (its title at x=352, y=172 against x=272, y=164 on every other workspace page) and the wizard-size "Back to review" button (190x48 among 40px workspace controls). A real miss for the layout (one frame, one page layout); the button itself stays, as a compact control (R-012's "Until decided" names menus; "Product doc issues").
- **UD-32, the switcher's list** (DR-4): the panel had no height limit, so with 19 projects it ran from y=178 to y=1228, over the sticky footer at y=852, and left the demo line reading "...essment of the real building". A real miss against rule 10 "Labelled everywhere" with 2.8 "Prominence" and WCAG 2.4.11.
- **Radios drawn filled** (DR-1): the kit's new rule for the register's select-all checkbox (`.sov-check__input:indeterminate`) also matched every radio of a group with none checked (HTML's `:indeterminate`), so the unanswered radios of step 1's project type, step 5's questions, the revision panel (UD-43) and the inline editors drew as chosen mint discs. A real miss (rule 7 "Nothing fills the gap"; rule 3; WCAG 1.3.1, 1.4.1), on phase 3's screens too.
- **Split words in the Documents inspector** (DR-8): Stage read "Technica" / "l design" beside its badge, while the Equipment inspector wraps the same value whole. A real miss against ADR 0040 decision 6 (in a narrow slot the badge moves under its text; words never split).
- **Expected, with their sources** (the design review's reading: not misses): the empty 48px footer band on a project that is neither the demo nor reading anything (App theme "Status footer: always present"); undecided System Scope rows with "Include" and "Leave out" in place of a switch (US-SCOPE-05 AC4: no decision is drawn as made); the square switch (the brand's 1px and 2px radius rule, app-alignment); the demo line in the footer on workspace pages and under the header in the wizard (ADR 0043 decision 5).

**After part B's fixes** (the flows' screens of the integrator's first part B `pnpm e2e`, saved with `SOVITECH_E2E_SCREENSHOTS` in the scratchpad's `build/p4b/integrator/shots/`, looked at against the findings): DB-15 with the inspector open (`d-DB-15-inspector`): the content starts at x=232, the register fits its column with each row's "Show details" and "More actions" in view, and Download fills the inspector's footer beside a square menu (DR-2, DR-15); file names still wrap at the column's width, and a long one breaks inside its extension ("lista-echipamente.xl" / "sx"), left for the phase 5 kit pass with DR-5's search field (no rule involved). DB-17 with the inspector open (`e-DB-17-inspector`): every column and both row controls in view, "Unknown" once per value with the Badge cell empty for a missing value (DR-6), and the floor control in the header with its two underlined actions (DR-5). DB-16 (`d-DB-16-system-scope`): Floor, then System, in the header (DR-5, V-10). The proposal page (`d-UD-07-proposal-in-frame`): the title left-aligned at x=232 like every workspace page (DR-7). Step 1 on a new project (`c-OB-1-new-empty`): the unanswered project type's four options drawn as empty rings (DR-1).

### Checks run

Builders' runs during part A (each ran only the tests of its own files, its package's typecheck, ESLint on its files and `pnpm checks`, at most one e2e spec under the lock): the kit's component tests (120) and `ui-kit.spec.ts` (27 of 27, under the lock with a scratch config and no stack); the view-model and API builder's case files and `tests/api/workspace-*.test.ts` over Testcontainers, with the phase 3 wizard API tests and wizard-route cases rerun green after the registry seam (G2-13, G3-19, G4-36, G4-38, G5-3, G5-4, G7-9 to G7-13, G8-21 to G8-23, G10-10); the page builders' component tests (Documents and the frame; System Scope, Equipment and the asset record, 47; Topology and Zones), each checked by eye and with axe against a scratch mock API at 1440x900 (0 violations; the scratch servers stopped); the spike builder's converter, plan and runner tests and its measured runs under the lock. Each builder's `pnpm checks` was 11 or 12 of 13, the index check failing only on the other builders' unindexed case files.

The integrator's runs (2026-10-02, logs in the scratchpad's `build/p4/integrator/logs/`; the e2e lock held from the first development run to the last full run, and released after):
- **Baseline** (`typecheck-0.log`, `checks-0.log`): `pnpm run typecheck` passed; `pnpm checks` 12 of 13, the index check failing on the eleven unindexed case files (188 ids at v1.8: 142 real, 20 pending, 26 no automated check yet, 11 other problems).
- **Development runs** against a stack and `vite preview` started by hand with a scratch Playwright config (both stopped, the config deleted): the 34 new screens through `screen-states.spec.ts` (34 passed) and the render project (34 passed in 4.1 min), then flows (d), (e) and (f), fixed until green (`dev-*.log`). Flow (e) first took 8.2 min, every check waiting out its 30 s settle budget on a request a navigation had cancelled; after the render test's tracker fix it took 1.3 min (each check about 4 s).
- **Route and page timings** on the demo (`route-timings.txt`): ADR 0041 item 6.
- **`pnpm check`** (`check-1.log`, about 12 min): **8 of 9 steps pass**: ESLint; dependency-cruiser (945 modules, 3,728 dependencies, 0 violations); both typechecks; Vitest **358 files, 4,306 tests** (the guardrails project: **153 case files ran as real, 506 tests, 0 failed**; the run matches the case files); the extractor's ruff and pytest; the render test **109 passed** in 3.2 min (79 app screens, the route, schema and server tests, the harness canaries and the 11 UI kit pages); the checks self-test 13 of 13. The checks step fails only on the index check, by design (D-33): **199 ids at v1.9 (179 T, 20 E): 153 real, 20 pending, 26 no automated check yet, 0 other problems**. Every other check passes: company-figures, config-exclusion, fixture-manifest (57 files reproduced), licences (469 npm packages, no copyleft; MPL 5 listed, web-ifc among them), lint-bans (500 files, 945 modules), loosening (no loosening, 18 gates closed, 30 exception lists as recorded, no approver named), mockup-figures, registry (sensitivity test 30 of 30), render (79 screens, 75 harness pages, 4 allowlist entries and 1 reviewed unreadable element, none added), reserved-terms (21 English and 14 Romanian terms, matching 2.8), scan-roots, version-sync (v1.9).
- **`pnpm check:selftest`** (inside `pnpm check`): **13 of 13**; every seeded bad input fails for its own reason (company-figures 10, config-exclusion 21, fixture-manifest 31, index 95, licences 19, lint-bans 96, loosening 75, mockup-figures 13, registry 20, render 51, reserved-terms 34, scan-roots 10, version-sync 10).
- **`pnpm e2e`, twice** (`e2e-1.log`, `e2e-2.log`; 4 workers): **223 passed** in 7.6 min, then **223 passed** in 7.7 min, no failure, no flaky test, no retry, the run guard silent: the render project's 109, and the e2e project's 114 (`screen-states.spec.ts` 79, the UI kit's 27, flows (a), (b), (c), the G8-21 refusal and the new (d), (e) and (f), `app-shell.spec.ts`). Phase 3's flows pass with the proposal page inside the workspace frame. The stack stopped each time (no container or port left).

### The viewer spike: measurements and the recommendation for D-03

The owner's answer of 2026-10-02 ("Trial now, decide later"): the spike ran; no viewer is in the app. Every number, its method and its machine are in ADR 0046 "Measurements"; ADR 0041 item 3 has the budget lines. In brief (Apple M1 Pro, 16 GB, a shared machine under memory pressure, so indicative):

| Measure | `perf` model (IFC4, 101,223,317 bytes, 186 storeys, 157,166 elements) | The four fixture models |
|---|---|---|
| Conversion in the sandbox (`--network none`, 4 GB, 2 CPUs) | 42–57 s wall (importer 31–40 s, header emptied 10–15 s); converter 751–767 MiB, container 812–844 MiB; Fragments 9,304,617 bytes | about 1–2 s and about 220 MiB each |
| Plans of every storey (lines only) | 4.4–5.4 s, 185 of 186 storeys | under 0.1 s; one of the ARH's six storeys has lines |
| First frame, integrated GPU (ANGLE Metal) | 2.95–3.44 s | 0.55–0.97 s |
| Orbit, integrated GPU | 60 fps (the display rate; 95th percentile 17.5 ms, worst 20.4 ms) | 60 fps |
| First frame and orbit in software (SwiftShader) | 3.93 s; 1.0 fps; interactive only after 240 s | 1.05–1.36 s; 38–48 fps |
| Tab memory | renderer at most 571 MiB, GPU process at most 149 MiB (217 MiB in software) | about 200–250 MiB |
| Storey shown alone | 96–288 ms (2.4–4.6 s in software) | 19–184 ms |
| Requests leaving 127.0.0.1 | none in 14 page loads | none |
| Bundle, as a lazily loaded chunk | 1,080,282 bytes gzipped (683,413 without That Open's components), plus a 278,189-byte worker | |

**Against the budgets** (ADR 0041; prompt 3 section 11): conversion within 15 minutes and 4 GB, met; first view within 5 s, met (GPU and software); orbit at 30 fps on integrated graphics, met on the M1 Pro's GPU and missed in software rendering (1 fps); the tab under 1.5 GB, met; storeys on demand, met as shown on demand and missed as loaded on demand (the whole file loads); code-split, not wired (no viewer). Not measured: a real, less repetitive model (it needs the owner's yes to a public sample download) and a typical office laptop's integrated graphics.

**Recommendation for D-03** (ADR 0046's, a recommendation and not a decision): That Open on web-ifc meets the budgets on integrated-GPU hardware with the synthetic model, and every default that reaches the network or marks the view can be switched off. If the owner wants a viewer before `ifc-values` opens, build it as the spike does: conversion in the sandbox, keyed by project id and content hash and erased with its document; the view profile (shapes, GlobalIds and the spatial tree only; the header emptied); the storey index written at conversion; the worker from the app's own origin; the logo removed; a lazily loaded chunk; keyboard camera controls and label-free storey stepping; geometry only while `ifc-values` and `view-provenance` are closed. Before relying on it, measure one real large model and an office laptop, and decide what the page shows with no GPU (under the rules, "Not available yet" with its reason, or the plan, which itself waits for P-4-PLAN-UNREADABLE).

### Guardrail review

Part A's own findings, found while building and integrating (part B's follow, below):

- **G1-26 (the planner): the stored default kind read as a category.** The upload stores `kind: 'other'` (2.3 has no unknown kind), so a category chip would have shown "Other" as read. The Category reads Unknown whatever is stored. Case G1-26 (indexed, 1.9); proposal P-4-DOCUMENT-KIND-UNKNOWN.
- **G9-6's Expected holds for the owner's entry, not for a document (the API builder).** The verifier holds no rule 8 qualifier words for rooms, so a document's "424 spaces including technical rooms" is stored with the qualifier unknown: stricter, and safe. The case file proves the Expected on the owner's own entry; the document path is beside the case. Logged in the 1.9 change log; proposal P-4-ROOMS-QUALIFIER-WORDS; the index check counts G9-6 by its case file, and this log says which path meets its Expected.
- **Topology's fire link through the HVAC group (the Topology builder).** A wrapping grid put Fire Safety on the second row at 1440 px and its monitoring link ran into the HVAC card above it, reading as fire joined to HVAC (rule 11; 7.1.1-L4). Fixed (its own lane); G11-11's rendered half asserts one link and a lane holding the fire group alone. Logged in the 1.9 change log.
- **The render test's stale in-flight requests (the integrator).** A request cancelled by a page navigation (the wizard's late-findings poll, routed through the API adapter) stayed "in flight", so every later check of the page waited out its 30 s settle budget and read the page as it stood, with a note and no failure. Fixed in `tests/e2e/render/render-check.ts`; logged in the 1.9 change log. A phase 3 screen whose arrange navigated while a request was open could meet the same wait (it passed, read after its budget).
- **That Open's defaults (the spike builder).** The worker and the WebAssembly from a CDN, the vendor's logo drawn into the view, the model's STEP header, names and property values copied into the converted file; each overridden in the spike (ADR 0046 "Findings"). Logged in the 1.9 change log.
- **A lines-only plan fails the render test (the integrator, IFC-12).** An inline SVG larger than an icon is pixels the render test cannot read, so a storey plan with no text fails it too; a live plan needs a reviewed `render.unreadable` entry, a loosening: proposal P-4-PLAN-UNREADABLE. Logged in the 1.9 change log; `tests/proposed/IFC-12.test.ts` shows both halves.
- **Open, for part B's fix list (fixed in part B: V-1, A-12; G8-24): the level and zone cells of zones and assets (the Topology and Zones builder; the view-model's files).** A zone's Floor cell, and an asset's Floor and Zone cells, show the stored level key (`ground_1`) and the zone subject's id, not the level register's label and the zone's name (R-061, R-077; 7.1.1-E4; G2-7's reading). Reachable only with TEST registries (no zone or asset field in production). Suggested case: "a zone whose stored level is `ground_1` shows its level as the register's label P"; suggested fix: resolve the level cell through the level register's label and the asset's zone cell through the zone's name.
- **Open, for part B (fixed in part B: DR-3, V-7): the skip link and the footer landmark.** `#main` holds the sidebar too, so "Skip to content" does not pass the sidebar (WCAG 2.4.1), and the StatusFooter sits inside `main` (it works there with its hidden heading; the kit suggests it outside as the contentinfo landmark). axe reports neither.
- **Open, for part B (fixed in part B: V-6): a life-safety flag on a zone's system decisions.** The zone details name Fire Safety in a constant (`MONITORING_ONLY_SYSTEMS`) to show its monitoring-only text, since `ZoneDetail.systemDecisions` carries no `lifeSafety` flag as System Scope's rows and Topology's groups do; an additive contract field removes the duplicate.
- **A process slip (the Topology and Zones builder).** `npx prettier` fetched prettier 3.9.9 from the npm registry into the npx cache, unpinned and in no lockfile or ADR (prompt 3 section 12: packages pinned, each in an ADR). No project file depends on it and the repository has no prettier configuration. Recorded; not repeated.
- **Smaller notes for part B** (the duplicated helpers merged in part B, DR-5; the rest stands): `workspace.systemScope.decide` answers the view built without the floor selection (the page reads its view again with its `level`, so nothing is wrong on screen); duplicated helpers (`useKeptView` and `use-kept.ts`; two `systems.ts`; three test envelopes); Equipment's System filter names "Fire Safety" without its monitoring-only text (a filter label, not a scope surface).

**Part B (2026-10-02).** The verifier (14 findings, V-1 to V-14), the read-only adversarial review (13, A-1 to A-13) and the design review (16, DR-1 to DR-16) ran on part A's tree: 43 findings raised, 38 distinct (A-12 repeats V-1, A-8 repeats V-3, A-2 is V-2's case, V-7 is DR-3 and V-11 is DR-14). Of the 38: 28 fixed, 7 fixed in part (the rest a proposal, A-7 and A-8, or left for a named later phase, DR-4, DR-5 and DR-15, or holding in part, V-8 and V-12), 1 written as a proposal (A-13), 1 logged (V-13) and 1 left for phase 5 (DR-11); five claims inside findings were rejected with their reasons. A triage put them in five fix groups (A: the API, store, view-model and contract; B: the UI kit; C: the shell, frame, Documents, the proposal page, phase 3's carried items and the copy catalogue; D: System Scope, Equipment, the asset record, Zones and Topology; E: the e2e flows and render screens, the viewer spike tool and this log), wrote three proposals and rejected five claims with their reasons; then this integrator. Outcomes, finding by finding (proof: the case or test that fails without the fix; "component test" names a test file under `apps/web` or `packages/ui`):

| Finding | Severity | Outcome | Proof, or where it stands |
|---|---|---|---|
| A-1 a tag written only in digits ("101", "1.2") threw in `ValueName`, and React Router's default error page replaced the whole app, the footer's demo line with it | high | **fixed**: `ValueName` never throws for a served display; a numeric display renders through the Value component's own element, with its badge and lines (kit); the workspace route group has its own error element inside the kept frame (web) | kit `selection.test.tsx` "A-1 …" (as a heading, in a button, as an option), `ui-css.test.ts` "A-1 …"; `WorkspaceFrame.test.tsx` "A-1 · rule 7 · rule 10 · GS-1 …" (a page that throws keeps the sidebar and the footer's demo line); `AssetPage.test.tsx` and `EquipmentPage.test.tsx` "A-1 …" for "101" and "1.2"; two render screens on the TEST state `assets-numbered` |
| A-2 / V-2 the delete confirmation said "0 values will return to Unknown" for a document that alone showed three equipment tags, which Delete then removed from Equipment | medium | **fixed**: the effect is derived with the domain's own derive, the document treated as removed: fields with a value now and none after, and assets counted now and not after (a tag is a value of its asset, 2.5) | **G4-41** (new); `workspace.test.ts` "A-2 · 2.5 …"; G4-39 unchanged and green; flow (e) still reads "0 values" for its PDF. The part of V-2 claiming the removed assets should carry "Source document removed" is **rejected** (that line is for a field left with no eligible candidate, 2.3 and 2.8; an asset with no live evidence is simply not counted, 2.5 "Counting", G4-28). A further reading is offered to the approver: P-4B-DELETE-EFFECT-ASSETS |
| A-3 the effect of deleting a declared newer revision said "1 value", while the older value came back | medium | **fixed** (with A-2) | **G4-42** (new); `workspace.test.ts` "A-3 · 2.3 …" |
| A-4 Delete racing an upload of the same bytes removed the second document's original after it registered (Download 404) | medium | **fixed**: the upload's registration takes the project's write lock and keeps its sealed copy until it commits, moving it in when the original is missing; Delete's file removal runs in a second request under the lock and re-reads the documents holding the bytes | **G13-10** (new; fails on the pre-fix upload service) |

**The integrator's own findings in part B.**
- **The wizard's write answers still served raw level keys and zone ids** after the workspace's fix (`fields.acknowledge`, `fields.concern`, `fields.edit`); now named through the same function (G8-24's reading; G2-7). Logged in the 1.9 change log with V-1.
- **A focus fallback that a browser would refuse.** Documents' "no row left" focus went to the register's region, which since DR-12 is not focusable unless it scrolls; happy-dom focuses any element, so the component test passed. The fallback is now the region only while it is a tab stop, else `main#main`, and the test asserts that the region has no tab index there.
- **The section 14 grep for bidirectional controls did not search.** Part A's grep script ran under bash, where `grep` is the system's BSD grep with no `-P`; its error went to /dev/null, so it reported no file. Rerun with Perl (and the wider class U+200B to U+200F, U+202A to U+202E, U+2060 to U+2069, U+FEFF, checked against a positive probe first), it names the two files below. Logged in the 1.9 change log.
- **Raw bidirectional controls in a case file.** `tests/guardrails/G2-15.test.ts` said its controls were written as escapes, but held the characters themselves (U+202E, U+2066, U+2069, U+200F, U+200B) in four literals; section 14's grep for bidirectional controls flags them. Now escapes; the case passes. The only other file the grep names is the kit's generated harness page `tests/e2e/pages/ui/layout.html`, whose U+202E comes from `kit.tsx`'s escaped TEST text (the isolation test of a stored owner text, as at phase 3's commit).
- **A render screen for System Scope's new system filter** (area D's request to area E, not taken up): "DB-16 System Scope, demo: the system filter open after Floor, All systems and the eight (V-10; rule 3; G3-22)".
- **Three case Expected cells indexed in a narrower form than their files first wrote**, each following from the rules as written: G3-21 names the confidence (Possible) and leaves the rejected entry beside the case, since the rules do not say a rejected entry is hidden (P-4B-HISTORY-ENTRY-WORDING may show it); G7-17 leaves the select-all checkbox's mixed state beside the case (a regression guard, not a rule's outcome); G8-24 names "the level register's label, the same on each" rather than E1, the interim notation that D-18 may change. The case files' headers now match.

**The final verification and the fix round after it (2026-10-02).** The final verifier re-ran the checks on the integrated tree, replayed every finding and found five new problems (NP-1 to NP-5, in its own numbering). One fixer reproduced each first (the scratchpad's `build/p4c/`: `browser/run-before.log` with `browser/out/before/np1-focus-trace.json`, `probes/run-before.log`, `repo-tests-before.log`, `api-test-before.log`), then fixed it:
- **NP-1 (medium; A-6's residual; WCAG 2.4.3) Deleting the last document on Documents from the keyboard left the focus on `<body>` in Chromium. Fixed.** With no row left, `focusAfterDelete` focused the register's scroll region. The region was a tab stop at that moment only because the open inspector made it overflow (DR-12). Once the inspector closed, the ResizeObserver removed its tab index a few milliseconds later, and Chromium's focus fixup moved the focus to `<body>` (reproduced: region focused at 146 ms of the trace, tab index removed at 151 ms; `<body>` from 50 ms to 4 s after Enter). happy-dom keeps the focus on such an element, so the component test had passed. With no row left the focus now goes to the page column, `main#main`, whose tab index is always -1; the region is never the target. Proofs: flow (f) deletes the last document from the keyboard and asserts that the page column holds the focus, and still holds it once the page settles (it failed on the old code); `DocumentsPage.test.tsx` "NP-1 · A-6 · WCAG 2.4.3 …" makes the region a tab stop through its observer before Delete (it failed with the focus on the region). No section 7 case: focus is layout behaviour, as for A-6.
- **NP-2 (low) The revision panel blocked a correction that the derive accepts. Fixed.** Once B was declared a revision of A by mistake, A's panel offered no B: `revisionCandidates` left out every document whose served chain reaches the row, a direct pair included. Yet the derive applies the reversed declaration on a pair as a correction (ADR 0016 decision 17; G4-44's first project). The panel now leaves out only a document whose chain reaches the row through another document, which would close a cycle the derive ignores whole (G4-44's second project), so the reverse of a direct pair is offered. The function moved unchanged to a pure module (`apps/web/src/workspace/pages/documents/revisions.ts`), and was changed there. A-5's component test had encoded the block, so its expectations are corrected. Proofs: `revisions.test.ts` (two tests failed on the old code); `tests/api/workspace-revision-correction.test.ts`, the verifier's probe sequence through the API, with the web's own function over the served rows (on the old code it offered only C on A). No new refusal, wording or gate. No section 7 case: G4-44 states the derive's outcome, and the panel now offers what the derive applies.
- **NP-3 (low; A-7's identity half) A tag made only of format controls normalises to a tag, so it becomes a counted asset whose tag reads Unknown. Not fixed: identity is the approver's.** P-4B-TAG-FORMAT-CONTROLS now names the case ("Proposals"), and `normaliseTag` is unchanged. Reachable only with TEST data until the API key exists (D-09).
- **NP-4 (low; TEST registries only) In an asset's history, an entry for a zone no longer named showed the word "Unknown" as its value, beside its badge, its source line and "Source document removed". Fixed.** The zone's name and code were read only from the deleted document, so the entry's text was the missing wording in a value display, as if the document had said "Unknown". A zone entry that is no longer current, and whose zone has no name or code, is now the missing display (Unknown as its badge) and keeps its 2.8 status line, as 2.3 shows a field whose only source document was deleted (`historyEntryDisplay`, packages/view-model/src/workspace/registers.ts). Naming the zone from its withdrawn name candidate was not taken: it would put another field's withdrawn value inside this entry, under this entry's source line. A current entry keeps the zone cell's display (ADR 0045). No new wording. Proofs: `workspace.test.ts` "NP-4 · V-3 · 2.3 …" (it failed on the old code with the text "Unknown" and shape `value`) and "NP-4 · V-3 · G8-24 …" (an entry whose zone another document still names keeps the name); the verifier's probe `api3.adv.test.ts`, rerun through the API (`probes/run-after.log`). No section 7 case: G3-21 states the entry's status line, and this changes only the text beside it.
- **NP-5 (info) G4-44's indexed Expected for its first project follows ADR 0016 decision 17's reading of the derive, not text in 2.3.** Logged as question 6 under "Questions for the owner", for the owner to confirm or reverse; nothing changes until then.

### Definition of done

CLAUDE.md items 1 to 5, for phase 4 after part B:

1. **The guardrail checks pass: holds, except the index check, by design (D-33).** Guardrail tests (every indexed case file ran as real, 0 failed), the index check (212 ids at 1.9, 0 other problems), registry validation with the sensitivity test and the loosening check, the reserved-term check and the render test all pass ("Checks run").
2. **The guardrail evals: not running yet.** `prompts/`, the model id and the AI output schema did not change in part B (the in-app prompt's version line stays 1.9). The 20 E cases stay pending: no API key (D-09).
3. **New behaviour has a case: holds for the 24 new cases of the phase**, indexed in section 7 at 1.9: part A's eleven (G1-26, G1-27, G3-20, G4-39, G4-40, G7-14, G7-15, G11-10, G11-11, G12-10, G13-9) and part B's thirteen (G2-15, G3-21, G3-22, G4-41, G4-42, G4-43, G4-44, G7-16, G7-17, G8-24, G12-11, G12-12, G13-10). G11-11's case file now holds both halves (V-12). G9-6 is real and **holds in part** (the owner's path; the document path is stricter, P-4-ROOMS-QUALIFIER-WORDS). G7-16's rendered half, like G7-14's, is in the page component tests ("G7-16 (web half)" on Topology, Zones, Equipment and System Scope) and two render screens; its case file proves the served register every floor control renders. **Does not hold**, each with its reason: the render test's tracker fix (harness behaviour; logged in the change log, no section 7 case); IFC-12 (in `tests/proposed/`, not indexed: it waits for D-03, D-04 and D-01, and for ifc-input 6.2.15's `view-provenance`); and part B's fixes that are not a rule's outcome, proven by component, API or e2e tests and not by a section 7 case: A-1 (a numeric tag renders, and a failing page keeps the frame and the demo line), DR-3 (the landmarks), DR-4 (the switcher stops above the footer), DR-7 (the proposal page's layout), V-8 (one write per press across the inline editor), A-6 (the focus after removing actions), A-11 (the spike's images erased), V-5 (the project list's read), DR-2, DR-5, DR-6, DR-8 to DR-10, DR-12, DR-13 and DR-15 (layout and naming).
4. **Version, labels and rule numbers agree: holds.** `docs/guardrails.md` 1.9, `CLAUDE.md` and the in-app prompt state v1.9 (the version-sync check). Part B extended 1.9's two change-log rows ("New cases only", "Log entries only": allowed without approval, section 10) and changed no rule text, so no version line moved.
5. **Rules and cases:** below.

- **Rule 10:** G10-10 extended; G10-12's workspace half.
