### Proposals (not applied)

- **P-4-DOCUMENT-PREVIEW** (a render-test loosening for the approver). Change: add a `render.unreadable` entry `document-preview` for one `<img>` per Documents inspector whose source is the stored preview of the selected document's first page (keyed by project id and content hash, served after the project access check, erased with its document: ifc-input 6.2.16's reading), marked with that entry id and the document's id. Failure behind it: US-DOCS-14 AC2 draws a preview, and an image of a page holds the document's digits, which the render test cannot read; without the entry the build shows no preview. Truth: neutral (the image is the owner's own page, unchanged; no value reads from it). Speed: up (the owner recognises the file at a glance). Proving case: a preview `<img>` with the entry and a matching document id passes; an image with no entry, an entry on any other image, or text drawn by the app over the preview fails.
- **P-4-DOCUMENT-KIND-UNKNOWN** (a clarification of 2.3 for the approver). Change in 2.3, `DocumentRecord.kind`: add `'unknown'`, "until a classifier, the owner or an engineer sets it". Failure behind it: the upload stores `kind: 'other'` because 2.3 offers no unknown, so "Other" could be shown as if read (the near miss G1-26 now catches at display). Truth: up. Speed: neutral. Proving case: G1-26 holds with `unknown` stored, and a document classified `other` by its evidence reads "Other" with its badge.
- **P-4-PLAN-UNREADABLE** (a render-test loosening for the approver; added by the integrator, from IFC-12). Change, in `tests/e2e/render/allowlist.ts`:
  ```diff
   unreadable: [
     { id: 'brand-logo', element: 'img', src: …, reason: …, source: … },
  +  {
  +    id: 'storey-plan',
  +    element: 'svg',
  +    // marked by the one plan component, once D-03, D-04 and D-01 allow it to be built
  +    reason:
  +      'A storey plan cut from a stored model: path elements in currentColor only, with no text, tspan, textPath, title, desc, foreignObject, image, use, a, style or script element and no character data, so it draws no name, figure or label (labels are page elements bound to value ids).',
  +    source: 'prompt 3 section 8 ("Plans"); ifc-input 5.4 IFC-12; docs/adr/0046-viewer-spike.md decision 8',
  +  },
   ],
  ```
  with the check refusing the entry on any SVG that holds a text-drawing or embedding element, or character data. Failure behind it: `tests/proposed/IFC-12.test.ts`'s second test: the viewer spike's storey plan of the ARH fixture, lines only, fails the render test as `unreadable-pixels`, because an inline SVG larger than an icon draws shapes the test cannot read (G2-1's `g2-1/svg-path-glyphs.html`). Without the entry no plan can be shown even once D-03, D-04 and D-01 allow one. Truth: neutral (no figure, name or label can be drawn inside an admitted plan, and labels stay page elements bound to value ids, as prompt 3 section 8 requires). Speed: up when a plan is built (the owner sees the floor), none meanwhile. Proving case: IFC-12's first test (a plan with an area printed into it fails on that number) keeps failing with the entry; the lines-only plan with the entry passes; an SVG with the entry and a `<text>` element fails; the entry on an `<img>` or a `<canvas>` fails.
- **P-4-ROOMS-QUALIFIER-WORDS** (for the approver; a loosening: the verifier would read more as stated; from the API builder). Change: add rule 8 words for the rooms qualifiers to the evidence verifier's `RULE_8_QUALIFIER_WORDS` (`packages/domain/src/evidence.ts`) once an approved glossary lists them, through `dataset-glossary`; for example "spații" / "spaces" → `all_spaces`, "camere de hotel" / "guest rooms" → `guest_rooms`, "chei" / "keys" → `keys`. Failure behind it: G9-6's Expected ("424 spaces including technical rooms: stored as all_spaces") cannot hold for a document value today; every document's rooms count is stored with the qualifier unknown and cannot feed a per-unit output (US-REVIEW-04). Truth: neutral to up (the basis shown as the document states it). Speed: up (the owner is not asked what a documented count counts). Proving case: G9-6's "beside the case" test turns into the case itself (stored `all_spaces` from the document), and a rooms count with no such word stays unknown.

- **P-4B-TAG-FORMAT-CONTROLS** (A-7, identity half; for the approver: it changes how 2.5 identity is computed and merges appearances that stay apart today, so it is treated as a loosening under section 10, "when unsure"). Diff: `packages/domain/src/assets.ts` `normaliseTag`: before its case and separator folding, remove characters of Unicode category Cf and the default-ignorable code points (U+200B-U+200F, U+202A-U+202E, U+2060-U+2064, U+2066-U+2069, U+FEFF), so they take no part in a tag's identity (storage keeps the tag as written; area A's part B fix already serves it without them). Failure behind it: one TEST document writing `TEST-VCV-07`, `TEST-VCV<U+200B>-07` and `TEST-VCV-07<U+2060>` gives three Equipment rows that look identical (the adversarial probe `api2.adv.test.ts`), against 2.5 "One tag, one asset" and rule 4 "Same-tag appearances are one asset". Effect on the two promises: truth up (one asset per tag, no phantom duplicates in the register or in later counts); asking neutral to up (no owner or engineer time on look-alike rows). Proving case (a new G4 id, indexed only on approval): the three spellings are one asset with three evidence entries, and a tag that differs by a visible character stays a separate asset.
  **It also covers a tag made only of format controls** (the final verifier's NP-3). A tag written only with such characters (U+200B U+2060; U+2066 U+2069; spaces with U+200F) normalises to a tag today, because `normaliseTag` does not drop them. Each such spelling is therefore a counted asset whose tag is served as the missing wording, Unknown (G2-15), one Equipment row per spelling (the verifier's probe `fresh.adv.test.ts` F4). Such an appearance names no tag, so 2.5 ("Untagged appearances are never merged or counted automatically") and G4-17 read it as untagged: never listed or counted. Under the diff it normalises to no tag (`null`), so it is untagged; the proving case adds one such tag, which is untagged. `normaliseTag` is unchanged until the approver decides, since identity is the approver's. It is reachable only with TEST data until the API key exists (D-09).
- **P-4B-LIFE-SAFETY-PARTS** (A-13; for the approver: new status wording and a catalogue change). Diff: `packages/registry/src/production/catalogue.ts`: elevators, lighting and access_control each declare a life-safety part (fire-fighter lifts; emergency lighting; door release on escape routes: rule 11, "What counts as life-safety"). System Scope, Topology and Zones show rule 11's monitoring-only text for that part beside the system, which is new status wording. The systems stay never preselected. Failure behind it: with Fire Safety, Elevators and Lighting included, Topology draws Elevators and Lighting in the ordinary lane beside HVAC with no life-safety note (the adversarial probes `api.adv` and `web3.adv`: groups `[hvac,false]`, `[lighting,false]`, `[fire_safety,true]`, `[elevators,false]`). Rule 11 lists fire-fighter lifts and emergency lighting. Nothing claims control today, because no control link is drawn. Effect on the two promises: truth up (the scope says which parts the BMS may only monitor); asking neutral (no question added). Proving case (a new G11 id): Topology with Elevators included shows rule 11's monitoring-only text for fire-fighter lifts and draws no control link to them.
- **P-4B-HISTORY-ENTRY-WORDING** (A-8 / V-3, rejected entries; for the approver: new 2.8 wording). Diff: guardrails 2.8 "Status lines and stage labels" gains a line for a history entry rejected by the owner or by SOVITECH (for example "Rejected by the owner on <date>"), and lines for acknowledgement and verification events. With them, R-068's "candidates and events with who, role, when and why" can be shown in full. Failure behind it: US-ASSETS-07 AC3 requires rejected, superseded and withdrawn entries in an asset's history. After area A's part B fix, superseded and withdrawn entries show with their existing 2.8 lines ("From a superseded revision", "Source document removed"). A rejected entry has no wording, so the fix leaves it out; without the fix it would read like the current value. Effect on the two promises: truth up (the whole history shows, each entry marked); asking neutral. Proving case (a new G3 id): an asset's history shows a rejected entry with its line, never as the current value.

- **P-4B-DELETE-EFFECT-ASSETS** (part B, area A; optional, not applied: a reading for the approver to confirm, with new wording only if the approver wants it). Today's reading: an equipment tag that only the deleted document holds counts as one of the "values that will return to Unknown" (2.5, `tag?: FieldRef`; 2.3, "A field left with no eligible candidate returns to unknown"; G4-41). Failure it guards against: the owner reads "3 values will return to Unknown", then sees Equipment list three rows fewer rather than three Unknown cells. Option: a second clause naming the equipment that will no longer be listed, which is new wording and needs approval. Truth: up (clearer about what leaves the list). Speed: neutral (no question, gate or step). Proving case: a G4-41 v2 with that sentence. Until decided, the shipped sentence stays.
- **A note to P-4B-LIFE-SAFETY-PARTS** (part B, area D): the catalogue holds the monitoring-only text for Fire Safety alone, so a life-safety system other than Fire Safety (a TEST case today) shows its own catalogue description on Zones, System Scope and Topology alike, chosen by the served `lifeSafety` flag. Rule 11's monitoring-only wording on every life-safety system needs new approved copy, which the proposal above would bring with its parts; no guardrail change follows from the copy alone.

### Product doc issues

- **Prompt 3 5.2 "Navigation" against PRD R-146 and 10.2** (D-02): 5.2 builds the 2.5 structure with tabs; the PRD's "Until decided" lines keep tabs, grouping, Overview and Property out. The stricter PRD lines are followed (ADR 0043).
- **Prompt 3 section 9 ("UD-02 Property | 4") and section 10 (phase 4: "Documents (DB-15), Property (UD-02)") against PRD R-050** ("no Property page is built"): the PRD decides scope (prompt 3 section 3); not built.
- **Prompt 3 section 9 (DB-10: "The one 2D plan component (UD-04), shared with 17 and 20"; phase 4: "The one 2D plan component for DB-10, DB-17 and DB-20") against PRD R-084** (Later; "the single plan component of `design/dashboards-spec.md` 2.5 rule 5 is neither built nor ruled out"): not built.
- **Prompt 3 section 9 (DB-01: "Proposal content as a view mode")** against R-078, R-079, R-085 (no model view, no mode control, no floor inspector): DB-01 is not built in this phase.
- **Prompt 3 phase 4 ("The 3D view from IFC geometry with the provenance behaviour of section 8")** against R-078 "Until decided" and the owner's answer of 2026-10-02: the spike only.
- **PRD R-060** ("Every zone count, including step 3's Zones row, is a Calculated count over this register") against phase 1's registry, where step 3's Zones row is `building.zones`, a count a document may state (rule 1, a `document` value): left as built; for the next PRD run.
- **ifc-input 2.2's spike on "one large public sample"**: replaced by the synthetic `perf` profile (prompt 3 section 12: no download without asking).
- **PRD R-068** (the asset detail's history: "candidates and events with who, role, when and why") **against 2.8**, which writes no wording for a candidate's events (acknowledged, rejected, verified, superseded): the history lists candidates only, by role and date (a partial build; for the next PRD run and the approver, since new history wording is 2.8's to write). After part B (V-3), each listed entry carries its own badge, source line and 2.8 status line, and a rejected entry, which 2.8 gives no wording, is left out with the events (P-4B-HISTORY-ENTRY-WORDING).
- **G4-39 and the delete confirmation** (part B, V-13): G4-39 asserts the count the delete confirmation states, which follows from 2.3 ("A field left with no eligible candidate returns to unknown"); the confirmation itself is the one 7.1.1-D4 requires (and R-016, US-DOCS-21 AC1), while the PRD flags for the approver whether this destructive-action confirmation counts as a section 10 "confirmation step" (R-016 Sources; traceability 10.2 item 14). G4-39 stands pending that ruling; nothing else changes.
- **The PRD summary against R-012's "Until decided"** (part B, DR-7): the summary (`docs/product/prd.md` line 43) says "after Generate the only way back into the intake is a 'Not available yet' line for a missing owner input (R-012; D-14, D-02)", while R-012's "Until decided" says "no menu shows a 'back to intake' entry". Phase 3 built "Back to review" on the proposal page as a page button, not a menu entry; the proposal page, now in the workspace frame, keeps it as a compact control (part B's fix list, DR-7). Which reading holds is for the next PRD run and the owner (D-14, D-02).

### Questions for the owner

Only those that change the build; the earlier phases' questions stand (the API key first).

1. **D-03, after the spike** (ADR 0046's measurements and recommendation, summarised under "The viewer spike" above): build the 3D view from That Open on web-ifc, as the spike does (geometry only while `ifc-values` and `view-provenance` are closed), and if so in which phase; or no viewer for now? If yes, also: what the page shows on a machine with no GPU (software rendering ran the `perf` model at 1 frame a second).
2. **A real public sample model** (prompt 3 section 12; ifc-input 2.2): may the build download one large public IFC sample (its source and size stated in the request) to measure a less repetitive model than the synthetic `perf` one? Nothing was downloaded.
3. **The workspace from the project list (D-02, D-14):** until phase 5 stores a proposal, a project from the list opens at step 1 and the workspace is reached through Generate. Should the list also open the workspace directly?
4. **Document categories (D-24):** do you accept ADR 0045's chip mapping (Architectural; MEP with electrical; Operational with the existing BMS and energy bills; Regulatory with certificates; Other)?
5. **Level labels (D-18):** until you choose a label scheme, levels read in the regim's notation (S1, P, Mz, E1 … Er). Keep that, or English labels (B1, GF, Level 1)?
6. **A revision declared both ways (G4-44; the final verifier's note, NP-5):** G4-44's Expected for its first project ("the later declaration on a pair stands") follows ADR 0016 decision 17's reading of the derive, not text in 2.3, which says nothing about a pair declared both ways. Today, declaring B a revision of A after A a revision of B corrects the first, and Documents' revision panel offers that correction (NP-2). Do you confirm that reading, or reverse it?

Part B adds no question; the final verification adds item 6, a reading to confirm. Its proposals (P-4B-TAG-FORMAT-CONTROLS, P-4B-LIFE-SAFETY-PARTS, P-4B-HISTORY-ENTRY-WORDING, and the optional reading P-4B-DELETE-EFFECT-ASSETS) wait for the approver under "Proposals (not applied)"; none blocks the build.

The earlier phases' questions stand, the API key first: with it, the AI reads the documents, the evals run 5 of 5, and the registers fill.

- **Left for later by part B's triage:** DR-11, the type scale (sizes from 12 to 34px through arbitrary classes, 15px beside 14px): body, small, caption and sidebar-label roles in the kit pass that phase 5's Overview and Reports pages need (no brand rule is broken today; app-alignment: body 14-17px); DR-15's unused kit `ModelArea` beside step 3's own phase 3 model area: settled in the phase that builds a model view once the owner decides D-03 (until then step 3's approved area stays); DR-5's one kit SearchField with one width (Documents 280px, Equipment 360px, Zones 280px): phase 5, with the kit's consolidation; DR-4's ordering of the switcher (the current project first) and its height following the name's line count: phase 5, with the project list opening the workspace (D-14), since no approved screen settles it.
